# Data Plan

## MUSE-ONLY editor account RESTORED — always signed in here, stripped before any live publish (Nov 21, 2026)
- Ben: "also on muse do the same thing again so i dont have to sign in only remove it before posting it up online" — the always-signed-in ViceSunshineEditor bootstrap removed earlier today (see the removal entry below) is RESTORED in this Muse artifact only, exactly as the Nov 20, 2026 build had it. It stays Muse-only and MUST be stripped before any publish to the standalone live website (the four publishes v246–v249 all verified 0 matches in the standalone files) — the live site always requires real sign-in and is unchanged by this restore.
- Server (`server/src/actions.ts`): action `bootstrapEditorAccount` recreated — creates (or returns) a full account bound to the CALLER'S OWN device player key; only device-minted keys (`p-…`, as `playerKey()` in `client/src/App.tsx` generates) are accepted, so it grants no credential and can never return or unlock anyone else's account. Gamertag `ViceSunshine` (fallbacks `ViceSunshineHQ`, `ViceSunshineBen` if a community profile holds the name, then the shared `ViceSunshineEditor`), automatic guest display name like every creation path, no email and no password (both addable later in Settings), 500 starting Vice Credits with the standard welcome credit log entry. Idempotent: a device that already has a profile gets it back unchanged.
- Client (`client/src/App.tsx`): the shell's bootstrap effect is restored — once the device's own profile read settles with no account, it calls `bootstrapEditorAccount` once for that key and re-reads the profile in place (staying on the open page). An explicit Log out is respected for the rest of that session (an in-mount flag set by `handleSignedOut`; the next fresh open signs in again automatically), each key is attempted at most once per mount, and a device already signed in to a different real account is never overridden. A failed bootstrap just leaves the visitor as a guest.
- HARDENED same day (Ben: "Just make sure I stayed signed in until I tell u to remove it" — after the post-flow build landed he found himself signed out): the bootstrap stays bound to the device's OWN random key, but it is no longer one-shot and the key is no longer kept in a single storage slot. (a) The effect retries a failed `bootstrapEditorAccount` attempt with backoff (3s growing, capped at 15s) for as long as the device has no profile, and regaining focus / tab visibility re-checks immediately — an attempt that lands in a deploy window can no longer leave the owner signed out for the whole session. (b) `playerKey()` now keeps backup copies of the device key (a second localStorage slot + sessionStorage) and recovers the SAME key from a backup before ever minting a new one, so an update that drops the primary stored entry no longer signs the device out; deliberate sign-out writes (Settings Log out, the sign-in gate's don't-remember path) write the signed-out marker into the backup slot too, so recovery never resurrects a chosen sign-out. An earlier draft of this hardening used one fixed shared editor key in the client; the security review rejected a fixed account key in shipped client code (the Nov 20, 2026 review had rejected the same shape), so it was removed before shipping and the per-device key design stands. Still Muse-only: the effect and the action are stripped before any publish, and a device signed in to a real account is never overridden. Explicit Log out is still respected for the rest of that session; the next fresh open signs back in. The auto sign-in stays until Ben says to remove it.

## Feed: videos first + two AI automatic accounts — GTA NEWS and GTA CLIPPER, labelled "AI" (Nov 21, 2026)
- Ben: "make videos easier to find / So I want videos prioritize / and no ai generated / streamer clips / funny video / gta Jokes meme / people like that more / i want the online feed to be the exact same and automatic generates" and "you can also like GTA NEWS (AI automatic accounts) where it will contiously post gta newd, while another AI is called GTA CLIPPER (ai clips like tiktok poster automatic) but if it is a bot next their name it should say AI". Muse artifact first; the website move (including a feed-posts-only data merge — never a live-DB overwrite) comes later, when Ben says so.
- VIDEOS PRIORITIZED (server `listFeed`): the Top sort now ranks posts carrying a real playable video FIRST (most liked, then newest within the group), then everything else the same way — a brand-new video with 0 likes can no longer fall outside the first 100 Top posts the way the 4 real Auto videos did on the live site. Latest stays newest-first. Client: a "Videos only" chip joins the Feed's SHOW row (passes kind "Videos"; server filters to posts with a real YouTube video, any category) — the one reader filter added on top of the standing no-category-chooser rule, because videos are the priority now.
- TWO AI ACCOUNTS, ONE SHARED SEARCH ROUND (Ben's budget rule, Nov 21 2026 ~17:51 MST: the accounts must NOT use searches up faster): `refreshFeedMedia` is unchanged in its searches — the same single round (11 video queries, 2 Rockstar queries, 4 news queries) fetches news and clips together, then splits them: video posts are inserted under authorKey `gta-clipper-ai` / author "GTA CLIPPER", trending-news posts under `gta-news-ai` / "GTA NEWS". No per-account searches were added; one existing query was reworded only ("GTA VI meme video" → "GTA VI meme jokes funny video", same count). Rockstar Games official posts keep author "Rockstar Games" / the shared auto key — they are Rockstar's own posts, not an AI account, and carry no AI label.
- RE-ATTRIBUTION: each `refreshFeedMedia` run first sweeps stored rows authored "ViceSunshine Automatic" under the old shared key `viceverse-auto`: a row with a real video becomes GTA CLIPPER's, the rest (news) become GTA NEWS's. Idempotent; community posts, example posts and Rockstar posts untouched. The shell already calls `refreshFeedMedia` on load (5-minute server throttle) and the existing crons (`viceverse-auto-feed-media` 3-hourly, `leonida-hub-daily-feed-media` daily) keep both accounts posting continuously — no new schedule.
- AI LABEL: `listFeed` and `profilePostItem` responses gain `isAi` (server helper `isAiFeedRow`: AI keys, AI author names, or a not-yet-reattributed "ViceSunshine Automatic" auto row). Client: an "AI" chip (accent background) sits immediately next to the author name in `FeedPostCard` (replacing the generic "Auto" chip for these accounts; Rockstar auto posts keep "Auto"), and in the Saved post cards and `ProfilePostSheet` for saved/profile copies of their posts. Per Ben's Nov 20, 2026 decision the seeded MEMBER bot accounts stay unlabelled — the AI label is only for the automatic poster accounts.
- NO AI-GENERATED CONTENT: the accounts are automated posters; everything they post is real — real news stories and real YouTube videos from real creators, embedded/linked to the original, never re-uploaded (unchanged pipeline: only real watch/shorts URLs accepted, embeddability verified, dead videos stripped). The video pass additionally refuses titles presenting as AI-generated (`ai-generated|ai-made|made with ai|sora`).
- Feed explainer copy above the posts now names both accounts and the rule: videos lead the Feed, GTA NEWS posts news, GTA CLIPPER posts streamer clips / funny videos / GTA jokes and memes, both labelled AI, nothing AI-generated, nothing re-uploaded. Empty state for Videos-only view names GTA CLIPPER. No schema or migration changes (author keys are plain text values in the existing `author_key` column).

## Camera screen reworked to Ben's spec — no left rail, flashlight top left, X top right, frame full to the top, tap = photo / hold = video (Nov 21, 2026)
- Ben's camera spec (re-confirmed Nov 21, 2026 08:43 MST, with iPhone camera screenshots): on the Feed Creator's camera screen, remove the left rail entirely — Create, Effects and the collapse chevron are all gone (the rail's preset looks live on in the Edit step, where each captured item can still be given one). A flashlight button sits at the top left corner (real torch via the live track's `torch` constraint; where the camera/browser offers none it says so, back camera hint included) and the X close button at the top right corner.
- FRAME FULL TO THE TOP: on the capture step the stage is now `absolute inset-0` inside the full-screen creator section — no more 9/16 rounded card sitting under the container's top padding; the preview (or the camera-unavailable fallback) fills the screen edge to edge, top included. Preview/Edit/Details steps keep their framed stage.
- BOTTOM STAYS AS IT WAS: gallery thumbnail left, capture button centre, flip camera right, Post/Story mode bar underneath — the mode bar now rides inside the camera's bottom overlay (it used to sit below the framed stage, which no longer exists on the capture step), and capture-step errors / the published confirmation render inside that overlay too, since nothing below a full-screen frame would be visible.
- ONE CAPTURE BUTTON, TAP = PHOTO / HOLD = VIDEO: the old photo/video `camMode` split (whose video mode was unreachable — nothing ever set it) is gone. Pointer down starts a 260ms hold timer: lift before it fires → photo (canvas capture, as before); hold past it → MediaRecorder starts (60s cap, as before), lift → stop, and the clip lands in Preview. Pointer capture keeps the release on the button; keyboard Enter/Space still takes a photo via the click fallback. The button's centre turns into the red record square while recording, and the hint line under the controls reads "Tap for a photo · hold for a video" / "REC — release to stop".
- ROUTING: a captured video opens the Details step aimed at the Videos destination and a photo at Post (a story in progress is never re-aimed), so camera videos land under the profile Videos tab and photos under Posts through the existing publish path — no server change.
- All in `client/src/App.tsx` (FeedCreator only). No schema, migration or action changes.

Guides/news article TEXT was not rewritten (ordinary mentions of clips in prose stay, per Ben's rule; no guide/news body carried a tab link to Clips — in-app links are tab-based and all covered above). KEPT ON PURPOSE: the NavIcon `case "clips"` glyph, the ClipsView/TikTokPlayer code and the listClips action (all gated, part of the backup/restore path); Feed post KINDS "Clip"/"Streamer Clip" (the Feed's own categories — not the section); Saved's "Posts & clips" heading (saved Feed posts, untouched per Ben's do-not-touch rule); the legal pages' cookie text mentioning TikTok embeds (legal pages were explicitly do-not-touch; the sentence is accurate again the moment Clips returns).

## Double-check pass: profile visits fixed permanently + map furniture removed, sea graded to the poster's cyan (Nov 21, 2026)
- Ben (Nov 21, 2026 10:31–10:35 MST): tapping a person kept opening his own profile (third time) — "fix it permantely" / "i can only look at my own profile from one tab which is if i personally go to my profile" (the left sidebar Profile tab) / "everytime when i press on someone else profile it shoudkl bring me legit to the person im pressing on" / stop adding features, double-check the whole site bug by bug, verify by tapping on screen. Root cause confirmed in code: most surfaces already carried the person's PUBLIC profile ID, but every one of them kept a gamertag FALLBACK (Feed/Repost authors, comments, Clips, Saved, search, profile post sheets) that fired whenever an ID was missing, and `findPlayerByGamertag` returned the FIRST account with the name — with ~40 accounts sharing "ViceSunshineEditor", the first match is the viewer's own account. The share link also carried the bare gamertag handle, so an opened share link resolved the same wrong way.
- PROFILE FIX (client `client/src/App.tsx` + server `server/src/actions.ts`): the gamertag fallback is DELETED everywhere — `onOpenAuthorTag` / `openPlayerByGamertag` / `viewProfileByGamertag` no longer exist; FeedPostCard (author + repost root), CommentRow/CommentsSheet, ClipsView, SavedView, SearchOverlay, SearchResultsView and ProfilePostSheet open a person ONLY by public profile ID, and a person with no ID behind them renders unpressable instead of guessing by name. Own-profile rule enforced in three layers: (1) the shell's `openPlayerProfile` and ProfileView's `viewProfile` refuse any identifier that is the viewer's own key or own public ID (new server action `getMyPublicId`, cached as ["my-public-id", key] and shared by shell, Profile page and share screen); (2) ProfileView's vv-open-player hand-off consumption refuses self hand-offs; (3) a backstop effect leaves the viewed state whenever a viewed profile resolves server-side to the viewer's own account (getSocialProfile returns the owner's real key for their own profile). Share links now carry the public ID (`#player=pub_…`, routed by the shell straight to the ID hand-off); legacy `#player=<handle>` links still resolve through `findPlayerByGamertag`, which now REFUSES ambiguous handles — more than one account with the name returns an empty publicId (opens nobody) instead of the first match. No schema/migration changes.
- MAP (Ben, Nov 21, 2026 10:43–10:54 MST): "I don't wnat no compass no label simple as that" / "not no compass or table" / "update the current map it alr looks similar you just have to make them identical" / "go to gtabase" (https://www.gtabase.com/gta-6/map/) / "copy them. the exact same but make mine way better" / "evry location and evrything like it shoud be a copy paste the only differen should be my theme on the side tool". Changes, all in-place on the current map (base untouched — the tiles are already the GTADB / GTA VI Mapping Community map that GTABase's map is built from, so locations, coastline, roads and names on the tiles are the same source data): (1) the printed-poster furniture is REMOVED — the VISIT LEONIDA legend box, the star compass rose and the Great Seal block are deleted from the map layer, the now-dead `posterUnit` sizing constant and the stale compass comment went with them, and the credit line no longer mentions them; county/place/sea name labels stay (they are the map's own place names, in their spots). (2) The colour grade is retuned to GTABase's map palette (Ben's final reference, studied live by the parent agent at https://www.gtabase.com/gta-6/map/ — flat dusty blue-teal open water ~#4A9AB8, brighter sky-blue lakes ~#7FBFE6, sage land, grey-white urban blocks; this superseded the earlier poster-cyan direction for the water colour). The old chain (#0093ac sea) also blew the pale city fabric to pure white (proven by running the exact CSS filter + blend math over real CDN tiles and rendering mosaics — old fabric #fafffc/#ffffff, detail lost). Final grade: gentle chain `saturate(0.95) brightness(1.05)` + green hue tint at 0.16 day / 0.08 night + a dusty-teal SCREEN wash (#57b8c4, 0.40 day / 0.18 night, duplicated in the frozen-tile layer like the tint) — screen lifts dark water far more than pale land, so the deep sea lands on #499bb8 (GTABase's #4A9AB8) while the land stays sage and the fabric keeps its grey tone (#e0e8e8) and detail; `SEA_SURROUND_DAY` #499bb8, `SEA_NIGHT` #235367 recomputed through the full stacks. Known approximation: the tiles' own bright shallows stay brighter than GTABase's darker shallow halo — a global grade cannot darken shallows while lifting deep water, and the tiles themselves are unchanged. All features kept: left panel (ViceSunshine theme) with search, all pins, Show all / Close all, smooth zoom, Day/Night, Save-to-profile, ocean effects, fan-made labelling. The map stays a real interactive tiled map — no picture was swapped in, and Rockstar's poster image file is still not used.

## Automatic self-correction — new GTA news fixes anything ViceSunshine had wrong, by itself, regularly (Nov 21, 2026)
- Ben's exact words (Nov 21, 2026 09:24 MST): "Like every news that comes if smth we had was wrong change it update it automatically regularly". The site's existing automatic updates (news ingestion + Database sync) now include a correction pass: each time new GTA reporting arrives, the fresh sourced results are checked against what is already stored — map label names, Database entries, news stories and the team's own guides — and anything the new official / well-sourced information proves wrong is fixed in place automatically, on an ongoing basis. No button, no new schedule, no new sources, no extra search budget: the pass rides inside the existing `refreshNews` and `refreshDbEntries` runs (which fire on their existing crons — hourly news, 6-hourly Database sync — and whenever an open page asks the server to re-check its sources), throttled to at most one pass per 6 hours via meta `last_correction_pass_at`.
- Server (`server/src/actions.ts`): shared helper `runCorrectionPass(ctx, fresh)` + throttled wrapper `maybeRunCorrectionPass`. It dedupes the run's own fresh search results, snapshots the stored content (Database entries with kind/name/category/status/description, the 40 newest news stories, team guides with author "ViceSunshine Guides" and body ≤ 2600 chars, and the map's label names mirrored server-side in `MAP_LABEL_NAMES` from the client constants), and asks `ctx.inference.complete` for corrections ONLY where a report directly contradicts a stored fact and states the correct one. Server-side validation then enforces the labelling rules before anything is written: the correction's `sourceUrl` must be one of the run's own fresh result URLs, copied exactly; Database renames, map renames and "Confirmed in trailer" upgrades require the source host to be rockstargames.com; a Database status is never downgraded (same statusRank ladder as `refreshDbEntries`) and an entry already sourced from Rockstar is never overwritten by a weaker source; a news story's category is NEVER changed (a Rumor stays labelled Rumor — only title/summary text can be corrected); guide bodies are only replaced whole with the wrong fact changed; community content (player guides, posts, bugs, pins) is never touched. Cap: 10 corrections per run. Inference failure = zero corrections that run, never a guess.
- Every applied fix is logged in the new `content_corrections` table (migration `0070_content_corrections_log.sql`; schema `contentCorrections`): target type/label, field, old value, new value, source name + verbatim source URL, timestamp. Map label fixes are stored as overrides in meta `map_label_overrides` (original name → corrected name) and served by the new `getMapLabelOverrides` action; the client map renders its built-in labels (county, minor place and region labels + region card names) through those overrides — positions, pins and the fan-made / unverified labelling are untouched, only a proven-wrong name's text changes.
- New action `refreshCorrections` runs the same pass on demand with the exact same queries as the news refresh / Database sync (kept for the owner/agent and verification; the automatic path needs no trigger). `getAutomationStatus` now also returns `correctionsLastAt`, `correctionCount` and the 6 most recent corrections. Client (`client/src/App.tsx`): the News automation line reports the self-correction run time and total fixes; the Owner Dashboard's Automatic systems section gains a "Self-correction pass" row and a "Recent auto-corrections" list (what changed, from → to, when, source link) so Ben can watch it work without doing anything.

## Map restyled to Rockstar's official Leonida map look — turquoise sea, green land, white official county + place names (Nov 21, 2026)
- Ben, with an iPhone screenshot of the Muse Feed post "Rockstar's official Leonida map is out as a free download" showing the official map: "Update my map to look close to it" / "Including colors etc" / "Keep my features but only remove when needed" / "I want same details like gta so change my little details to make it look like gta rather than made uo" / "And especially names and so change them if you have to".
- Rules honoured: the base map is STILL the licensed YANIS V18 community map (tiles from the GTAMap.io CDN, GTADB CC BY 4.0) — Rockstar's official map image file was NOT swapped in, copied or traced, and no pin data was copied. The official look is matched with a colour grade over the licensed tiles plus ViceSunshine's own overlay labels carrying Rockstar's published names. The map stays labelled fan-made / not official / locations unverified everywhere it was before.
- Colour grade (client, `client/src/App.tsx`): new `MAP_GRADE_DAY` filter chain (`hue-rotate(-22deg) saturate(1.18) brightness(1.05)`) on the tile layer and the frozen-tile crossfade layer, plus a green hue-blend wash (`MAP_GRADE_TINT` #2eb357, `mix-blend-mode: hue`, opacity 0.22 day / 0.10 night, duplicated inside the frozen set so held tiles grade identically). The chain was tuned off-line against REAL tiles pulled from the tile CDN during the build: a plain hue-rotate alone turned the tiles' pale yellow-green land mustard, and a solved 3×3 colour matrix blew the saturated lake/shallow blues out to neon, so the final recipe is rotate + saturate + the hue wash, which samples the tiles' own sea (#2c68a6) out as turquoise and the land out as green. Night is the same grade with the old dark chain appended (`MAP_GRADE_NIGHT`). Stage surround constants recomputed through the full chain so the sea still fills the screen seamlessly at minimum zoom: `SEA_SURROUND_DAY` #067f84 (viewport background, day), `SEA_NIGHT` #023f45 (was #163356); the in-layer backdrop stays raw `SEA_DAY` because it is graded identically to the tiles.
- Names (the part Ben emphasised): new `MAP_COUNTY_LABELS` overlay — the five official counties Rockstar published (Lummox County, Leonard County, Mariana County, Kelly County, Vice-Dale County), drawn big, white, wide-tracked and centred like the official poster's county names, at ViceSunshine's approximate placements on the YANIS geography. `MAP_MINOR_LABELS` rebuilt with only real published GTA VI place names: Vice Beach, Watson Bay, Grassrivers and Lake Leonida kept; the made-up Key Lento and Goose Key are REMOVED; Hamlet, Southwind, Frogswater, Buckskin Swamp, Waning Sands, Millport, Belville, Grayson, Bonanza, Emerald Springs, Breakwater Quarry, Mile Zero Air Station, Draper Island, Capri and VCIA added (approximate positions, fan-made placements). Region and minor place labels are now white with a soft dark shadow in day mode too, matching the poster's white labels (they were dark green / dark grey before).
- Everything else kept exactly as it was (Ben: keep my features): left panel with search / Show all / Close all, the premium pin palette and schematic pins, smooth gesture zoom with the frozen-tile crossfade, Day / Night toggle, zoom stack, Show whole map + Fullscreen, measure / note tools, ocean shimmer, the notice pill, the credit line, and Save-to-profile map prefs with the guest Log in / Sign up popup. No server, schema or migration changes.
- Ben's follow-up in the same conversation ("every news that comes if smth we had was wrong change it update it automatically regularly") is a separate queued item (auto-correction from incoming GTA news) and is NOT part of this build.

## Recommendations show the realistic bots, never the owner's duplicates + bots made lifelike (Nov 21, 2026)
- Ben, with an iPhone screenshot of the Feed stories row (guest2107, guest4057, guest4… with grey default avatars and @ViceSunshi… handles): "But the recommendations are still these — there's no other account named ViceSunshineeditor or so. Keep them realistic and even make them post stories about the GTA and have posts and videos and followers. Just make it like real people."
- Root cause, proven in the sandbox before the fix: the ~40 duplicate accounts (editor-bootstrap devices all holding gamertag `ViceSunshineEditor`, display names still the automatic `guest####` placeholders) had LIVE test stories (screenshots Ben posted while testing), and the Stories row ranks unseen/story-holding people first — while the 200 seeded bots had no stories at all, so the duplicates filled every recommendation slot in the row. "Suggested for you" could surface them the same way through shared platform/region signals.
- Server (`server/src/actions.ts`): new shared matcher `isOwnerDuplicateAccount` — a non-bot profile whose gamertag (normalised) starts with `vicesunshine`, or whose display name is still a `guest####` placeholder. Wired into exactly the two recommendation surfaces: `listStories` (duplicates are skipped in the row's people list AND their story groups are dropped, so the viewer never swipes onto them; the viewer's OWN group always stays — their current account is untouched) and `getProfileSuggestions` (duplicates are never suggested). NO account is deleted or renamed; duplicates stay fully usable and findable everywhere else (search, profiles, their own Feed posts untouched).
- New server action `refreshBotCommunity` (no args) — keeps the seeded community lifelike, topping up only what is missing, real players never touched: (1) follows — every bot ends up with 100–1000 followers (mutual bot follows topped up until nobody sits under 110; sandbox run: 1,077 follows added, final range 110–186); (2) Feed posts with pictures — an original ViceSunshine illustration pool (6 landscape briefs: ocean-drive convertible, lowrider meet, causeway supercar, boardwalk, swamp truck, marina) generated once into owned blobs (meta `bot-post-art-pool`), 36 picture posts (Screenshot / Car Meet / Post) from 36 different members, each liked by 5–16 members and commented on, the picture carried in `image_blob_key` exactly like editorial news pictures so Feed + profile grids resolve it; (3) video posts — 18 posts sharing real GTA VI YouTube videos ALREADY sourced by the automatic Feed refresh (IDs read from stored feed posts, canonical watch URLs, played at the source, never re-uploaded; kinds Clip / Gameplay / Streamer Clip / Tutorial / Meme); (4) stories — a rotating cast (RNG seed rotates every 3 hours) posts fresh GTA stories (16 caption/sticker pairs: GTA VI news, gameplay, car meets, memes, launch week) until ~60 members hold a live story, ~70% carrying a portrait pool picture (6 briefs, meta `bot-story-art-pool`), the rest text stories, with 8–23 member views and 2–9 member likes per new story. Per run caps: 3 new pictures per pool, 40 stories — re-runs converge and then no-op (sandbox run 2: pools completed, stories topped 40→60, 0 duplicate posts/follows).
- Schedule: new space_action cron `vicesunshine-bot-community-refresh` every 3 hours (registered in `space.json` managedCronJobs) calling `refreshBotCommunity`, because stories expire after 24 hours; also declared as this build's post-submit action so the live data gets stories/posts/follows the moment the build lands.
- Bot stories → UNIQUE 5–10s CLIPS (owner's direction, Nov 21 2026: "make sure that the bots stories are unique and instead of generated images post clips on theirs story like 5-10sec story clip about gta"): `refreshBotCommunity`'s story section rewritten. Stories no longer use the generated portrait picture pool at all (the `bot-story-art-pool` generation is gone; the Feed picture pool is untouched). Each bot story is now a short clip window into a REAL GTA VI YouTube video: `mediaUrl = https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=<id>&t=<start>s&len=<5–10>`, `mediaType = "embed"`, played at the source, never re-uploaded. Clip sources, in order: (1) the persistent meta pool `bot-story-clip-pool` ({videoId, title} list, capped 300); (2) every YouTube video ID already stored in `feed_posts` (sourced earlier by `refreshFeedMedia` / member video posts, embed-checked when sourced); (3) only while the pool holds <72 clips, one web-search round (`site:youtube.com GTA VI shorts clip`, `site:youtube.com GTA 6 clip gameplay short` via the existing `webSearchResults` helper) whose candidates join ONLY after passing the existing `youtubeEmbeddable` oEmbed check (max 20 checks/run). Uniqueness is enforced against the live set: no two live bot stories share a video, a clip window (`videoId@start`, also remembered 72h), or a caption text (32 base lines × optional second sentences, or the clip's own stored title when it reads as a shareable line); a member never reposts their own last clip; if no unused clip exists the run posts nothing rather than duplicating or falling back to a picture. Retirement pass: any still-live bot story that is not a unique clip (old generated-picture / text stories, duplicate clips) is deleted with its views/likes/replies/highlight links (same cascade as `deleteStory`), so the next run replaces them with clips; real players' stories are never touched. Client (`client/src/App.tsx`): `YoutubePlayer` honours a link's `t`/`start` + `len` as embed `start`/`end` params (ordinary links unaffected); `StoryViewer` plays YouTube embed stories full-frame via the vertical `YoutubePlayer` (no Feed card chrome), holds a `&len=` clip for exactly its 5–10s window (progress bar + auto-advance; other embeds keep 15s), and story tiles (viewer Browse grid, highlight pick tiles) show the clip's YouTube thumbnail (`i.ytimg.com/vi/<id>/hqdefault.jpg`, the same thumbnail host the player facade already uses).
- Verified in the sandbox end-to-end: before the fix, `listStories` for a fresh viewer returned the 5 guest####/ViceSunshineEditor duplicates with live stories; after, the row is Carmen Thomas, Solar Coyote, Owen Costa, Henry Gonzalez, Emma Iversen (real names, avatar art, live unseen stories), 60 bot story groups with GTA captions/pictures/likes, zero duplicates anywhere; `getProfileSuggestions` returns bots only; a bot profile (MidnightLagoon07) shows a picture post + a YouTube gameplay post in Posts, the video in Clips, a live story, and follower counts in range (Carmen Thomas: 186 followers / 148 following).

## Test highlights deleted — 'lol' / 'zz' highlights + their test pictures only, system kept (Nov 21, 2026)
- Ben: "also remove every pic ive put on this web for highlights. no just thje pcitures i used to test the highlights and story. like the highlight i created yes but not the suystem. the highlights i created delete them. not the system." — delete the highlight(s) Ben created for testing (titles `lol`, `zz`) with their test pictures, but NOT the highlights system/feature itself.
- Server (`server/src/actions.ts`): new one-time action `deleteTestHighlights` (no args), guarded by meta flag `test-highlights-cleanup-2026-11-21-v1` so it runs exactly once (later calls return `alreadyDone: true`). It targets only `story_highlights` whose trimmed lowercased title is `lol` or `zz` AND whose `player_key` does NOT start with `bot-` (real accounts only; the seeded bot community is never touched). For each such highlight it deletes the `story_highlight_items` links and the highlight row, plus the cover ownership meta record (`hl-cover-owner:<coverBlobKey>`) when a cover was uploaded. Test pictures: a story counts as a test-only picture when EVERY highlight referencing it (via items or via `coverStoryId`) is one of the test highlights and it belongs to a test highlight's owner — those stories are fully deleted (`stories` row + `story_views` / `story_likes` / `story_replies` + any `album_media` row for the same player with the same `media_url`, so the picture also leaves the album pickers). A story that also appears in any kept highlight is NOT deleted; only the test highlight's link to it goes. No schema or migration change; no client change — `listHighlights` / `createHighlight` / `updateHighlight` / `deleteHighlight` / `uploadHighlightCover` and the whole profile highlights row / viewer / editor are untouched, so anyone can still create new highlights afterwards.
- Runs as this build's post-submit action against the live data; sandbox verification creates throwaway highlights/stories to prove the targeting (see build notes).

## Album full access in the pickers — whole album in New post / New clip / Add to story, kind-filtered (Nov 21, 2026)
- Ben, with screenshots of the near-empty "New post" picker and the Profile Photos / Videos tabs: why are not all my pictures from my album here — allow a feature where people can give full access to their album so ALL their album pictures show, not just session picks / the ViceSunshine album. Pressing Upload on the profile Photos tab must bring up the picker with pictures only and only pictures shown (album full access there too); pressing Upload on the Clips/Videos tab must bring up the same picker with videos only, no pictures, and the video posts to that category.
- Server (`server/src/actions.ts`, `server/src/schema.ts`, migration `0069_add_album_media_table.sql`): new `album_media` table (player_key, media_url, media_type image|video) holding a player's album ADDITIONS — media uploaded through the existing `uploadFeedMedia` and recorded by the new `addAlbumMedia`, which refuses any URL whose blob key does not carry a `feed-media-owner:` meta record naming that player (verified in the sandbox: another player's key is refused). New `listAlbumMedia` (own additions, newest first, deduped, cap 120). New `getAlbumAccess` / `setAlbumAccess`: the per-player full-access grant, stored in meta as `album-access:<playerKey>` = "full" | "limited" (default limited). No other schema changes.
- Client (`client/src/App.tsx`): shared `useAlbumAccess` (the grant + toggle mutation), `useFullAlbum` (merges the player's own posts, clips, story archive — the same sources/keys as the Add to story grid — plus `listAlbumMedia`) and `AlbumAccessPanel` (OFF: "See all your pictures/videos here" + "Give full album access" button; ON: status line + Turn off). `FeedCreator` pickers: the panel sits under the Recents row; with access on, the grid also shows the whole album kind-filtered by the session (Photos session → images only, Clips session / clip picker → videos only), tapping an album tile fetches its bytes and adds it as a normal creator item (clips 60-second rule checked from real metadata; story picker taps go straight to Preview), and device picks made while access is on are also uploaded + recorded into the album in the background (`savePickToAlbum`) so they show in the grid from then on. `StoryAddFlow`: same panel under the Recents header; with access on, album additions join the grid alongside the ViceSunshine album and Phone album picks are recorded into the album once their background upload lands. Profile: the Photos and Videos tabs keep their Upload button when the tab already has content (not just in the empty states), opening the same kind-restricted pickers as before (Photos → New post picker photos-only, Videos → New clip picker videos-only, publishing as kind "Clip" into the Videos category — that hand-off already existed via `vv-open-create`).
- Honest limit (unchanged platform fact): a web page cannot enumerate the phone's own photo library — device pictures enter through the phone's own picker (Select / Your library / Phone album tiles), where the phone itself shows its full album; full access governs everything ViceSunshine holds for the player plus everything they pick from then on.
- Verified: build compiled clean; sandbox probes — `getAlbumAccess` default false, `setAlbumAccess` true persists, `uploadFeedMedia` → `addAlbumMedia` → `listAlbumMedia` round-trip returns the item, cross-player `addAlbumMedia` refused with the ownership error. The signed-in picker tap-through was not driven in the sandbox (builders do not create test accounts — Ben's rule); Ben to tap-test: Profile Photos → Upload → Give full album access, and Clips → Upload (videos only).

## New highlight screen — cover, Edit cover, name, blue check (Nov 21, 2026)

Simple and easy.
- Client (`client/src/App.tsx`, `HighlightEditorSheet`): the picker is now two steps on the same full page (never a bottom sheet). Step 1 is the existing story grid (date boxes + selection circles) with a Next button instead of the old name field + save button. Step 2 is the New highlight screen: back arrow (returns to step 1), centred "New highlight" / "Edit highlight" title, blue (#0095f6) circular check button top right that saves (dimmed until a name is typed), a 190px circular cover preview, blue "Edit cover" text under it, and a centred borderless name field (placeholder "Highlights", Enter also saves). Editing an existing highlight only sends cover fields if the cover was actually touched, so a saved album cover is never wiped by a rename.

- Server (`server/src/actions.ts`): new `uploadHighlightCover` — JPG/PNG up to 4 MB, moderation-checked via `assertImageAllowed` BEFORE storage (same rule as avatar/banner uploads), stored as an owned blob under an opaque random key `highlight-covers/<uuid>-<name>` — the playerKey is a secret identity code and never appears in a served URL; ownership is recorded server-side in `meta` as `hl-cover-owner:<blobKey>` → playerKey. Returns `{ blobKey, url }`. `createHighlight` / `updateHighlight` accept `coverStoryId` (must be one of the player's own stories, else ignored) and `coverBlobKey` (accepted only when the meta ownership record names this player, else ignored). `listHighlights` resolves the cover in order: album upload blob → chosen cover story → the highlight's first story picture (the previous behaviour), and returns `coverStoryId` so the editor can preselect it.
- Verified: build compiled clean; sandbox action probes returned the expected profile-guard errors for `createHighlight` / `uploadHighlightCover` with an unknown player key and `{ items: [] }` from `listHighlights`.

No schema or migration change.
- Client (`client/src/App.tsx`): the accept buttons are now labelled **Approve** in both places (was Confirm in Notifications, Accept on the profile card), with aria-labels saying the person can then see your profile, posts and videos; the Notifications success notice reads "Approved — {name} can now see your profile, posts and videos." After an approval, the person stays listed in an "approved" list in BOTH places (Notifications Follow requests section and the profile card, which now stays rendered while anyone was just approved) with a **Follow back** control: Follow back → Following (tap to unfollow) or Requested (when their own account is private, tap to cancel), driven by the real `setFollow` response; the initial state comes from `viewerFollows` / the activity item's `followedByMe`. Approving from a Notifications activity-list `follow_request` row feeds the same approved list. The Notifications `followBack` mutation became a `followToggle` (follow/unfollow) that also tracks this state. Decline/Delete behaviour unchanged.

## Private account with follow requests (Nov 21, 2026)
- Ben: in Profile settings, a private account option — visitors can still open the profile, but it says "private account" and shows none of the owner's content; a Follow tap sends a follow REQUEST that waits until the owner accepts, and once accepted the requester can see the profile — the owner does NOT have to follow back. Refinements from Ben in the same conversation: a private profile still shows the username, bio, joined date and followers count; who they follow / who follows them (the lists) stays hidden — only the count numbers show.
- What already existed: `profiles.profile_private` with a checkbox in Edit profile → Profile settings and a toggle in Settings → Permissions, instant-follow in `setFollow`, and server-side content gating for non-followers (posts, stories, highlights, Feed). Missing: the request/accept flow, bio visibility, list locking, and the locked-profile presentation.
- Schema (`server/src/schema.ts`, migration 0067): new `follow_requests` table (`requester_key`, `following_key`, `created_at`) — one row = a pending request. No row is ever created for public accounts.
- Server (`server/src/actions.ts`):
  - `setFollow` now branches on the target's `profilePrivate`: public → instant follow as before; private → creates a `follow_requests` row and returns `{ following: false, requested: true }` (response gained `requested`). `follow: false` unfollows, or cancels a pending request when not following. Blocking (in `blockUser`) also deletes pending requests both ways.
  - New `listFollowRequests({ playerKey })`: the owner's waiting requests, newest first, with requester profile basics. New `respondToFollowRequest({ playerKey, requesterKey, accept })`: accept creates the real `profile_follows` row + signed `follow` event (one-way — no reciprocal follow) and deletes the request; decline just deletes it.
  - `getSocialProfile`: response gained `viewerRequested` and `followListsVisible`. The bio is now returned to private-locked visitors too (only a block in either direction blanks it); name, username, joined date (`memberSince`), `followersCount` / `followingCount` were already always returned. Location, region, playstyle, links and crewmates stay blanked while locked, as before.
  - `listFollowList`: returns `{ locked: true, items: [] }` for a private target when the viewer is neither the owner nor an accepted follower (blocks lock too); items gained `viewerRequested` / `isPrivate` per row. `getProfileSuggestions` items gained `isPrivate`.
- Client (`client/src/App.tsx`): `FollowButton` takes `viewerRequested` / `isPrivate` — on a private account it reads "Requested" after a tap (server answer wins via the mutation response) and tapping "Requested" cancels; suggestions cards do the same. Another player's locked profile: header keeps name / @username / bio / Joined / counts, counts render as plain text (lists never open), a lock panel replaces the posts grid and stats card ("This account is private" + why + the Follow/Requested button), and the header note explains the request flow ("they don't have to follow you back" / "your request is waiting"). Own profile: a "Follow requests" card (renders only when requests are waiting) with Accept / Decline per person. Edit profile's setting is relabelled "Private account" with the full rule spelled out; Settings → Permissions' toggle is renamed the same way with a matching hint.
- Verified end-to-end in the sandbox with seeded bot accounts: set a bot private → a non-follower's `getSocialProfile` showed bio + counts with `canViewPosts: false`, `followListsVisible: false`, region/playstyle/friends/links blanked; `setFollow` returned `requested: true` with the follower count unchanged; `listFollowList` returned `locked: true`, `listProfilePosts` empty; the owner saw the request in `listFollowRequests`; cancel cleared it; re-request + `respondToFollowRequest` accept made the viewer a follower (`viewerFollows: true`, posts and lists open, count restored). Test state was cleaned up afterwards (target back to public, follow restored to its seeded state). The separate Notifications tab Ben asked for next is where these requests will also surface — not part of this build.

## Crew tag replaces the personal clan tag everywhere (Nov 21, 2026)
- Ben: "remove clan tag completely in this section [Edit profile]. Show how Crew tag works: it will be chosen when a crew is created — for instance I'm creating a crew tag named [lol], everyone that joins including me should have that tag, and when you leave it goes away, and when you go to another crew you get the new crew['s tag]. Also make the tag appear next to your name, for instance [lol]zazz, and the tag colour can also be chosen — that should be located when creating a crew." Then, asked "Crew tag or clan tag?", Ben: "ok do it change all" — the name is **Crew tag** everywhere user-facing.
- What already made this true server-side: a member's tag was always derived live from their crew membership (`clanMembers → clans.tag / clans.color`) in `getProfile` / `getSocialProfile` — joining inserts the membership row, leaving deletes it, and a member can only be in one crew (`assertNotInAnotherCrew`), so leave → tag gone, new crew → new tag was already the mechanics. The leak was the SEPARATE personal tag (`profiles.clan_tag`, set in Edit profile, returned as `ownClanTag`, shown as its own badge) — that is what this build removes.
- Server (`server/src/actions.ts`): `updateProfile` no longer accepts, screens, gates or returns a tag at all (request/response/handler cleaned; its description states there is deliberately no crew tag there); `getProfile` and `getSocialProfile` no longer return `ownClanTag`; the `nameChangeGate` "clan tag" branch and the 30-day clan-tag cooldown are deleted (display-name lock untouched); the Ask Vice site description now explains the Crew tag rule instead of the old 30-day rule. `createClan` / `updateClan` now store the Crew tag EXACTLY as typed (`lol` stays `lol` — uniqueness stays case-insensitive) instead of forcing uppercase, and their taken-tag errors say "Crew tag". No migration: `profiles.clan_tag` / `clan_tag_changed_at` stay as dormant legacy columns (comment in `server/src/schema.ts` updated) so existing rows keep loading; the crew's tag + colour remain `clans.tag` / `clans.color`.
- Client (`client/src/App.tsx`): the Edit profile "Clan tag" field, its state, sync and save payload are gone completely. Create Crew: the field is labelled "Crew tag (2–4 characters)" with placeholder `lol` and no forced uppercase; the colour picker is labelled "Crew tag colour"; a "How the Crew tag works" box in the form plus a live line under the colour picker show `[tag]yourname` in the picked colour and explain join = get it, leave = lose it, another crew = that crew's tag. Profile headers (yours and other players', top bar and main name): the crew tag now renders as a coloured prefix stuck to the name — `[lol]zazz` — and the old separate tag badge pills (personal + crew) are removed. Crew page: a "Crew tag [tag] — every member shows it in front of their name" line under the crew name, a joining note ("Joining gives you this crew's Crew tag…"), and the Leave confirmation now says the tag comes off your name when you leave (and that joining another crew gives that crew's tag instead). Internal API/DB names (`clanTag`, `clans`) are unchanged — only user-facing wording changed to Crew tag.

## Search fixed + finish-your-sentence suggestions (Nov 21, 2026)
- Ben (screenshot of the Feed with its search box open): "the search does not look like its working i cannot search anything and also put the feature where it helps u finished ur sentence with multiple possibilities reading everything u write and suggest."
- Root cause of "cannot search anything": every search matched the WHOLE typed phrase as one exact substring of a single field (`title/body/author.includes(whole phrase)` in `globalSearch`, `listFeed`, `listNews`, `listBugs`, `listEggs`, `listGuides`, `listClans`, `listDbEntries`, `listEvents`, and a client-side `includes` in Clips). Any natural query — two words living in different fields, words in a different order, an @handle, the start of a sentence — returned nothing, and a failed search request rendered nothing at all (no error state), so search looked dead.
- Fix (server, one shared matcher in `server/src/actions.ts`): `searchNorm` / `searchTokens` / `searchScore` / `searchMatches` — queries are normalised (case, @/#, punctuation), filler words dropped, and a row matches when its combined searchable fields cover the query words (exact phrase scores highest; 2 words must both appear; 3+ words may miss one, scored by coverage). `globalSearch` now also searches more fields per group (kind, category, source, description, region, type, status…) and ranks each group by score. The same matcher now backs `listFeed`, `listNews`, `listBugs`, `listEggs`, `listGuides`, `listClans`, `listDbEntries` (description added) and the name path of `listEvents`, so tapping a global-search result lands on a section list that agrees with the search instead of an empty page. Clips' client filter is word-based the same way.
- New action `getSearchSuggestions({ query, limit })`: autocomplete built ONLY from real stored content — recorded search terms from `search_terms` (with their real counts, popular first), the curated trending topics, profile display names / gamertags / @handles, Database entry names, news titles, Feed post titles + authors, crew names + tags, guide titles, event titles and bug titles. Ranking: exact → starts-with → word-start → all-words → contains, with recorded searches boosted. Nothing is invented: a completion exists only because that content or search exists.
- Client (`client/src/App.tsx`): the global Search overlay shows a "Suggestions — tap to finish your search" list while typing (tapping completes the words in the box and the live grouped results switch to the completion), and both the overlay and the full Search results view now show a "Search didn't load — Try again" state on request failure instead of blank nothing. The Feed/Clips `SocialTopBar` search shows the same suggestions dropdown under its box (tapping one finishes the words and filters the list immediately). No schema or migration change; no new external source.

## Edit profile Save fixed: your own username read as "already taken" (Nov 21, 2026)
- Ben (screenshot of the Edit profile sheet, Name "lol", Clan tag VICE): "the profile saving is not working for me — nothing is save like it cant save." Reproduced exactly in the sandbox before fixing: `bootstrapEditorAccount` for a fresh device key, then the sheet's first save call `updateProfile` with the account's own unchanged gamertag failed with "That username is already taken — every ViceSunshine username is different, so pick another one." Because the sheet's Save runs `updateProfile` first and `saveProfile` second, the throw killed the whole save — name, bio, clan tag, everything — and the sheet stayed open.
- Root cause: `assertUsernameAvailable` (`server/src/actions.ts`) excluded only the caller's own player key. Ben's editor account is one of ~40 duplicate profiles all holding the gamertag "ViceSunshineEditor" — the editor bootstrap's fallback (`["ViceSunshine","ViceSunshineHQ","ViceSunshineBen"].find(not taken) ?? "ViceSunshineEditor"`) hands that same name to every later device key once the first three are taken (audits and new browser contexts each mint a fresh key). So the check saw OTHER rows with Ben's own name and refused every save. The same unconditional check also sat in `saveProfile` and `updateSettings`, so those saves were broken for the same accounts.
- Fix (one place, the helper): `assertUsernameAvailable` now returns early when the caller's existing profile already holds exactly the submitted name (trimmed, case-insensitive) — keeping your own current username is never "taking" it. Claiming a name you do not already hold is unchanged: verified in the sandbox that a different key creating a profile with gamertag "ViceSunshineEditor" is still refused with the same message, and `createAccount`'s sign-up check is untouched. No client, schema or data change; no duplicate rows were deleted (they are separate device accounts by design).
- Verified end to end in the sandbox with the exact calls the sheet makes: `updateProfile` (displayName "lol", bio, clanTag "VICE") → ok; `saveProfile` → ok; `getProfile` reads back displayName "lol", the bio and ownClanTag "VICE" — the values persist on the account.

## MUSE-ONLY editor account: always signed in here, never on the live site (Nov 20, 2026 23:58 MST) — REMOVED Nov 21, 2026, see the removal entry at the top of this file
- Ben's direction, verbatim intent: here in Muse he wants to ALWAYS be signed in to an account — "an account already signed in instead of me continuing doing it, just for editing, this will not go on the live version, this will let me access crew, profile, settings to do changes." Reason: the sign-up email codes do not arrive inside Muse, so he could never finish creating an account here; the live website's sign-in works and is NOT changed by this.
- Implementation (this artifact only — DO NOT port `bootstrapEditorAccount` or the client bootstrap effect to the standalone live website): server action `bootstrapEditorAccount` in `server/src/actions.ts` creates a full account bound to the CALLER'S OWN device player key — it grants no credential and can never return or unlock anyone else's account (an earlier draft handed out one shared fixed key to every viewer; the security review rejected that and it was replaced before shipping). Only device-minted keys (`p-…`, as `playerKey()` in `client/src/App.tsx` generates) are accepted. The account is created exactly like a real sign-up minus the email step: gamertag `ViceSunshine` (fallbacks `ViceSunshineHQ`, `ViceSunshineBen` if a community profile holds the name), no email and no password (both can be added later in Settings — `setPassword` needs no current password when none exists), 500 starting Vice Credits with the standard welcome credit log entry. The client shell calls it once on load only while the device has no account of its own, then re-reads the profile in place (staying on the open page). An explicit Log out is respected for the rest of that session; the next fresh open is signed in again automatically. A device already signed in to a different real account is never overridden.
- The editor profile is a normal non-bot account: if a future test-account cleanup ever deletes it, the next open simply recreates it fresh via the same bootstrap path.

## Database v5 repaint fix: why "nothing changed", fixed and proven (Nov 20, 2026)
- Ben's screenshot (00:57 UTC) showed the 556 detail page with the EXACT old neon pink/teal rifle. Diagnosis from the inspection evidence bundle + production data: (1) production meta had NO `db_art_v5_ids` key at all — the v5 repaint had never run on the live data (the previous build only verified it in the sandbox); (2) the v5 queue sat BEHIND the leftover character-redo queue in `seedArtwork`, so scheduled runs painted other passes first; (3) batch progress was saved only once at the end of a 12-image batch, so a cut-short run lost its bookkeeping; (4) generation failures were swallowed by a bare `catch`, leaving no trace; (5) `generateDbArtFor` repainted over the SAME blob key (`db-art/entry-<id>`), so the picture URL never changed and browsers/CDNs kept serving the cached old image even after a repaint.
- Fixes (`server/src/actions.ts`): every repaint now lands under a FRESH versioned blob key `db-art/entry-<id>-v<n>` (counter in meta `db_art_versions`) — new key = new URL = the new picture actually displays; `seedArtwork` queue reordered to missing → v5 (weapons first) → character → v4 → v3, with progress saved to meta after EVERY successful repaint; generation failures are recorded in meta `db_art_last_error` (entry id, name, message, time) instead of swallowed. New action `repaintDbEntry({ entryId })` repaints one named entry immediately under the v5 brief, marks it done in all queues and returns the fresh picture URL — declared as this build's post-submit action for entry 3 (the 556) so the owner's test case is repainted on the live data the moment the build lands; the existing 15-minute `leonida-hub-artwork-generation` schedule then works through the remaining v5 queue (weapons → vehicles → characters → locations, 12 per run, progress kept per item).
- TRN side-by-side grounding (Ben: "use TRN as a reference", "look at their browse side by side and do it, item by item"): TRN's own 556 page (tracker.gg/gta6/weapons/556, read in-build) lists the 556 as Assault Rifles, manufacturer Duke, $1,450, 30-round mag — the Duke 556, modelled on the Colt LE6920 / M4A1. The "556" spec was rewritten to that exact pattern (carry handle, triangular front sight post, ribbed handguard, straight STANAG magazine, matte black, no attachments); the "ADB FRA" spec was rewritten to the FN FAL pattern its real basis uses (long barrel, slotted flash hider, curved magazine, straight in-line stock, explicitly NOT an AR — the first FAL attempt came back AR-shaped because the spec mentioned a carry handle).
- Verified with my own eyes in the sandbox (not just action return values): the repainted 556 is a plain matte-black M16/M4-pattern rifle — carry handle, front sight post, ribbed handguard, magazine ahead of the grip, worn military finish, ZERO neon/pink/teal — on the unchanged flat dark-navy ViceSunshine weapon background, served from the new URL `db-art/entry-3-v1`. The repainted ADB FRA (v2) reads as a real FAL-pattern battle rifle on the same background. A timed-out sandbox batch still kept the 3 repaints it finished (per-item saving proven). Sandbox invoke path caps at 120s (~3–4 pictures per call); the production schedule's runs have a longer budget (a 327s run is on record).
- Legal line kept, as in every previous build and Ben's own standing rule that ViceSunshine must stay legally safe: the pictures remain original ViceSunshine illustrations drawn identical to the real GTA VI item on ViceSunshine's own background/theme — Rockstar's / TRN's actual artwork files are never hosted, and every entry keeps its "See original picture" link out. Ben's repeated instruction to host Rockstar's exact files was not followed, for the reason already given to him: that is the one thing that could get the site taken down.

- Ben (with the Duke 556 example): ViceSunshine's 556 was a neon pink/teal sci-fi rifle while the real GTA VI 556 is a plain matte-black M16-pattern rifle — every Database picture must show the item itself identical to the real GTA VI item (same shape, proportions, factory colours, materials, details; instantly recognisable), with NO neon, glow or sci-fi restyling on the item. Backgrounds are ViceSunshine's own and stay exactly as they were (dark studio backdrop / flat dark navy for weapons / in-world settings for vehicles / character studio framing) — colour and mood live in the background and lighting only, never on the item. Legal line kept (Ben's standing rule): the art remains original ViceSunshine illustration drawn to match the real item from a fresh angle — Rockstar's own artwork files are never hosted; every entry keeps its "See original picture" link out to the real thing.
- Implementation (`server/src/actions.ts`): new `DB_ART_IDENTICAL_RULE` embedded in `DB_ART_STYLE` and `DB_ART_STYLE_FLAT`; Customise avatars split onto their own `AVATAR_ART_STYLE` (the old neon brief) so they are untouched by the Database rule. Spec corrections in `DB_ART_SPECS`: "556" rewritten as the realistic matte-black M16-pattern Duke 556 (conventional layout, magazine ahead of the grip — the old spec wrongly said bullpup), "ADB FRA" as a realistic matte-black tactical rifle, "Lectro" as a plain dark electric sports motorcycle, "Railgun" as a bulky matte-gunmetal experimental railgun with no glowing parts. Repaint queue in `seedArtwork`: meta key `db_art_v5_ids`, `v5Pending` ordered Weapons → Vehicles → Characters → Locations, priority missing art → character queue → v5 → v4 → v3, batch of 12 per run via the existing `leonida-hub-artwork-generation` schedule; `generateDbArtFor` reuses the same `db-art/entry-<id>` blob key. Verified in the sandbox: `seedArtwork` returns dbGenerated > 0 and advances the v5 set (media generation is slow, so a single sandbox call completes one repaint; the scheduled job rolls the rest out). Honest limit: the repaint is a rollout — entries still show their previous art until their v5 turn comes.
- Detail pages (`renderDetailPage` in `client/src/App.tsx`): the overview card is now TRN Sentinel XS-style — `lg:grid-cols-[1.6fr_1fr]`, the picture taking the large left share (~62%) with name / manufacturer / class, description, performance and rating organised on the right; character pictures' height cap raised 620px → 680px. Phone still stacks picture over info. No content or background changes.
- Search: every Database section (Vehicles, Weapons, Characters, Locations and All entries) already carries its own search box at the top ("Search vehicles…", "Search entries…" for All), filtering instantly through `listDbEntries` (name, category, manufacturer, type, case-insensitive substring) — verified in the sandbox: kind=Weapon + search "556" returns exactly the 556 entry.

## Sign-in gate: TRN's exact account process + TRN-style code email (Nov 20, 2026)
- Ben (with 7 TRN screenshots): copy TRN's account creation / sign-in process step by step, in ViceSunshine's own branding (VS logo, ViceSunshine colours, officialvicesunshine@gmail.com as sender) — including the email itself. `AuthGatePage` in `client/src/App.tsx` is now a self-contained stage machine (previously it only asked for the email and handed off): (1) "WHAT'S YOUR EMAIL?" — Email box, Continue, "or" divider, "Sign in with Username →" button; Continue runs the existing `checkEmail`. (2) New email → "CREATE NEW ACCOUNT?" — "No existing account found. Would you like to register a new account for [email]?" with Back and Create New. (3) Create New → "VERIFY YOUR EMAIL" — "To register your account, please enter the code we sent to [email]", the shared `SixDigitCode` boxes, Back and Resend with a live 60s countdown ("Resend (57s)"); the code is issued by the existing `requestEmailCode` (purpose "verify") and checked WITHOUT consuming by the NEW `checkGateCode` action (peek, same 5-attempt limit) — on success the gate hands {email, emailCode} to `SignUpView` via the existing `vv-auth-open` device key, and `createAccount` consumes that exact code so the account starts with its email verified (no second code). (4) Existing email → "WELCOME BACK!" — "To sign into your account, please enter the code we sent to [email]", 6 boxes, Back and Resend (countdown); entering the page fires `requestLoginCode`, and the code signs in code-only through the existing `signInWithPassword` (emailCode path), adopting the account in place via the shell's `handleSignedIn` — no reload. (5) "Sign in with Username" → "USERNAME SIGN IN" — Email or Username box, Password box (the shared `PasswordInput` with show/hide eye), Back and Sign In, "Forgot your username or password?" below (hands off to SignUpView's existing reset flow, email prefilled); a correct password moves to the WELCOME BACK! code screen to finish (the server's needsEmailCode path), and 2-step accounts get a gate TOTP stage. Remember this device / Trust this device checkbox sits on the welcome, username and TOTP stages and drives the same `leonida-trust-device` / signed-out-tombstone storage the log-in form uses. Existing rules kept: username permanent, Gmail-only sign-up, password rules, 6-digit Gmail codes.
- The email (`deliverEmailCode` in `server/src/actions.ts`): registration subject is now exactly "Welcome to ViceSunshine! Here is your registration code!" (sign-in: "Welcome back to ViceSunshine! Here is your sign-in code!"; reset: "ViceSunshine — here is your password reset code!"). The spawned Gmail-connector delivery task sends ONE HTML email (`+send --html`) from officialvicesunshine@gmail.com only. The emailed code is the same `email_codes` row the verify/welcome screens check — one code, stored only as a hash, 10-minute life. Honest limit (unchanged): real delivery still requires officialvicesunshine@gmail.com to be connected in the Gmail connector; the delivery task reports plainly when it is not and never sends from another address. No schema change (checkGateCode reuses `email_codes` + `peekEmailCode`).
- **New-account path restored to the exact TRN process + lands on Profile (Nov 20, 2026, owner's TRN screenshots; this supersedes the interim no-code sign-up from the account-rescue build)**: (1) The "CREATE NEW ACCOUNT?" screen is now exactly TRN's — VS logo, heading, "No existing account found. Would you like to register a new account for [email]?", Back + Create New, nothing else (the extra explanatory paragraph about no-code sign-up was removed). (2) Pressing Create New now opens a NEW gate stage "verify" — "VERIFY YOUR EMAIL": "To register your account, please enter the code we sent to [email]", the 6 `SixDigitCode` boxes, Back (to the Create New screen) and Resend with the live 60s countdown. Entering the screen fires `requestEmailCode` (purpose "verify", registration subject, the new branded HTML email); the typed code is checked with `checkGateCode` (peek, 5-attempt limit) and, when accepted, kept and passed as `emailCode` to `createAccount`, which consumes that exact code — so the emailed code IS the code the screen accepts — and the account starts with `emailVerified = 1`. Then username → password → created. (3) Finishing lands the player straight on their Profile tab: the gate's `adopt` → shell `handleSignedIn` already does `setTab("profile")`, and the Profile tab's existing Edit profile sheet (display name, bio, location, avatar/banner, linked accounts) is where they change their name and details. Server descriptions for `requestEmailCode` / `createAccount` and the handler comment were updated to describe this flow (an empty emailCode still creates the account unverified with a Settings confirmation code emailed, for non-gate paths).
- **Existing-account login re-confirmed exactly TRN-style (Nov 20, 2026, owner's TRN screenshots)**: the gate's flow for an existing account is (1) "WHAT'S YOUR EMAIL?" — VS logo, email box (kept filled), Continue, "or" divider, "Sign in with Username →"; Continue on a known email sends the login code and moves to (2) "WELCOME BACK!" — "To sign into your account, please enter the code we sent to [email]", the 6 `SixDigitCode` boxes, and ONLY Back + Resend with the live 60s countdown ("Resend (56s)") under them, all in ViceSunshine branding (VS logo, ViceSunshine colours — no TRN branding). This edit streamlined that screen to match: the extra full-width Sign In button was removed (typing/pasting the 6th digit already submits via `SixDigitCode` onComplete → `signInWithPassword` emailCode path) and the Remember-this-device row was removed from this screen (the preference still defaults from `leonida-trust-device` and is offered on the Username, TOTP and create screens). One small fallback link stays under the buttons so a player whose email never arrives is never locked out: "Use my password instead" (email path → Username sign in) or "Can't get the code? Finish with my password only" (password already typed → skipEmailCode sign-in) — a deliberate, recorded deviation from the pixel-exact TRN screen, kept from the earlier account-rescue build.
- **Email redesign (Nov 20, 2026, owner: the old email "looks so fake"; then: go further than his screenshot of it)** `deliverEmailCode` now builds a professional branded HTML email — email-client-safe table layout, inline styles, no external images. Dark navy (#0a0a12) shell matching the site; the 600px dark card (#12121f) is wrapped in a 2px hot pink → neon purple → teal GRADIENT BORDER (outer gradient table cell, inner card). Header: an 88px pink→purple gradient VS logo block with WHITE "VS" letters (the screenshot's black letters were Gmail dark-mode inversion of the old build), the "ViceSunshine" wordmark (Sunshine in hot pink) and a "YOUR GTA VI AGENT" teal tagline. Purpose headline: "Welcome to ViceSunshine!" (sign-up), "Welcome back to ViceSunshine!" (sign-in), "Verify your email", "Reset your password". The code is shown ONCE in six compact dark boxes (42×52px, 26px white digits, 10px radius, 6px gaps — made smaller and neater Nov 20, 2026 per the owner; the old duplicate letter-spaced code line under the boxes was removed because it made Gmail previews read the code twice). Under the boxes: NO button or banner — the pink-purple "Verify your email" gradient pill was removed completely Nov 20, 2026 per the owner (it read as a confusing fake button); only the line "Enter the code above in ViceSunshine to continue." remains under the code. Then an "expires in 1 minute" pill, a never-share security note, and a full footer: ViceSunshine name, official sender address, why-you-got-this line, the unofficial fan-made / not-affiliated-with-Rockstar-or-Take-Two disclaimer, and a © year line. A hidden preheader carries the single line "Your verification code is: [CODE] — enter it in ViceSunshine within 1 minute." so inbox previews read cleanly and Gmail can still surface its Copy code chip. The plain-text fallback was restyled to match (branded header, code, expiry + security lines, signed footer). All three purposes (verify/signup, login, reset) share the design with their own subject, headline, code label, CTA and intro. Delivery path unchanged (spawned Gmail-connector task, official address only).

## Map: fast-zoom stability, Show/Close all, seamless sea, subtle ocean, guest Log in popup (Nov 20, 2026)
- Ben: (1) fast mouse-wheel zoom glitches — must stay smooth and stable, no jumping, broken tiles or lag; (2) the panel needs Show all AND Close all; (3) zoomed all the way out (his screenshot, Night mode) the land sat tiny inside a big surround of a DIFFERENT dark blue with a visible rectangle edge — at minimum zoom the sea must fill the whole screen in one colour, no dark border; (4) subtle ocean effects — gentle, barely noticeable, not distracting; (5) guests who try to save get a small popup with Log in first and Sign up as an option.
- Fast-zoom fix — frozen-tile crossfade: the glitch was the tile-set swap at each committed zoom (new detail level's images arriving late = holes/flashes). Now, whenever the committed zoom changes (wheel, +/− buttons, centre-on), the previous tile set stays rendered above the new one, transformed by the exact view-change (`translate(pan′ − k·pan) scale(k)` about the stage centre — derived from the px/py math, so it lands pixel-exact), then fades out at 480ms and unmounts at 700ms once the new level has loaded. No holes, no broken-tile flash, no jump.
- Show all / Close all: the pins-count row now carries both — Show all clears every hidden group + category (existing behaviour); Close all hides ALL groups + ALL categories (0 pins, clusters and egg pins included, since they follow the Places group). Re-showing a single category row now also re-opens its group, so one tap recovers a category after Close all.
- Seamless sea: the visible rectangle was the Night filter — tiles are darkened by a CSS filter but the surround was the flat `SEA_NIGHT`, a different blue. Fixes: a sea backdrop in the exact tile sea colour now sits INSIDE the filtered tile layer (whole viewport, filtered identically), `SEA_NIGHT` recomputed to the filtered sea (#163356) for the Art-off case, the edge vignette from the premium pass was REMOVED (it read as the "dark border"), and minimum zoom raised 0.55 → 0.6. Day mode was already exact (#2c68a6 = tile sea).
- Ocean: `.map-ocean` — two faint repeating line fields drifting opposite ways (34s loop) over the water at 5% opacity by day / 3.5% at night, inside the map layer above the tiles and below the pins; animation off under prefers-reduced-motion. Deliberately barely noticeable.
- Guest popup: MapView now receives `gamertag`; tapping "Save my map setup" without an account opens a small centred popup immediately (Log in as the primary gradient button, Sign up as the secondary option, X / backdrop to dismiss — both route to the sign-up tab, which hosts the log-in form), and a refused save from the server lands in the same popup. The old inline need-account text in the panel footer is gone.
- Note on the earlier audit finding ("new tab"): a literal new browser tab is not possible inside the artifact shell (the artifact has no shareable per-page URL and the shell owns tabs); the user's own clarification — a full web screen of its own with a Back button returning to the previous page at the same place — is what is built and kept.

## Map: full-screen tab + Back, smooth gestures, premium pins, profile-saved setup (Nov 20, 2026)
- Ben: (1) the full map must open in its own full-screen tab, not cramped inside the page, with a clear Back button returning to the page he came from at the same place; (2) the map lags — panning, zooming, panel and pins must be smooth even on phone; (3) pin colours look cheap — richer, cleaner, premium, and the whole map theme properly finished; (4) a Save button so the map setup (done pins, visible categories, filters) saves to his profile and comes back as he likes it.
- Full-screen map tab: the map card is now a fixed full-viewport screen (100dvh, dialog) whenever open. While open, the page behind is scroll-locked and Esc closes it, so closing returns to the exact same place. Back lives in the panel header and, when the panel is closed, in the top-left cluster beside "Map panel". The shell passes `onBack` (back-stack) like every other detail view, and Database's "open map" now enters via `goDetail("map")`, so Back from a Database-launched map returns to Database at the same scroll spot; from the Map page itself Back just closes the screen onto the untouched page. Region cards' "Show on map" now opens the full-screen map and centres the region.
- Smoothness: pan and wheel-zoom no longer setState per pointermove/tick (that re-rendered 1,020 pin buttons + tiles + panel every frame — the lag). Gestures now drive ONE wrapper div via a GPU `transform` from refs (zero React renders mid-gesture); pan commits once on pointer-up, wheel zoom accumulates and commits once after 140ms idle as a zoom-about-centre (pan vector scaled by the same factor, so nothing jumps when the state render lands). Tiles also decode async.
- Premium pins: the whole `MAP_PIN_CATS` palette replaced — deep jewel tones at consistent depth (emerald, burnt orange, sapphire, wine, violet, crimson…), white pictograms on every saturated tile, dark only on the two pale tiles (Convenience ivory, Landmarks gold, Fast food amber). Every tile surface (map pins, panel rows, search results, pin card) now shares one finish: `pinTileBg` top-light gradient + `PIN_TILE_SHADOW` hairline edge / inset highlight; pins are 28px, radius 8. Panel gained an inset top highlight; a subtle edge vignette sits over the stage. Base-map colours are untouched (they are the YANIS tiles' own, per the entry below).
- Save my map setup: new `account_saves` kind `mapprefs` (refId 0; meta limit raised 2,000 → 40,000 for the done-pins JSON; no migration — kind is a text column). MapView loads the account's setup once on open and applies it over device defaults (done pins union-merged with the device list), and the panel footer carries the Save button ("Save my map setup" → "Saved to your profile ✓"; a dirty state lights it pink when the setup changed). Guests get an inline create-account/log-in prompt (the server's requireProfile is the authority, as everywhere else). Saves: donePins, hidden groups, hidden categories, Day/Night, Pins/Art/Grid/Labels layers.

## Clips alignment: For You reel starts below the header, exactly like Following (Nov 19, 2026)
- Ben (with screenshots): on For You the TikTok video started higher than the content on Following and the banner overlapped the video. Cause: when clips exist, `ClipsView` rendered the header (banner, "Clips" title, Following/For You tabs) INSIDE the snap scroller as its first block, on the reel's purple gradient — so the video area began right under the tabs inside the reel frame; the Following empty state instead renders the same header in normal page flow with its content below it. Fix: the header now sits ABOVE the reel in normal flow on both tabs — `ClipsView` (clips present) is a viewport-height flex column (`h-[calc(100dvh-5.5rem)]`, lg `6.5rem`): header `flex-none`, reel wrapper `min-h-0 flex-1` with the snap scroller at `h-full`. The first video therefore starts at the exact spot Following's content starts — below the banner/header, no overlap — and swipe-up snap behaviour inside the reel is unchanged. This supersedes the earlier "banner inside the scroller so it scrolls away" arrangement for Clips. (Also hardened the Codes detail Back restore: the saved list scroll position is now applied in an effect after the list re-mounts, instead of during the click, where the shorter detail view clamped it to the top.)
