## Clan Wars removed → Community Events hosting + discovery (edit, Nov 19 2026)
- Clan Wars removed completely at the owner's direction: the `ClanWarSection` UI, the live Clan vs Clan scoreboard, and the server actions `getClanWar` / `joinClanWar` / `closeClanWar` are deleted; `recordWarActivity`, `WAR_POINTS`, `WAR_CHALLENGES`, `ensureCurrentWar` and `clanWarsWonMap` are deleted and every call site removed — no war points are counted anywhere anymore. All user-facing copy that mentioned Clan Wars / war points / "Event / War Rewards" was reworded (Rankings, How to Earn, Clan Perks, profile stats — the "Clan Wars won" tile became "Events held" — About, Terms, Ask Vice rules and chips). The `clan_wars` / `clan_war_entries` / `war_activity` tables remain in the schema as unused legacy tables (historical rows are not deleted). Seasonal Rankings (Global/Local/PlayStation/Xbox) and the separate Green vs Purple faction standings were NOT part of this removal and remain. One legacy seeded example event still carries its old "Clan War Showcase Night" title in stored data; its type is presented as "Clan Event" and fresh seeds use a Screenshot Contest example instead.
- Community Events (migration 0030: `events.visibility` Public/Private, `events.hashtags`, `events.host_clan_name`; the host clan is stored in the existing `events.clan_id`): creation is server-gated — `createEvent` requires the creator to be the clan's Leader (role checked via membership) of a clan with at least 10 members (counted server-side), with clear errors for non-Leaders ("you are Recruit…") and small clans ("needs at least 10 members — it has N"). `getMyEventHosting` returns each of the player's clans with member count, role, eligibility and the reason, driving both the Events create area and the Clans-page `CommunityEventsPromo` card that replaced the Clan War section in ClansView. Creating an event increments the clan's `eventsHeld`.
- Discovery: `listEvents` accepts search / hashtag / type / platform / visibility filters. Search by name matches title, type, description, host and host clan; a search starting with `#` (or the hashtag filter) matches hashtags only. Hashtags are normalised (lowercase, no #) and every event auto-carries type tags (Car Meet → #carmeet/#meetup/#cars, Race → #race, Time Trial → #timetrial, Screenshot Contest → #screenshot, Clan Event → #clan, etc.) plus the host's own tags. The Events page is a discovery page: search box, hashtag chips derived from real events with counts, type/platform/Public-Private filters, result count, and cards showing Public/Private badge, date, platform, max players, spots left, host + host clan, and clickable hashtag chips. Private events are filtered out of `listEvents` for everyone except the host clan's members and the host, and `rsvpEvent` re-checks privacy server-side.
- Private-event invites (follow-up, migration 0031: `events.invite_code`): creating a Private event generates a unique 6-character invite code (unambiguous alphabet). The code is returned by `listEvents` only to the host and host-clan members, whose event card shows an invite panel with the code and a Copy invite button. Anyone with the code can view and join that one event: `getEventByInviteCode` returns the event by code, `rsvpEvent` accepts an optional `inviteCode` that admits a non-clan player, and invite links work as `#event=CODE` (parsed on load like the existing `#ref=` referral links, stored on-device, auto-looked-up when the Events page opens; the Events page also has a manual "Joining a private event?" code box). Verified in the sandbox: Leader gate and 10-member gate error correctly; an outsider's list hides the private event; #carmeet search finds car meets without leaking the private one; RSVP without a code is rejected and with the code succeeds.

## Starter Guides library (edit, Nov 19 2026)
- `seedExamples` now also seeds nine starter guides (author "ViceSunshine Guides", idempotent — only while no such guides exist): Beginner first-day guide, Money (honest launch economy + scam warnings), Cars and Weapons (built on the Database's confirmed/reported/unverified/returning statuses — no invented rankings or stats), Missions (spoiler-safe walkthrough approach), Map & Locations (trailer-named regions, official map not released), Tips & Tricks (12 launch tips, including the new event hashtag/invite flows), Settings (PS5/Xbox checklist framed as what to look for), and Fixes (launch-day troubleshooting). Bodies are step-formatted plain text with the official Rockstar URL (supplied verbatim in earlier briefs) where relevant. Guide categories were extended client-side with Missions, Map & Locations and Tips & Tricks, and every guide card now shows its category's own original ViceSunshine illustration (GUIDE_ART map over existing owned assets — no new or external images).

## Database is automatic — no user entries or edits (edit, Nov 19 2026)
- At the owner's direction, users can no longer add or edit Database entries: the `addDbEntry` action is deleted (verified: calling it returns "action is not defined") and the client's Suggest-an-entry form, its state and mutation are removed, replaced by a "Maintained automatically — no user entries" card explaining that the ViceSunshine team maintains the Database from official Rockstar sources and errors can be raised via Ask Vice → support. Community 1–5 ratings remain (an opinion score shown as "Community rating", not an entry edit).
- `seedExamples`' Database section is now a true sync against the team-maintained catalog in code (the two Confirmed protagonists + the weapons/vehicles/supporting-characters catalog): it inserts missing entries (`dbAdded`), updates stored entries whose category/description/status/source/type/manufacturer/featured differ from the catalog (`dbUpdated` — this is what makes status badges automatic from official data), and deletes stored entries that are not in the catalog together with their ratings (`dbRemoved`) — non-catalog rows can only be old user suggestions. The sync runs as the post-submit action, so production is cleaned and kept current on every build; Ask Vice's rules gained the matching Database fact.

## Automatic / self-monitoring systems + automatic moderation (edit, Nov 19 2026)
- Automation cadence (artifact-action schedules installed by this build, on top of the existing daily agent jobs for news/feed/artwork): `refreshNews` every 1h (news + patch tracking), `refreshFeedMedia` every 3h (trending media), `automationSweep` every 1h (moderation re-check), `seedExamples` daily (runs the automatic Database sync). "Every second" tracking is not possible or honest for source-bound data — sources are checked on these schedules and the app says so. `getAutomationStatus` reads the meta timestamps each refresh already writes (`last_refreshed_at`, `last_feed_media_refresh_at`, `last_tiktok_clips_refresh_at`, plus new `last_db_sync_at` written by the Database sync and `last_automation_sweep_at` written by the sweep) and powers a live status line on News ("Automatically updated — last checked …") and the full status card in the Owner Dashboard.
- Automatic moderation: `screenText` / `assertClean` / `screenMediaUrl` (server helpers) screen ALL public user content BEFORE it is stored — Feed posts (title/body/media URL), comments, quote-repost comments, clan chat, guides, LFG posts, events, clan names/tags/mottos, profiles (gamertag/display name/bio), bug reports and Easter egg reports. Categories: sexual content involving minors (illegal, always blocked), explicit sexual content / nudity, leaked-pirated-illegal download offers, scams (generators, password/code/card requests), real-world illegal trade, severe hate slurs, personal contact details in public posts, and direct file-download media links (videos are shared via YouTube/TikTok embeds only; users can also upload images/clips to the Feed via `uploadFeedMedia` — JPEG/PNG uploads are vision-screened by the moderation model inside that action BEFORE storage (nudity/sexual content, minors, graphic real-world violence, illegal trade, scam/leak adverts rejected and never stored); WebP/GIF/video uploads cannot be read by the checker and rely on text screening, reports, the sweep and the owner queue, which the copy states honestly). Blocked posts never reach the database; the error names the rule and offers a human review via support. The hourly `automationSweep` re-screens the newest 300 Feed posts / comments / clan messages: failing posts are flagged, failing comments and clan messages are removed; automatic (team) posts are skipped. Reports: the existing report system now has teeth — at 3 reports a Feed post is flagged AND `listFeed` hides flagged posts from everyone except their author; the owner clears or removes them from the Owner Dashboard moderation queue.

## Owner access & Owner Dashboard (edit, Nov 19 2026)
- The Owner is the first / creator account — the earliest profile row in `profiles`. No separate login, website or secret: the owner uses their normal account, `getProfile` returns `isOwner` for that account only, and every owner action (`getOwnerDashboard`, `ownerRemoveFeedPost`, `ownerClearFeedFlag`, `ownerRemoveBug`, `ownerSetSupportStatus`) re-checks server-side via `assertOwner` and throws for anyone else. UI entry points render only when `isOwner`: an "Owner Dashboard" item (with OWNER label) in the bottom-left account popover, an OWNER pill beside the owner's name on the account trigger, and the `owner` tab view itself, which shows a restriction note to non-owners.
- Dashboard contents (all real counts from the database): total players, new players this week + the 12 newest, Premium members, clans, Feed posts, events; Premium & revenue — honest state: payments are NOT connected (Stripe test mode), so revenue taken is CA$0.00 and the card says exactly that rather than estimating sales; automatic-systems status (last run of each refresh/sweep + counts + currently flagged + open support); moderation queue (flagged Feed posts with report counts/reasons and Remove / Clear-flag controls, flagged bugs with Remove); recent reports; support requests with Mark resolved / Reopen. Verified negatively in the sandbox: a non-owner key gets the owner-only error; the positive view can only be exercised from the owner's own device key.

## Premium avatar effects are animated scenes, not glow rings (edit, Nov 19 2026)
- The four Premium effects were rebuilt as layered SVG scenes over the avatar (`AvatarFxScene` in the client, `.fxs-*` keyframes in theme.css; the stored effect values are unchanged, so existing equips keep working): `fire` = Flame Flyby (flame shapes streak left→right across the top, smoke puffs drift right→left), `neon-surge` = Hamster Wheel (a hamster with running legs patrols and bobs on top of the avatar while a dashed neon ring spins beneath it, second ring counter-rotating), `lightning` = Storm Strikes (three bolts flash down at staggered intervals from drifting storm clouds), `gold-aura` = Top Greeter (a little character stands on top of the avatar waving, gold sparkles rising). The heavy box-shadow glow animations for these four were retired to thin quiet rings; scenes are aria-hidden, pointer-transparent, and freeze under prefers-reduced-motion. Catalog item names/descriptions, the Premium page and Ask Vice were renamed to match (Flame Flyby, Hamster Wheel, Storm Strikes, Top Greeter).

## Scope note + automatic glitch / tips coverage (edit, Nov 19 2026)
- Scope (owner, final word): ViceSunshine is a GTA VI site. It does NOT expand into a general all-GTA site — an expansion to GTA V / IV / San Andreas / Vice City / III was started and then explicitly cancelled by the owner before any of it was built (no game column, no classic catalog; the empty migration file was deleted unused). GTA V appears only where it helps explain GTA VI: "Returning from GTA V" Database entries and labels, comparisons in guides, and GTA Online context where relevant.
- Glitches / tips / tricks now flow in automatically for GTA VI: `refreshNews` added GTA VI glitch/bug-discovery and tips/tricks searches (hourly schedule), and `refreshFeedMedia` added a "GTA VI glitch bug discovered" Gameplay search (posts labelled as community reports with the source linked, pointing readers to the Bug Tracker for confirmed follow-up) and a "GTA VI tips and tricks" Tutorial search (3-hourly schedule). The existing title filters still block leak/download/piracy results, and nothing is invented when a search finds nothing usable.

## Events rule made explicit + Leader event control; LFG clearly separate (edit, Nov 19 2026)
- Owner restated the split, now enforced and labelled end-to-end: Community Events can ONLY be created by a player who is in a clan AND is that clan's Leader AND the clan has at least 10 members total (server-gated in `createEvent`, unchanged). NEW: the hosting clan's Leader now actually controls the event afterwards — `eventItem.canManage` (Leader of host clan; legacy clan-less rows fall back to host gamertag) drives a "Leader controls" panel on the event card, and two new server actions enforce the same check (`isEventManager` helper): `updateEvent` (edit everything, including switching Public ↔ Private — going Private mints a fresh invite code so old codes die, going Public clears it) and `deleteEvent` (removes the event and all its RSVPs, logged in clan activity). The 10-member minimum is creation-only: a Leader keeps control of an existing event if the clan later dips below 10. `setEventResults` previously had NO auth (anyone could post results) — it now requires the managing Leader; `hostConfirmEvent` likewise accepts the hosting clan's Leader (not just the host gamertag). Managing Leaders also receive their own event's lobby details from `listEvents` / `getEventByInviteCode` so they can edit them (still never sent to anyone else except Going RSVPs).
- Find a Group (LFG) is labelled as the opposite, everywhere it matters: nav renamed "Find a Group", an explicit "No clan needed — open to everyone" panel (no clan, no Leader, no 10-member minimum; a quick, small, instant group), and the Events page opens with a two-panel "Community Events — clan-hosted" vs "Find a Group — open to everyone" explainer with cross-links both ways. Server behaviour for LFG was already open (`createLfg` / `joinLfg` have no clan checks) and is unchanged; `createLfg`'s description and Ask Vice's knowledge now state the contrast explicitly.

## Find a Group redesigned — clean and spacious (edit, Nov 19 2026)
- Owner rejected the old Find a Group layout (screenshot): platform chips, activity chips, the mic toggle and the post button all ran together in one unlabelled wrap, the "No clan needed" explainer was a big text block, and each card crammed author/presence/when/language/skill/mic into one wrapping line. `LfgView` was restructured (client only, no server changes): (1) the explainer is now one slim line ("No clan needed — open to everyone" + a Community Events text link); (2) one big obvious primary CTA "+ Post a quick group" (54px tall) with a "takes 10 seconds" helper sits directly under the header and toggles the form, which itself got a heading, roomier padding and a full-width-on-mobile submit; (3) filters live in their own card as three clearly labelled groups — Platform / Activity / Options — with larger 42px chips, plus a "Clear filters" button that only appears when a filter is active, and a result-count line ("N open groups"); (4) group cards are now a single spacious column (p-5/6): pills row (activity, platform, Priority, "Your listing", Full/closed), large title, its own author + presence row, details as a labelled 2/4-column definition grid (When / Language / Skill / Mic — nothing shares a line), author account links on their own row, then a separated footer with a spots-filled progress bar and a big 48px Join group / Close listing button, and a one-line Discord note. Empty state points back to the top CTA.

Premium page, Settings plan card, Customise intro and Ask Vice copy updated to the kept sets. Clan Awards (`AWARD_ITEMS`) are a separate earned system, not a Customise catalogue section, and are unchanged. Client design maps for previously equippable values (old banner designs, calling cards) were left intact so profiles/clans already wearing a removed item still render.

## Premium avatar effects are PHOTOREALISTIC now — cartoon scenes removed (edit, Nov 19 2026 — supersedes the SVG scenes note above)
- Owner rejected the animated SVG scenes (hamster, greeter, drawn flames) as childish/cartoon and demanded REAL effects. The four Premium effects are now photorealistic photographic overlays: generated photographs of a real ring of fire, real lightning arcs, real smoke wisps and real golden embers/sparks on a pure black background (build-time assets `client/src/assets/fx-fire.webp`, `fx-lightning.webp`, `fx-smoke.webp`, `fx-embers.webp`, generated with the media tool for this purpose), rendered by `AvatarFxScene` as an `<img>` over the avatar with `mix-blend-mode: screen` (black disappears, only the real element rings the avatar) plus quiet realistic motion in theme.css (`.avatar-fx-photo`, `vv-fxp-*`: irregular flame flicker, sharp lightning double-flash, 48–64s smoke swirl, ember shimmer; still under prefers-reduced-motion). The wrap's old glow animations/box-shadows are switched off for photo effects. Stored values unchanged (`fire`, `lightning`, `neon-surge`, `gold-aura`, `shadow`), so existing equips keep working; display names are now Real Fire, Real Lightning, Real Smoke (value `neon-surge`; `shadow` drops render the same real smoke, darker/slower) and Ember Sparks (value `gold-aura`). `ice` has no photo and keeps its frost ring. Catalogue names/descriptions (incl. monthly-drop effect items reusing these values), the Premium page list and Ask Vice copy were renamed to match. The hamster / greeter / flyby scenes are deleted from code and CSS entirely.

- Owner's final direction: Avatar Frames are removed completely and Name Colours are removed completely; Avatar Effects STAY (the earlier "remove avatar effects" ask was corrected twice — effects, including the photorealistic Premium Real Fire / Real Lightning / Real Smoke, are untouched).
- Server catalogue (`COSMETIC_ITEMS`): all 8 `frame` items and all 16 `nameColor` items (personal + clan) deleted; the `frame` / `nameColor` kinds are gone from the item type, and the equip/unequip handlers no longer write `avatar_frame` / `name_color`. Customise now has 10 sections (Avatars, Avatar Effects, Profile Banners, Badges, Calling Cards, Themes, Clan Emblems, Clan Colours, Clan Banners, Clan Calling Cards), each still 5 free + 3 Premium.
- Names are white only: every server response now returns `nameColor: ""` / `authorNameColor: ""` / `avatarFrame: ""` / `authorFrame: ""` regardless of any legacy stored value, and the client no longer applies any name-colour style anywhere — profile names, clan names, clan roster names, Feed/clan-post authors, friends and the player directory all render in the default white text colour. Legacy `profiles.name_color` / `profiles.avatar_frame` / `clans.name_color` columns remain in the schema but are never written or read for display anymore.
- Client: `CosmeticAvatar` no longer takes a `frame` prop (plain thin border only; the effect wrapper/FX_PHOTO system is unchanged), no `frame={...}` props remain, the Avatar Frames / Name Colours / Clan Name Colours sections, KIND_LABEL entries, CosmeticVisual and equipped-item branches for those kinds are deleted, and the dead `.frame-anim` CSS was removed. All user-facing copy (Customise, Premium page, profile Customise tab, Ask Vice rules, action descriptions) no longer mentions frames or name colours.

## Avatar Effects split — free glow frames, Premium realistic layered (edit, Nov 19 2026)
- Owner's direction: the 5 free avatar effects must have NO real effects — each is just a glowing frame (CSS glow ring) around the avatar. The 3 Premium effects must be animated, realistic and impressive (the previous single flat photo overlay looked fake).
- Free effects (Ember Glow / Frost Ring / Shadow Veil / Neon Pulse / Sunset Haze): `FX_PHOTO` no longer maps their values, so no photograph renders for them at all — only the `.fx-*` CSS glow-ring animations in theme.css. Catalogue descriptions now say "just a soft glow ring, nothing more".
- Premium effects (Real Fire / Real Lightning / Real Smoke): `AvatarFxScene` now renders TWO layers of the same real photograph — a blurred ambient layer underneath (the light the element throws off, on its own slower cycle) and the sharp main layer on top — giving genuine depth instead of one flat picture. Motion rebuilt for realism: fire = fast irregular 7-step flicker with rotation/saturation shifts + slow ambient breathing; lightning = long quiet stretches then a sharp double flash with an ambient light burst; smoke = two layers counter-rotating at 52s/78s with scale/opacity breathing. Reduced-motion still freezes all of it.

## Integrated avatar effects, custom avatar upload, Discord-style status (edit, Nov 19 2026)
- Avatar Effects redesigned AGAIN at the owner's direction: the photorealistic photo overlays (Real Fire / Lightning / Smoke) read as a SEPARATE thing floating around the circle, so they are gone — the fx-fire/fx-lightning/fx-smoke photo assets are no longer rendered and all photo-overlay CSS is deleted. Effects are now INTEGRATED accessories drawn as original inline SVG in the same 100×100 box as the avatar circle, bases overlapping / growing out of the circle, with the circle's rim taking the effect colour — effect + rim + avatar are one piece. The 8 catalogue value keys are unchanged (equipped effects survive), only names/descriptions/rendering changed. Free 5: Vice Crown (gold-aura), Frost Horns (ice), Bat Mask (shadow — the owner's own example), Neon Halo (neon-pulse, resting ON the circle), Sunset Cap (sunset-haze). Premium 3 (animated): Inferno Mohawk (fire — flame tongues rooted in the circle's top arc, flickering in place), Storm Helmet (lightning — metal dome following the circle's arc, lightning crest, sparking rivets), Dragon Horns (neon-surge — horns rooted into the circle, gold bands, centre gem). Section counts stay exactly 5 free + 3 Premium.
- Custom avatar pictures (migration 0032, profiles.custom_avatar_blob_key): new `uploadCustomAvatar` action — JPG/PNG only (the formats the moderation model can read, so no upload can skip the check), ≤4 MB, profile required. The image is screened by `ctx.inference` vision moderation (shared `assertImageAllowed` helper, also now used by uploadFeedMedia) BEFORE the blob is stored: nudity/sexual content, anything sexualising a minor, graphic gore, real-world illegal trade and scam/leak/piracy adverts are rejected and never stored. On success the blob key is stored on the profile and the picture takes precedence over the catalogue avatar everywhere avatars resolve (getProfile + friends, listPlayers, clan roster, LFG, Feed — via profileAvatarUrl / pickAvatar + customAvatarUrls helpers). Equipping a catalogue avatar clears the custom picture; `removeCustomAvatar` clears it directly. UI: "Your avatar picture" block at the top of the Profile edit form with Upload / Replace / Remove, the moderation rule stated up front, and errors shown next to the control.
- Discord-style presence (migration 0032, profiles.custom_status): internal presence values unchanged (Online / Away / Do Not Disturb / Ghost / Offline) but displayed Discord's way — Away = "Idle" (yellow crescent-moon icon), Ghost = "Invisible" (grey hollow ring; still Offline to everyone else), Do Not Disturb = red circle with white minus, Online = green dot. New `setOnlineStatus` action sets presence and/or a custom status text (≤80 chars, text-screened by assertClean before storing). Profile header gains Discord's speech-bubble "Add Status" button next to the avatar (shows the custom status when set) opening a "Change Online Status" sheet: title, "Online Status" label, one radio card with Online / Idle / Do Not Disturb / Invisible (filled ViceSunshine-pink radio on the selected row), a separate "Set a custom status" card below that expands to an editor with Save / Clear, and the invisible/inactivity note. Settings' Status picker now offers the same 4 labelled choices and shows the current custom status.
- Slim footer (same edit): the full-width footer block (disclaimer + two legal paragraphs + 9 pill buttons + big "New to ViceSunshine?" CTA, ~450px on every page) is replaced by a slim collapsed bar — the one-line unofficial-fan-community disclaimer (always visible), a "Legal & About" expand toggle, and a quiet "Create Account — free" text link for visitors. Expanding reveals the full legal text verbatim and all 9 legal links (Privacy Policy, Terms, About & Contact, Cookies, Refunds, Community Guidelines, Copyright / DMCA, Contact & Support, Ads Disclosure) as compact text links, plus the New-to-ViceSunshine line. All legal content stays reachable in one tap (and unchanged in Settings / the Legal pages); the big gradient CTA block is gone from the footer (Create Account still lives in the sidebar, phone header and Sign Up page).

## Calling Cards / Banners merge + COD-style redesign (Nov 19, 2026)
- Banner colours REMOVED entirely: the `bannerColor` catalogue (5 free colours + 3 Premium designs) and the clan `banner` styles (5 free + 3 Premium) are deleted from the server catalogue and from Customise. Stored legacy values are no longer surfaced (getProfile/listPlayers return bannerColor "", clan summaries return bannerStyle "").
- MERGED: the Calling Card IS the banner. Profile header renders the equipped card's design (background + scene art + title) as the banner; the clan home header does the same. The separate card strips under the profile/clan headers were removed (one thing, not two). The Founders Clan Award is now a gold "founders" calling card (kind callingCard) instead of a banner style.
- Merged sections keep exactly 5 free + 3 Premium: personal cards Vice Nights / Street Heat / Palm Reaper / Keys Runner / Skull Strip (free, STATIC) + Vice Haze / Hydro Vice / Magic Palms (Premium, ANIMATED); clan cards mirror that (Vice Haze / Dead Palms / Inferno Premium animated).
- COD-style original art: CALLING_CARDS designs now carry a `scene` + `animated` flag; CardSceneArt renders original inline SVG motifs (cannabis-style leaves, skulls, flame tongues, smoke) — inspired by COD weed / Dark Ops cards, never copied art. Animation classes (cc-*) apply only to animated designs; free cards are static. Reduced-motion disables all of it.
- Challenge calling cards (separate from the 5+3 catalogue, like Clan Awards): Dark Vice (reach Level 5), Skull Harvest (10 Feed posts), Vicestronaut (RSVP Going to 5 events) — progress computed server-side from in-app activity only (challengeProgress), shown in Customise with Locked/Unlocked state, and equipCosmetic refuses locked cards server-side. All three are animated once earned.

## Season Challenges, XP levels + auto-generated season calling cards (edit, Nov 19 2026)
- XP = Rank Points (one automatic count, in-app activity only). Rates now exactly the owner's table: mission ticked +10 (was +5), attend event (RSVP Going) +15, Feed post +5 (NEW — createFeedPost now records points), post reaching 10 likes +10, recruit +15, form LFG +10, join LFG +10 (was +5), event win +50/+30/+20, verified egg +25, confirmed bug +20, clan post +2. Daily caps adjusted (mission 100, feedpost 25, lfg 50).
- Player level thresholds rebuilt to the owner's ladder: Level 2 at 200 XP, 3 at 500, 4 at 1,000, 5 at 2,000, then the widening steps [3500, 5500, 8000, 11000, 14500] and growing +4,000/+4,500/… steps beyond (xpThresholdForLevel / playerLevelFromPoints now returns levelStart too; getWallet exposes viceLevelStartPoints). The old sqrt formula (L2 at 50) is gone. Clan rank level already used the same ladder and is unchanged. Level milestone credit payouts (L5/L10/…) unchanged.
- Season Challenges (new Challenges tab + getSeasonChallenges action): seasons are calendar months, Season 1 = Nov 2026 (seasonNumberOfKey / seasonKeyForNumber). Seven web-based-only challenges per season, progress counted server-side from the player's own rows in that season's month (seasonMetricValues): RSVP-Going events, done mission ticks (updatedAt), original Feed posts, distinct LFG teams formed/joined, likes received on own posts (excluding self-likes), members recruited into the player's clan (excluding self), and lifetime XP level. Targets scale every season: events 5·s, feed posts 5·s, likes 25·s, missions 3+2(s−1), LFG 3+2(s−1), recruits 2+2(s−1), level target 3/5/7/10/12/15/18/20 then +3/season. Past seasons remain viewable with their historical progress.
- Unlocks are automatic: grantSeasonUnlocks inserts the owned_items row when a completed challenge's board (or Customise) is read — no claim step. equipCosmetic re-checks season challenge progress server-side and refuses locked season cards with the challenge + progress named. Season cards also appear in Customise (current season set with progress, plus owned past-season cards) and stay equippable forever once unlocked.
- Auto-generated season calling cards: definitions are generated deterministically from the season number (theme list Vice Nights → Neon Storm → Haze Empire → …, per-metric card names/scenes), item ids `card-season-<n>-<metric>`, design values `s<n>-<metric>`. The ARTWORK is genuinely generated: generateSeasonCardArtFor paints an original wide banner per card via ctx.tool.generate_media with a brief that escalates by design tier (tier = season number: T1 bold flat neon; T2 + smoke/leaves/depth; T3 + flames/glowing eyes/contrast; T4 + shimmer/cinematic light; T5+ maximum detail, "strictly richer than tier N−1"), stored as owned blobs under meta `season-card-art:<value>`. Generation is driven automatically by the existing artwork schedule (seedArtwork paints one missing current-season card per run) and by the refreshSeasonCards action (up to 2 per call); a new season therefore generates its own set with no manual step. Client rendering mirrors the escalation even before art lands: seasonCardDesignOf derives palette/scene from the key, adds light layers at tiers 2/3/5, animation from Season 2, and the shimmer sweep from Tier 4; cardDesignOf is now the single lookup for profile/clan banners so season cards render everywhere catalogue cards do. Style kept: the loved COD-style dark cards with skulls, neon-green leaves, fire and smoke — original ViceSunshine art only.

## Sign-up Step 1 back to email-only + simplified egg detail tab (edit, Nov 19 2026)
- Owner reversed the Discord sign-in / 2FA direction: Create Account Step 1 is email sign-up ONLY again (username + email + platform + "Sign up with email"). The half-wired "Continue with Discord" OAuth flow and the email-code 2FA phase were removed from `SignUpView` (the `createAccount` mutation is restored: saveProfile creates the account with its 500 starting credits + referral, then saveSettings stores the contact email, then Step 2). No Discord button, no OAuth return handling, no code entry anywhere on Step 1. The Discord OAuth + email-code machinery was then removed entirely (audit correction): `completeDiscordSignUp` / `getDiscordSignInStatus` / `setDiscordOAuthConfig` / `discordOAuthConfig` / `discordAuthorizeUrl`, `requestEmailCode` / `verifyEmailCodeAndSignUp` and the email-code helpers are deleted from actions.ts, the Settings `DiscordOAuthSettings` panel is deleted, the `email_verification_codes` table is removed from schema.ts, and migration 0039 drops the table (0038 itself is untouched, as applied migrations are never edited).
- Step 2 "One more step / Link & join" unchanged and confirmed as the owner asked: linked accounts (PSN / Xbox / Discord username) side by side with the Discord server card on desktop, card stacked directly after the fields on phone, Join opens https://discord.gg/NDH9XxyVkW (via the stored invite), and "Save & finish" sits all the way at the bottom after the Discord card.
- Egg detail tab simplified (owner's direction): ONE best-matching video about that exact egg playing inline (first Video from the automatically gathered egg_media), OR one picture (the egg's own Ideas illustration) when no video exists — the old "Videos about this egg" list, "Guides & coverage" list, "From ViceSunshine" related posts/guides and "More eggs to try" sections are gone from the tab. New "Is it real?" verdict block: Confirmed → "Real — confirmed", Reported → "Unverified — reported, not yet confirmed", Rumor → "Rumor — not confirmed", each with a short plain explanation, plus ONE check link — labelled official Rockstar when the egg's source URL is rockstargames.com, otherwise the original source report (falling back to the first gathered coverage). Automatic refresh on open (stale > 6h) is unchanged. Ideas page untouched; the Easter Eggs exploring list stays text-only (no pictures on egg entries).

## First idea whole-card tap + egg Recommendations + no-repeat content pictures (edit, Nov 19 2026)
- Ideas: ONLY the first card under "Trending ideas to try" (Trending #1, idx 0) is whole-card tappable — the article itself carries role=button/tabIndex/onClick/onKeyDown (Enter/Space) calling the existing openEgg(id), which switches to the eggs tab and renders that egg's simplified EggDetailView at the top. Inner picture/title/Try-it controls keep working (stopPropagation so the tap fires once); the Save button is wrapped in a stopPropagation span so saving never navigates. Other idea cards are byte-for-byte as before. Ideas hero and layout unchanged in structure.
- Egg detail Recommendations: at the VERY bottom of EggDetailView, a "More Easter eggs to try" section. Data = detail.moreEggs (server, other eggs) merged with listEggs, deduped, current egg excluded, capped at 12. Cards are SMALL (148px wide, 84px picture, truncated title, StatusBadge only). The row is a snap-x overflow-x-auto flex row: swipe/touch on phone, scroll arrows (desktop header + mobile footer) scrolling 340px smoothly, and pointer-drag scrolling on desktop (a drag suppresses the click so dragging never opens a card). Tapping a card calls openEgg(rec.id) — that egg's own detail tab, scrolled to top.
- No-repeat content pictures (Customise explicitly OUT of scope — avatars/calling cards/emblems/effects/badges untouched): audited every static content image in App.tsx; each content image variable is now used exactly once. New original illustrations generated at build time with media.generate_image into client/src/assets/ (painted Vice neon style, no text/logos): ideas-hero.webp (Ideas hero, was sec-eggs), saved-hero.webp (Saved header, was hero), release-hero.webp (Release Times background, was hero), news-featured.webp (News featured story, was hero), goals-wallet.webp (Goals wallet card, was clan-arena), guide-beginner/money/cars/weapons/missions/map/tips/settings/fixes/help.webp (GUIDE_ART per category, were hero/clan-arena/db-vehicles/db-weapons/sec-missions/sec-map/sec-guides/sec-profile/sec-bugs/sec-news), egg-fallback-map/door/reef/sky/swamp.webp (ideaArtFor fallbacks). ideaArtFor(name, id) picks a fallback from a hash of name+id; IdeasView then de-duplicates the assignments across the rendered grid — the used set is seeded with the Ideas hero, and a card whose picture is already on the page gets the next unused fallback instead — so no two cards on the Ideas page ever show the same picture. EggDetail recommendations de-duplicate art inside the row the same way (never the current egg's picture, never repeated in the row). hero.webp now appears only as the Home hero; sec-eggs.webp only as the Easter Eggs section banner; clan-arena.webp only as the Crews list hero (ClanHome's no-calling-card fallback is now a crew-colour gradient with no picture, not a repeat); sec-events.webp only as the Events banner, and the Community Events promo uses its own dedicated events-promo.webp (a beachfront car meet at dusk, generated for this slot — audit correction: it previously reused shop-racer.webp, which is now referenced nowhere; the Shop itself was removed earlier, so its leftover shop-*.webp files are orphaned assets, not displayed pictures). Database entries keep their per-entry server illustrations (imageUrl) with per-entry monogram tiles as the no-image state; Feed/News editorial media stays per-post source media. Remaining same-file reuse is only dynamic per-item media (post mediaUrl, user avatars), which is the item's own picture, not a shared stock image.

## Egg detail origin-aware Back + sidebar (edit, Nov 19 2026)
- The egg detail tab now remembers WHERE it was opened from (eggOrigin state in the app shell): openEgg(id, origin) is called with "ideas" from IdeasView, "eggs" from EggsView and "saved" from SavedView; recommendation taps inside EggDetailView pass no origin, so the original origin is kept while browsing recommendations. The detail's back button is labelled for the origin — "← Ideas", "← Saved" or "← All Easter eggs" (both the main and the not-found back buttons) — and onBack returns there: go("ideas") / go("saved") for those origins, or clearing the open egg back to the list for the eggs origin. The bottom of the detail tab (Recommendations) and everything else in it are unchanged.
- Sidebar follows the origin, not the render tab: a derived navTab (the origin while an egg detail is open, otherwise the current tab) drives aria-pressed/aria-current highlighting in the desktop sidebar, the mobile drawer and the bottom nav, and openEgg opens the origin's sidebar section (Your Agent for ideas/saved, Explore for eggs). So a detail opened from Ideas keeps Ideas highlighted with Your Agent open; only a detail opened from the Easter Eggs list itself highlights Easter Eggs.

## Ideas hero tap + VS logo + GTA VI-inspired home theme (edit, Nov 19 2026)
- Ideas hero: the "Start with one good idea" hero card at the top of Ideas is now whole-card tappable — role=button, tabIndex, Enter/Space (guarded so keys on inner buttons don't double-fire), cursor-pointer — and pressing it goes DIRECTLY to the Easter Eggs section via in-app navigation (go("eggs"); same tab, no external link, no new browser tab). The idea cards below the hero are unchanged.
- Ideas hero redesign (same edit): the hero now reads as a banner, clearly a different part from the cards below — a 2px sunset gradient frame (hot pink → neon purple → teal, .ideas-hero-frame in theme.css) with a soft neon glow shadow, panoramic art inside (320px tall on phone, 430px on desktop, full content width) with a stronger pink/teal sunset-glow overlay plus a deep navy scrim, the "Trending now" pill with a pink glow, and the title in the big cream condensed all-caps .vice-display style (38px / 56px) overlaid on the art. The paragraph, count line and the three buttons sit on a deep navy strip (.home-navy) under the art inside the same frame. Same picture (ideas-hero.webp), same text, same buttons, same whole-banner tap to Easter Eggs.
- VS logo (original mark, NOT Rockstar's): a new build-time generated asset, client/src/assets/vs-logo.webp — bold capital "VS" letters filled with a hot pink → neon purple gradient on a very dark navy rounded square, flat and clean, no other symbols or text. It is an original ViceSunshine mark: it does not copy the GTA VI "VI" logo, the Grand Theft Auto logo, or the Rockstar "R" logo. Used as the site logo beside the ViceSunshine wordmark in the desktop sidebar brand button and in the mobile header, and shown as the server icon on the Create Profile Discord card with a download link (download="vicesunshine-vs-logo.webp") so the same file can be used as the Discord server icon or a profile avatar. Customise is untouched — the logo was NOT added to any avatar/calling-card/emblem catalogue.
- GTA VI-inspired home theme (style only, inspired by rockstargames.com/VI — no Rockstar art, layout or copy reproduced): new theme.css classes .home-navy (very dark navy #0b0b1e surface), .vice-display (bold condensed all-caps cream #fff6e9 display type), .btn-pale (pale pink pill CTA) and .card-big (24px radius). HomeView's hero is now a big rounded (28px) navy card with the original hero illustration taller on top and the briefing kicker + headline overlaid on the art in cream condensed all-caps, with the briefing text and launch countdown below on navy; the hero quick cards and Home stat cards use the larger radius, and the primary Home CTAs ("Open the Feed", "Browse crews") are pale pink pills. White titles + light grey supporting text under cards follow the same pattern. All Home features, sections and copy are unchanged, and the rest of the site keeps the existing dark neon theme.

## Instagram-style Comments sheet, Feed views + Stories (edit, Nov 19 2026)
- Migration 0040: feed_comments.parent_id (threaded replies, one level deep — a reply to a reply attaches to the top-level comment), feed_comment_reactions (comment likes), feed_views (one row per distinct viewer per post — the only view count ViceSunshine shows; never source-platform numbers, never a guess), stories / story_views / story_likes / story_replies.
- Comments sheet (CommentsSheet, shared by Feed posts and Clips): Discuss/Comments now opens a fixed bottom sheet in Instagram's layout — backdrop, slide-up animation (vv-sheet-up, reduced-motion safe), drag handle (drag down >80px closes), centred "Comments · N" header with an eye line showing the post's view count, close X (plus Escape and backdrop tap), scrollable list (CosmeticAvatar, bold username + body inline, time ago, like count, Reply; heart button right with count; replies hidden behind "View N replies", indented), and the comment input pinned at the bottom with the viewer's avatar, a replying-to chip with Cancel, and a Post button that appears when text is present. The old inline Feed comments block and the old Clips comments panel are deleted. Like/Discuss actions and the Feed layout are otherwise unchanged.
- Views: listFeed/listClips return views = feed_views distinct-viewer count. FeedPostCard records a view via recordFeedViews when the card is ≥35% on screen (once per mount); the active Clip records its view the same way. The count shows on the post (eye + "N views" in the actions row), in the Clips caption meta, and in the comments sheet header.
- Stories (StoriesRow at the very top of the Feed, above the banner): "Your story" circle with a + badge, then one circle per author with stories from the last 24 hours — conic neon ring (pink → purple → teal) while unseen, grey ring once seen, name below. listStories groups by author, filters created_at > now−24h (expired stories are never returned — they disappear), and returns per-story views/likes/likedByMe/mine plus group hasUnseen.
- Story posting (StoryComposer bottom sheet): pick a photo/video from the device (reuses uploadFeedMedia — same size limits, 60s video check and image moderation — so story media is moderated exactly like Feed media), or paste a clip/picture link (YouTube/TikTok embeds play via FeedMediaBlock), add a text overlay (createStory.text, screened) and one sticker label from a fixed text-sticker list (no emojis), see a 9:14 live preview with the sticker/text placed as the viewer shows them, then Post. createStory derives mediaType server-side (image/video/embed/text).
- Story viewer (StoryViewer, full-screen, Instagram behaviour): segmented progress bars (animated fill, 6s photos/text, 15s embeds, native videos advance on ended and show a filled segment), creator avatar/name/time-ago header, close X, tap right 68% = next / left 32% = previous (across stories then across authors), Arrow keys + Escape, swipe left/right to navigate and swipe down to close, sticker + text overlay, views count at the bottom (eye + count), heart Like with count (toggleStoryLike), and a Reply input (replyToStory) for other people's stories. Opening a story calls viewStory — one view per player, the author never counts as their own viewer. For the poster's own story the views line becomes a "Seen by N" button opening insights (getStoryInsights, author-only): viewer list with avatar/gamertag/time, like count, and the replies sent to that story. Feed and Clips layouts are otherwise unchanged, and Customise is untouched by this edit.

## Ideas hero full text + Instagram profile hub replacing Goals (Nov 19, 2026)
- Ideas hero: kept the short, wide calling-card banner (sunset frame, art strip ~74–92px on top), but the hero paragraph now sits in a distinct solid dark navy strip (#0d0d20) UNDER the art — no gradient-over-picture text, NO line-clamp: the full paragraph renders at 11–11.5px / 1.45 line-height so it all fits and stays readable. All three buttons kept (Try an idea now / Map status & known regions / All Easter eggs); the whole banner still navigates straight to Easter Eggs in the same tab, buttons keep their own actions.
- Followers are real data: migration 0041 adds `profile_follows` (follower_key, following_key, created_at). New actions: `getSocialProfile` (any player's IG-style public profile + real Posts/Followers/Following counts + viewer's follow state, respecting profilePrivate for bio/links), `setFollow` (requires BOTH accounts to be real saved profiles — following only works signed in; self-follow rejected), `listFollowList` (followers/following with per-row viewer follow state), `listProfilePosts` (a player's Feed posts / clips for the grid, flagged/blocked rules match the Feed), `getFeedPostsByIds` (the profile Saved grid, fed by the on-device viceverse-saved-feed-v1 ids).
- Profile redesign (Instagram layout, dark neon kept): StoriesRow at the very top of the profile (Your story + posts a story from the profile), calling-card banner, large avatar, display name + @username, bio, tappable Posts / Followers / Following counts (Posts jumps to the grid; Followers/Following open the real lists in a bottom sheet), Follow / Following / Follow back states (Follow back when they follow you and you don't follow them), and a 3-column posts grid — tap a tile to open the post in a sheet with its player, like, comments (Instagram CommentsSheet) and views. All prior ViceSunshine content kept: Level/Rank + Rank Points, reputation, Vice Credits wallet, crew, badges, calling card, Missions Done, Events Attended, linked PSN/Xbox/Discord, Friends/crewmates (now tappable through to their profiles), private note, Activity and Customise tabs, Edit Profile form, player directory (now with View profile buttons). Other players' profiles open in the same view (directory / friends / follow lists) with their own grid + stats.
- Profile = media hub: grid tabs Posts / Videos-Clips / Saved / Ideas under the header. Saved tab shows saved Feed posts (with a Saved videos only filter) plus saved ideas; Ideas tab shows saved Easter egg ideas as art cards that open each egg's detail tab. Other profiles show Posts / Videos-Clips tabs only.
- Goals replaced: Your Agent nav (sidebar + bottom nav) now lists Profile where Goals was; Community no longer duplicates Profile. `go("goals")` redirects to the profile, and the Home "Your goals" card now opens the profile. GoalsView was removed; mission progress display moved onto the profile as `MissionProgressCard` in the Main tab (same listMissions data + the on-device tracked list with done toggles) — no mission tracking data was deleted or changed.

## X-style phone drawer, sidebar top/bottom tidy, mixed X + Instagram profile (edit, Nov 19 2026)
- Phone navigation (owner's X screenshots — behaviour/layout only, no X logo or branding): on phone there is NO sidebar and no "All sections" panel anymore. The mobile top bar's left control is now the round profile avatar ball (the player's real avatar, or the Instagram-style grey silhouette default); tapping it slides a drawer in from the left (`.mobile-drawer` in theme.css, backdrop tap / close X / Escape close it, `drawerOpen` state in the App shell; `go()` and `openSettings()` close it). Drawer content, top to bottom: avatar (54px) + close X, display name (+ OWNER badge), @username (gamertag, spaces stripped; "@guest" with no profile), real Following / Followers counts from `getSocialProfile` (same query key as the Profile page, 12s refetch — 0 / 0 until real follows exist), then the X-style item list mapped onto real ViceSunshine destinations: Profile → profile tab, Subscription (right label "Premium" / "Premium active") → premium tab, History → updates tab (update/patch history), Calls → messages tab (in-app voice calls were removed earlier; voice lives on the Discord server), Communities → crews (clans) tab, Lists → saved tab (saved lists/library), Spaces → events tab, Creator Studio → customise tab, Settings and privacy → Settings modal (general), Help Center → Settings modal (help). Below a divider, a "Browse ViceSunshine" section carries the same four accordion groups the old panel had (Your agent / Explore / GTA VI Database / Community, shared `openNavSection` state with the desktop sidebar) so no destination was lost, and a plan · Vice Credits line at the bottom. Calm, sourced, and organised." under the VS logo + "ViceSunshine" name is REMOVED — the sidebar top is just logo + name above the Search button. The Create Account button moved from the top of the sidebar to the BOTTOM, directly above the two-line account trigger, and is now a SMALL compact pill (32px, 12.5px text, left-aligned, gradient kept) shown only to visitors; the phone header's full-width Create Account button is unchanged.
- Profile redesign — a MIX of X and Instagram, X aesthetic dominant (owner's direction; original ViceSunshine blend, no X or Instagram logos/branding copied): both your own profile and other players' profiles now open with an X-style top bar (back arrow — to Today on yours, to your profile on theirs — name, real posts count), then a large banner (the equipped calling card at 168–204px via the new `ProfileBanner` component, or the original profile illustration), the big avatar (112px) overlapping the banner's bottom-left with a bg-coloured ring, and action pills on the right (yours: Edit profile + View archive as compact outline pills; theirs: Follow / Follow back / Following + Message). Underneath: bold display name, @username, badges row, bio, a "Joined {Month Year}" line (`JoinedLine`, from the real member-since date), and X-style counts (`XCounts`: bold number + dim label, Following then Followers, tappable to the real follow lists; posts count lives in the top bar — all three counts always shown, starting at 0, live-updating). The Instagram parts stay BELOW: story highlights row, the spacious icon grid tab bar (Posts / Videos / Saved / Reposts / Tagged / Ideas) and grids, then all the ViceSunshine details (Main / Activity / Customise, stats, crew, linked accounts, wallet). The duplicate calling-card strip that used to sit inside the details section / under another player's grid was removed — the banner appears once, at the top. No Note bubble anywhere (unchanged from the previous direction); tapping your own avatar still opens Change Online Status.

## Live Server Status on Home (edit, Nov 19 2026)
- New `getServerStatus` action + `LiveServerStatus` block rendered as the LAST section of HomeView, directly above the shell footer (owner: the section was missing). Small clean card, no invented numbers: "Online" + green pulsing dot (`.status-dot` in theme.css) is shown only when the action itself just answered — a failed poll honestly shows "Can't reach the server — retrying automatically" (15s `refetchInterval` poll). Players: `playersOnline` counts profiles by the app's own presence rule (chosen status not Ghost/Offline AND `lastSeen` within `PRESENCE_STALE_MS` = 5 min — the same rule `effectivePresence` shows other players), next to the real total profile count. Uptime: the module-level `SERVER_PROCESS_STARTED_AT` (when the current server process loaded the actions bundle — a genuine restart resets it; never stored or padded), counted up live on a 1s client tick, re-synced from `serverNow` on every poll. Automatic pipelines: News / Feed / Database / Moderation each read the real meta stamp that pipeline writes when it runs (`last_refreshed_at`, `last_feed_media_refresh_at`, `last_db_sync_at`, `last_automation_sweep_at`) and render "Active · updated {timeAgo}", or an amber dot + "Waiting for first run" until a stamp exists.

## Profile analytics, live Latest Update, Home slideshow, Service Status card, splash (Nov 19, 2026)

- **Profile analytics** (owner's direction, from his Home screenshot): Home's "Your profile" card now opens a Profile analytics view (new `analytics` tab). `getProfileAnalytics` counts everything from stored rows — followers (`profile_follows`), posts (`feed_posts`), and likes / views / comments / reposts received on the player's own posts (`feed_reactions`, `feed_views`, `feed_comments`, `feed_reposts`, other players only, never self-actions). It returns all-time totals, a 14-day cumulative daily series, and last-7-days vs previous-7-days deltas with % change (null % when the previous period is 0 — never an invented percentage). The graph is Recharts: a clean green line (#4ade80) on a black card, faint dotted grid, dot on the current value, total + % change underneath (Wealthsimple graph STYLE only, from the owner's screenshots — no branding copied). Below, the Growth details list states Grew / No change / Went down per metric with real numbers. Polls every 15s.
- **Saves metric**: Feed saves were on-device only, so a new `feed_saves` table (migration 0044) + `setFeedSave` action now records a save server-side alongside the saver's unchanged on-device list (wired into the Feed post card and Clips save toggles; saving your own post is ignored). Saves therefore count from this build onward, and the analytics view says so in a footnote — earlier on-device-only saves are honestly not counted.
- **Latest Update, live** (owner's direction): the Home card polls `listPatches` every 30s and shares the `["server-status"]` 15s poll. Its dot is strictly server-derived: green Live when the server answers and the news/update pipeline stamp is under 3h old, amber Delayed when the stamp is older or missing, red Down when the server cannot be reached. The card shows the real tracked time (`patches.created_at`, now exposed by `listPatches`) and the source's last-checked time. Pressing the card opens a clean update detail view (in Updates): live status, what's new (the patch's real notes), the previous update, and the official Rockstar link (the patch's own source URL, else the official-site verify link).
- **Home slideshow** (owner's direction): directly under the hero cards, the latest 5 real news stories auto-advance every 5s; swipe / arrows / dots move manually and pause auto-play for 15s, with a Pause/Play control; reduced motion disables auto-play. Slides use the existing original per-category section art and each story's real title, source and time; tapping opens the full story at its source URL.
- **Service Status card** (owner's direction): the Home "Saved" card is replaced by a Service Status card driven by the same `getServerStatus` data — server Online/Offline, News / Feed / Database auto-update and Moderation rows with green/amber/red dots from each pipeline's real last-run stamp judged against its own cadence (hourly pipelines 3h, daily/through-the-day pipelines 26h), uptime, and last checked time. Saved remains in Your Agent and Profile; the fuller Live Server Status block (with players online) still sits at the bottom of Home.
- **Splash screen** (owner's direction, X-style): on page load / reload only, a full-screen dark (`--bg`) overlay with the VS logo centred and nothing else, fading out over ~0.6s after ~1s into Home. In-app navigation never re-shows it; reduced motion skips it.

## Vice upgrade — advanced guide + privacy + clean chat (Nov 19, 2026)

- **Ask Vice, broader and guide-first** (owner's direction): `askVice` now always loads the 3 latest news stories and 3 latest public Feed posts (so "today's news / what's trending" always has real material), and keyword-matches Easter Eggs, Bugs and public Crews on top of News / Guides / Database / Missions / public Events / patches. A static HOW TO USE VICESUNSHINE block gives Vice exact step-by-step guides for every tool (create account, link PSN/Xbox/Discord, join/create a crew, post in the Feed, Stories, save, Search, Messages, follow, Events, Find a Group, Database, Missions, Easter Eggs, Customise, Premium, profile analytics, Settings, report/block), and the prompt instructs guide-mode answers: short numbered steps in simple language plus a link to the exact page. Recent chat turns (max 6) ride along as context only. Link validation now accepts the real tab ids (fixing a "crews" vs "clans" mismatch that silently dropped crew links, and adding messages / challenges / analytics) and official-post URLs that actually appear in ViceSunshine's own content (a news story's or Database entry's stored source URL, or the official GTA VI page) — no other URLs can be emitted.
- **Vice privacy rule** (owner's direction, structural + prompted): Vice's context contains ONLY the asking player's own account line and public content. Private events are filtered out before matching; event lobby details and invite codes are never loaded; crews contribute public directory fields only (name/tag/platform/motto); no other user's email, payment info, messages, saved items or account details exist anywhere in the pipeline. The prompt additionally hard-refuses requests for another person's private information, explaining why and offering the player's own account or public content instead.
- **Vice chat UI**: redesigned clean and spacious, Muse-style — Vice answers as unboxed text beside a small VS mark, the player's messages as gradient bubbles on the right, a three-dot typing indicator, auto-scroll, a pill composer with a round send button, suggestion rows only at the start of a fresh chat (news / how-to questions plus support shortcuts), and a persistent "Need a human instead? Contact support" entry to the same human support-request form (auto-opens when Vice flags needsHuman). Vice still only ever answers when asked — nothing is sent or pushed unasked.

## Drawer = ViceSunshine nav only, dropdown icons, Home→Updates first page, cards row, analytics always-on, carousel release slides, sidebar theme, Rockstar ID (Nov 19, 2026)

- **Phone drawer**: the X-style item list (Profile, Subscription, History, Calls, Communities, Lists, Spaces, Creator Studio, Settings and privacy, Help Center) is REMOVED from the phone drawer. The drawer now shows only ViceSunshine's own navigation — the same four accordion groups as the desktop sidebar (Your Agent / Explore / GTA VI Database / Community) — plus the account block at the bottom (avatar, name + OWNER badge, @handle · Following · Followers, plan · credits), which opens Settings. Slide-in, close X, backdrop and Escape behaviour unchanged.
- **Sidebar icons**: one shared `NavIcon` component (original clean outline glyphs, stroke 1.8) renders on dropdown items ONLY — desktop sidebar and phone drawer alike. No icons in the header (VS logo + name only) and none on top-level items outside the dropdowns.

- **Analytics always-on (Spend-insights style)**: `getProfileAnalytics` now also returns `months` (per-month event counts for the last three UTC months) and `recent` (the 12 latest individual growth events with generic labels — never another player's identity), and returns zero-filled days/months/compare instead of empty arrays when there is no profile. The view always shows the graph: Trends / Categories tabs, big monthly total with "in {Month Year}" and a "3 month avg." on the right, a flat baseline line at 0 (Y domain forced to at least 1), a month dropdown, and an Activity list with a "No activity yet" empty state. No profile = a slim banner above the graph, never a replacement for it.

Switching is a calm crossfade only (`.carousel-slide`, 0.7s opacity) — no slide/bounce/flash; auto-advance 5.5s with the usual pause rules, arrows/dots/swipe, reduced motion disables auto-play.
- **Sidebar + theme colours**: the sidebar active state is ONE solid colour — hot pink #FF2E88 (soft pink fill, pink border, pink left bar) in theme.css `.sidebar-link[aria-pressed="true"]`; the Database "Selected" labels are pink too. The Create Account button at the sidebar bottom is full-width but slim (34px) with the VS logo gradient (hot pink #FF2E88 → neon purple #9D4EDD) and white text (the phone header button uses the same two-stop gradient). Off-theme Instagram blue is gone: `.btn-blue` is now the VS gradient and `.unread-dot` is hot pink; `--accent2` is the owner's teal #00E5CC. Main content widened a little (shell 1280→1400px; page column 760→860px, profile/analytics 880→960px, messages 1080→1160px), sidebar width unchanged.
- **Splash**: just the VS letters as hot pink → purple gradient text directly on the dark background — no box or tile behind them.
- **Rockstar Social Club ID** (migration 0045: `profiles.rockstar_id`, `profiles.show_rockstar` default 0): an OPTIONAL typed ID like PSN/Xbox (no Rockstar sign-in exists for a fan site). The Public/Private choice sits right next to the field in Create Account step 2, Edit profile and Settings (plus a Permissions toggle in Settings), with PRIVATE as the safe default. Visibility is enforced server-side in `visibleLinks`: public = everyone; private = only the player and their crew (crew roster passes sameCrew for clan members; the public profile passes isSelf/sameCrew; directory/LFG/Feed stay public-only). Display: the shared `PlayerLinks` component shows the Rockstar ID first on profiles, the player directory, crew rosters and LFG posts, with a one-tap Copy button and an "Add on Rockstar" quick action that copies the ID and tells the player to paste it into Social Club's friend search — the fastest real add flow, since no Rockstar API exists for fan sites. Vice's website guide covers the field too.
