## Community Feed — no category chooser, spacious stories, reference-style balls + album picker (edit, Nov 20 2026)
- **No category chooser**: the Feed's category pill row (All / Clips / Funny / Memes / Streamer Clips / Tutorials / Gameplay / Car Meet / News / Discussion) is GONE (FEED_TABS deleted); the Feed always queries kind "All". The only remaining filter row is Show (All posts / Automatic only / Community only) + Sort (Top / Latest). The "Suggested for you" row no longer renders in the Feed (still on other people's profiles) — fewer recommendations, more space.
- **Category moved to posting**: in the Create creator, the category radiogroup now sits at the BOTTOM, directly above Publish, headed "Which category do you want to post to?" (Clips / Story destinations show a "Posting to:" line instead).
- **Stories row, reference style (owner's two pictures)**: Your story + at most 5 other people (server returns up to 24; client slices 5), wide 24px gaps, 72px balls. Balls with a story carry a solid purple ring (#9333ea); display name in white under the ball, @handle in grey under that (listStories people now also return `gamertag` for the handle). Under your own ball: "Your story" + your @handle, plus a small Watch link when you have a live story.
- **Fast-add pill**: anyone who is not your friend gets a purple pill (white + and person icon, 54×30) overlapping the bottom of their ball — one tap calls setFollow(follow:true) and flips to an "Added ✓" pill. Tapping the ball itself watches their story (or opens their profile when they have none). Inside the story viewer, the header name/avatar and a purple "Profile" pill both visit that person's profile (StoryViewer gained an optional onOpenProfile; other callers unchanged).
- **Your ball = album picker**: tapping your own ball opens the "Add to story" sheet in the reference layout — X + "Add to story" title, Trending / Music / Collage boxes (each sets that sticker and opens the picker), a Recents row with a Camera tile (capture input) and an album tile. A web page cannot enumerate the phone's album: the tiles open the device's own photo picker, which asks for album access first and shows the real Recents grid on-device; nothing is read until a file is picked. A picked file uploads via uploadFeedMedia and lands in StoryComposer pre-filled (new initialMediaUrl/initialMediaKind/initialSticker props). SUPERSEDED Nov 21 2026 by the StoryAddFlow below — the Trending / Music / Collage boxes, the Select affordance and StoryComposer are gone.

## Add to story flow + profile avatar + badge (edit, Nov 21 2026)
- Owner's references: two iPhone screenshots of Instagram (Add to story picker: Recents tight grid + camera tile; story preview with a Share sheet) plus a photo of his own ViceSunshine profile showing a green ball at the avatar's bottom right. Direction: pressing your own profile picture opens the Add to story picker — all your album plus a camera option to take a real photo or video; only 1 item can be picked for a story; the pick opens the preview zoomed to the story-frame position, smooth quality; the share sheet has only one feature, Your story; press Share and it shows up on the ball. And on the profile, instead of the green ball at the bottom right of the avatar, put a + there.
- **New `StoryAddFlow` component** (replaces `AddToStoryPicker` and `StoryComposer`, both deleted, along with the now-unused `STORY_STICKERS` list): step 1 "pick" — black full-screen sheet, X + "Add to story", a "Recents ⌄" row with a "Pick 1 for your story" hint and NO Select/multi-pick control; one tight grid (`grid-cols-3 gap-[2px]`, square tiles): Camera tile first (`capture="environment"` file input — real photo/video), Phone album tile second (device file input; the phone asks for album access and shows its real Recents — a web page still cannot enumerate the phone album itself), then the player's ViceSunshine album: their own posts (`listProfilePosts` mode posts), clips (mode clips) and story archive (`listStoryArchive`), image/video only, deduped by URL, newest first; video tiles carry a play badge. Videos over 60 seconds picked from the device are rejected before upload; device picks upload via `uploadFeedMedia` and advance automatically. Step 2 "preview" — the picked media fills a 9:16 story frame (`object-cover`), opening at scale 1.16 and settling smoothly to scale 1 over 900ms (the requested zoom, smooth quality); a Share button opens step 3. Step 3 "share sheet" — bottom sheet titled Share with exactly ONE row: own avatar + "Your story" + "Visible for 24 hours" + a filled radio; the big Share button calls `createStory` (media only, no text/sticker) and on success closes the flow and invalidates the stories queries, so the story shows up on the "Your story" ball.
- **Entry points**: the Feed Stories row's own ball now opens StoryAddFlow (was AddToStoryPicker → StoryComposer); the Profile's own avatar tap now opens StoryAddFlow over the profile (was the FeedCreator opened on STORY — FeedCreator itself is unchanged and still serves the + create button and the Profile Upload hand-off). Profile's onPosted also invalidates `story-archive` / `story-album-archive` so highlights/story history refresh.
- **Profile avatar badge**: on your OWN profile only, the green `PresenceDot` ball at the avatar's bottom right is replaced by a purple (#9333ea) + badge with a bg-coloured ring, matching the Feed ball's +; presence is untouched elsewhere (other players' profiles keep their dot, and your status still shows/changes on the status line under your name).

## Test-account reset — fresh start for the owner (edit, Nov 20 2026)
- Owner: every account he tried creating locked him out (no working log in / forgot password path he could use), so he asked for all of them deleted to start fresh. New one-time action `resetTestAccounts` (no request fields, meta-flag guarded `test-accounts-reset-2026-11-20`, later calls no-op): deletes every profile whose playerKey does NOT start with "bot-" (pre-launch, those are only the owner's test sign-ups) plus their tied rows across ~55 tables (posts + engagement on their posts, stories + views/likes/replies on their stories, highlights + items, follows both ways, email codes for their addresses, payment methods, DMs, points, wallet transactions by actor, etc.), in one batch, profiles last. The 200 seeded bot community members and their crews/posts/follows are untouched. Invoked once against production via post_submit_action on this edit's submit.

## Reset password — six boxes, then the new-password step (edit, Nov 20 2026)
- Owner's reference (his screenshot of the old page): the reset code was one wide input with the new-password fields on the same form. Now: Forgot password is three steps in the same box — (1) Gmail, (2) the 6-digit code in SIX separate boxes (new `SixDigitCode` component: one digit per box, auto-advance, Backspace steps back, paste fills all six, auto-submits when complete; Continue button + Send a new code + Use a different Gmail alongside), (3) new password + confirm, reached only after the code checks out.
- Server: new `verifyResetCode` action + `peekEmailCode` helper — checks the reset code WITHOUT consuming it (wrong guesses still count toward the 5-attempt limit; expired/exhausted codes die the same way). `resetPassword` is unchanged and consumes the code when the password is actually set.

## Clips tall stage + social top bar on Clips and Feed (edit, Nov 20 2026)
- **Clips aspect (supersedes the 16:9 from the previous edit)**: owner's new reference is an Instagram-style post — tall and width-filling. The clip stage is now 4:5 (`aspectRatio: "4 / 5"`), and the snap-reel height grew to match (stage width × 1.25 + actions/caption). TikTok/native players fill the tall stage as before.
- **Clips header**: the Feed and "Check now" buttons at the top of Clips are REMOVED (refresh still exists in the empty states). In their place — and at the top of the Community Feed too — is a shared `SocialTopBar`: a search icon (expands a search field filtering that view's content — clips by creator/handle/title/caption, Feed via listFeed search), a message icon (goes straight to Messages), and an activity heart icon with an unread badge (per-device last-seen in localStorage) opening a Notifications panel in the Instagram reference layout (left sheet, Today / This week / Earlier groups, avatar + actor + "started following you. / liked your story. / liked your post. / commented on your post. / reposted your post." + time, tap a row to open that person's profile).
- **Notifications data**: new `listActivityNotifications` action builds the list only from real stored rows — follow_events (follows of me), story_likes on my stories, feed_reactions / feed_comments / feed_reposts on my posts — newest first, max 30, with real actor profiles/avatars. Nothing fabricated; guests and new accounts get an honest empty state.

## Sign-up slimming + automatic code emails + password eye toggles (edit, Nov 20 2026)
- **No Gmail confirmation during sign-up**: the "Confirm your Gmail (recommended)" box, its "Email me a confirmation code" button and the whole emailed-code step are removed from the Create Account Gmail step. Confirmation lives in Settings → Account & security ("Gmail not confirmed yet" + emailed-code flow via `requestEmailCode` / `verifyEmailCode`), where it can be done later.
- **No phone or linking during sign-up**: the phone-number field is removed from the Gmail step (sign-up sends `phone: ""`), and the entire "Link & join" step is removed — `SignUpStep` no longer has "link", the journey is Username → Gmail → Password → Platform → 2-step → Done, existing pre-password accounts start at Security, and `createAccount` success goes straight to Security. PSN / Xbox / Rockstar / Discord linking lives in Settings → Connectors (inputs already there; intro now says linking is not part of sign-up), and the phone number lives in Settings → General / Account & security (`set-phone`, helper now says add it any time there). Edit Profile also still offers the linked IDs.
- **Automatic code emails from the official Gmail**: `deliverEmailCode` now spawns an automatic delivery task instructed to send immediately — no manual step, owner pre-approved standing instruction — from `officialvicesunshine@gmail.com` ONLY, via the Gmail connector (+send on that connected account), with the exact recipient/subject/body; if that Gmail is not connected it must not send from any other address and must report that plainly. Triggers: `requestPasswordReset` (reset code), `requestEmailCode` (Settings confirmation), and NEW in `createAccount` — both the new-account and completed-legacy-account paths call `autoSendSignupConfirmationCode` (best-effort try/catch, never blocks creation), which issues a "verify" code and emails it with a signup-variant body telling the player to enter it in Settings. At the time of this edit the Gmail connector in the builder environment reported `not_connected` with no accounts, so actual inbox arrival requires Ben to connect officialvicesunshine@gmail.com in the Gmail connector; the wiring itself is automatic once connected.
- **Password eye toggles**: a shared `PasswordInput` component (eye / eye-off SVG, `aria-label` Show/Hide <field>, `aria-pressed`, type=button so it never submits) replaces every raw password input: sign-up (password + confirm), log in, reset (new + confirm), Settings change-password (`SetPasswordForm`: current + new + confirm) and the 2-step turn-off password.

## Stories scroll + private rule, true Back, trusted device, picture menus, custom banner, archive removed, top follow sheet, profile Share/Upload (edit, Nov 20 2026)
- **Story circles scroll sideways**: the client cap of 5 people is gone — the row renders everyone `listStories` returns (server caps at 24, followed first), in a snap-x overflow-x-auto row with touch scrolling; balls keep their size/spacing, the row scrolls, never wraps. Press behaviour (already present, kept): a ball with a visible story opens the StoryViewer; a ball with none opens the person's profile. Your own ball still opens the Add-to-story album picker.
- **Private accounts**: `listStories` now withholds a private account's stories from anyone who does not follow them (a `profile_follows` row = accepted follow; there is no separate follow-request system) — the story never reaches the client, so the tap lands on the profile, which shows the existing private/locked state instead of posts. `listStories` groups and people now carry `isPrivate`; a private non-followed person's ball shows a lock badge + "Private account" line. `listHighlights` got the same gate (private + blocked-by), and `listProfilePosts` already enforced it for posts.
- **Back always goes to the exact previous page**: `go()` no longer clears the back stack — every real page change pushes {tab, scrollY, openEggId} (cap 20), re-taps push nothing, and sign-in/sign-out start a fresh stack. Views that took `onBack` only when the stack was non-empty now always receive `goBack`. ProfileView takes `onBack`: its own-profile top arrow (previously hard-wired `go("home")` / "Back to Today") now goes back; viewing another profile keeps an internal stack for person→person hops, and a profile handed over from Feed/Clips (vv-open-player) goes back to that exact page. `goBack`'s empty-stack fallback goes Home directly without pushing, so Back can never bounce.
- **Remember me / Trust this device**: the log-in credentials step has a "Remember me — trust this device" checkbox (default on, remembered per device in `leonida-trust-device`). Ticked: the account key persists in `leonida-player-key` and the player stays logged in on that device. Unticked: the signed-out tombstone takes the storage slot — this session is signed in in place, the next visit on that device starts signed out.
- **Edit profile picture menus**: pressing the avatar circle opens a small menu (Upload profile picture — device picker; Take picture — capture="environment" input feeding the same moderated upload; Choose avatar — the catalogue grid, moved up beside the banner grid; Remove picture when a custom picture is set). The old bottom avatar box with its Upload / Choose buttons is deleted. Pressing the banner (whole banner is the button, "Change banner" pill is the hint) opens its menu: Upload a video, Upload a picture, Choose a banner (the calling-card grid), Remove uploaded banner when set. No Take picture for the banner.
- **Custom banner media (new backend)**: profiles gain `banner_blob_key` / `banner_media_type` (migration 0063). New actions `uploadCustomBanner` (JPG/PNG picture ≤4 MB, moderation-checked before storage like the avatar; MP4/WebM video ≤12 MB, same limits as Feed media) and `removeCustomBanner`. While set, the uploaded media is the profile banner (ProfileBanner renders it, video autoplaying muted/looped), taking precedence over the calling card; equipping a calling card (equipCosmetic, both editor and Customise paths incl. cache writes) clears it server- and cache-side. `getProfile` and `getSocialProfile` return `bannerMediaUrl` / `bannerMediaType`.
- **Archive removed**: the "View archive" button, the ProfileArchiveSheet, the `archiveOpen` state and ProfileHighlights' dead `onOpenArchive` prop are gone. `listStoryArchive` remains only as the Highlight editor's story source (not a user-facing archive).
- **Followers/Following sheet from the top**: FollowListSheet is now a smaller top-anchored box (top-3/top-8, max-h 64dvh, fully rounded, new `.top-sheet` drop-in animation in theme.css, reduced-motion covered); the bottom-sheet grab handle is gone.
- **Profile Share / Upload**: my profile gains Share and Upload pill buttons beside Edit profile, and the empty Posts tab ("Share Photos") gains a pink Upload button next to "Share your first photo". Both presses hand over via the `vv-open-create` device key to the Feed's Create tab (a new page for sharing/uploading; Back returns to the profile). The value is "share" or "upload": for "upload" the creator auto-clicks its gallery label once, opening the phone's OWN album picker — Recents grid, All/Videos/Photos tabs and the photo-access permission prompt are the phone's, never the site's; if the browser blocks a programmatic open, the creator's upload controls are on screen.

## Share-a-GTA-link removed from Feed; search covers users/videos/topics + personal history (edit, Nov 20 2026)
- **"Share a GTA post from YouTube / X instead" removed**: the toggle link under the Feed's stories row, the paste-a-link form it opened, and their client state/mutation (`shareExternal`) are deleted from FeedView. Existing shared posts in the Feed are untouched; the server `shareExternalPost` action remains but has no client entry point.
- **Search covers more**: `globalSearch` gains two groups — **Videos** (Feed posts whose media is YouTube/native video or whose kind is Clip / Streamer Clip / Gameplay / Tutorial, matched on title/body/author) and **Events** (title/type/host/description) — and the client group list is reordered and relabelled: Users (formerly Players), Videos, News, Feed, Events, Bugs, Guides, Crews, Database. Video hits open Clips, event hits open Events, in both the overlay and the full results view.
- **Personal search history**: the search overlay saves every submitted/picked search on-device (`vv-search-history`, newest first, max 10, deduped) and shows "Your recent searches" above Most searched when the box is empty — tap to run it again, × removes one term, "Clear search history" wipes the list. Separate from the global Most searched counts (recordSearch unchanged).

## Banner/avatar overlap fix + picture positioner; profile Share link; Gmail-code log-in; Messages full width + chat-header profiles; crew page stripped to its header card (edit, Nov 20 2026)
- **Edit profile overlap fixed**: in the Edit profile sheet the banner wrapper sat at z-20 above the avatar row at z-10, so the banner painted over the top of the avatar circle. Stacking swapped (banner z-10, avatar row z-20) — the circle now paints on top of the banner's bottom edge, as on the profile itself.
- **Picture positioner (preview before save)**: picking an avatar picture (upload or camera) or a banner picture no longer uploads immediately. A `PicPositioner` dialog opens with the picture framed in its real shape (circle for the avatar, 10:3 strip for the banner): drag to reposition, zoom slider (1–3×, centre-anchored), Cancel, or Save picture. Save renders the framed picture to a canvas (640×640 avatar / 1280-wide banner JPG) and hands that file to the existing safety-checked upload (`uploadCustomAvatar` / `uploadCustomBanner`), so the stored picture is exactly what was framed. Banner videos still upload directly. Type/size validation messages are unchanged and run before the preview opens.
- **Profile header**: the Upload button is removed (uploading still lives in the Feed Create tab and the empty Posts tab). Share is now a share link of your own profile for outside ViceSunshine, following the existing #ref / #event convention: the share text carries `#player=<handle>`; the phone share sheet is used where available, otherwise the text is copied with an on-page confirmation. New server action `findPlayerByGamertag` resolves the handle (case-insensitive, spaces stripped) and the shell's `#player=` handler stores `vv-player-link` and opens the Profile tab, where ProfileView resolves it and opens that profile. No playerKey is ever placed in a link.
- **Log-in: password + Gmail code, switchable both ways**: `signInWithPassword` now accepts `emailCode` and `skipEmailCode`, and its response gains `needsEmailCode`. A correct password automatically issues a "login" email code (new purpose in the existing email-code helpers; `deliverEmailCode` gained the log-in subject/body) and returns `needsEmailCode` — the client moves to a Gmail code step in the same box: six boxes (the shared SixDigitCode), Resend code, Remember this device, Confirm, and "Other way". The code is only peeked until every factor passes, then consumed, so a pending authenticator step never burns it. "Other way" on the code screen finishes with the password alone (`skipEmailCode`), or — when the player came in code-only — returns to the password screen. The password screen gained "Use a Gmail code instead": new action `requestLoginCode` emails a code by identifier (generic response either way, once-a-minute limit) and the code alone signs in, no password — including for pre-password legacy accounts. Authenticator/backup-code 2-step still applies afterwards when enabled. Verified on the sandbox DB: password → needsEmailCode; wrong code → stays on the code step; wrong password → generic fail; skipEmailCode → signed in. Actual inbox arrival still depends on the official Gmail being connected in the Gmail connector (unchanged blocker).
- **Messages fills the tab**: the shell's content wrapper no longer caps the Messages tab at 1240px (`max-w-none`); the sidebar is untouched. The chat area and composer stretch the full tab width.
- **Chat headers open profiles**: pressing the avatar or name at the top of a DM opens that person's profile (vv-open-player hand-off); the same press works on the empty-thread avatar/name and on every member row in a group/crew/event chat's members list.
- **Crew page (ClanHome) stripped to its header card**: only the banner, emblem, name, motto, chips, level bar and the two stats grids (Trophies, Members, Wins, Events, Crew Level, Season Points, Crew Wallet, Events Held, Season Rank, Avg Member Level) plus the member bar stay, with Join / Leave. Removed: the points-explanation text, crew description text and rules box under the stats; the Crew Awards section; the Leader picture & banner editor; the Edit crew section; the crew info modal (emblem is no longer a button); and the Feed / Roster / Chat / War & Squads / Points / Log tab bar with all its panels. The now-unused client helpers (`PlayerAvatar`, `ClanPointsPanel`, `PointEntryRow`, `activityIcon`, `ClanChannelManager`) and ClanHome state/mutations that only served those sections were deleted with them (noUnusedLocals). Server actions are untouched — crew chat still exists in Messages → Crews & Events, but application review and roster management no longer have a UI entry point, per the owner's "only this stays" direction.

## Step-by-step posting; name rules; region picker; linked-accounts page; Settings lock + real avatar; profile Main tab removed; log-in code screen cleaned (edit, Nov 20 2026)
- **Posting is step by step**: `FeedCreator` is rebuilt as four steps — Capture (live camera photo/video with a Photo/Video mode toggle, or the phone's library picker, several picks at once up to 4 items) → Preview (exactly how it will look; Retake, Remove this one, Add more) → Edit (per item: Exposure, Shadow, Contrast, Colour sliders + effect presets None/Neon/Sunset/Mono/Cool — the same filter string drives the preview and, for photos, is baked into the file on a canvas at publish; on videos the settings are a live preview only and the clip publishes as recorded, stated on the step) → Details (Post/Story/Clips destination, title, description, category at the bottom for posts, then Publish; each picked item posts as its own post/story with the shared title/description). Uploads now happen at publish time from the local items, so nothing is sent before the last step. A text-only post can skip straight from Capture to Details. The Stories row no longer renders on the Create tab — stories cannot be viewed while posting.
- **Username is permanent; display name + clan tag change monthly**: new `profiles.display_name_changed_at` / `clan_tag_changed_at` (migration 0064). `assertGamertagPermanent` runs in `updateProfile`, `saveProfile` and `saveSettings`: a different gamertag (beyond letter case) for an existing profile is refused. `nameChangeGate` runs in `updateProfile` (display name + clan tag) and `saveSettings` (display name): changing either away from a non-empty current value stamps the time and, for non-Premium players, blocks another change for 30 days with the exact unlock date in the error; Premium (via `premiumActiveFor`) changes any time. First-time setting from empty is free and starts no clock. Verified on the sandbox DB: first set free, second change stamps, third change blocked ("…you can change it again on December 20, 2026"), gamertag change refused. Client: Settings username field is read-only with the permanent note; Edit profile's username is read-only for existing profiles; sign-up's username step warns the name is permanent; the Name and Clan tag helpers state the 30-day rule; the About sheet's "Username changes" row now reads "Permanent — usernames can't be changed" at zero.
- **Display name cooldown tightened (Nov 21, 2026, owner's direction)**: display name only — free members can change it once every 3 months, Premium members once a month (calendar months via `addMonths` in `nameChangeGate`, server `actions.ts`); Premium is no longer unlimited for the display name. Clan tag is unchanged (30 days free, Premium any time). The blocked error names the exact unlock date and the applicable rule. Client helpers updated in both places that state the rule: Edit profile's Name helper ("once every 3 months… Premium… once a month") and the Settings username note ("once every 3 months; Premium once a month"). The Ask Vice site description string was updated to match.
- **Display name LOCKS on entry (Nov 21, 2026, owner's direction: "it should be locked once i enter one")**: the tightened cooldown above still let a name be entered AND changed once for free — the gate only started the clock at the first change away from an entered name, so from the owner's seat the name could be retyped repeatedly right after entering it. Fixed at the cause in `nameChangeGate` (server `actions.ts`): entering a display name now stamps `displayNameChangedAt` on the spot (including a name entered on the create-profile insert in `updateProfile`), so the name is locked from the moment it is entered; clearing it counts as a change and is refused while locked too. Lock anchor = the entry/change stamp, falling back to the profile's `createdAt` for names entered before stamps existed (never locks a long-held name longer than the rule allows). Periods unchanged (3 months free / 1 month Premium, calendar months). New server helper `displayNameLockedUntil` mirrors the gate and is returned by `getProfile` and `getSettings` as `nameLockedUntil` (ISO, "" when unlocked / no name): the Edit profile Name box and the Settings Display name box render read-only (dimmed, not-allowed cursor) with "Your name is locked — you can change it again on <date>" while it is in the future; the unlocked helper text now says entering a name locks it. Verified in the sandbox against the owner's real row (zazz — locked until February 21, 2027) and fresh probes: entry → immediate change refused, clear refused, Settings-path change refused, same-name save with other edits allowed, create-with-name locked from birth.
- **Searchable region picker**: a shared `RegionPicker` combobox (type a few letters, the list filters, tap to pick; free text still saves) replaces the plain region inputs in Edit profile (saveProfile form), Settings and Create crew, backed by a fixed list of ~60 regions/countries.
- **Linked accounts are their own page**: the Linked accounts fieldset no longer sits inside Edit profile. Edit profile carries a "Linked accounts" row (summary of what's linked, chevron) that opens a full-screen Linked accounts page over the editor — same fields and visibility toggles, same profile state, its own Save (runs the same profile save) and a Back arrow to Edit profile.
- **Settings / Your account**: `getSettings` now returns `avatarUrl` (via `profileAvatarUrl`), and the account card shows the player's real avatar (uploaded picture first, else equipped avatar art) via `CosmeticAvatar` instead of a gradient letter. Account info is behind the password (owner's direction): new action `verifyAccountPassword` (returns only ok/hasPassword); while locked, the Your account section shows only the account card and an unlock card (password with the eye toggle); Status, Plan and the account form render only after a correct password, for as long as Settings stays open. Accounts without a password are not gated (nothing to enter).
- **Profile Main tab removed**: the Main panel of the profile details section (Tracked news/goals, Vice Credits Balance card, Member Since, Friends, Crew, Level/Rank, Reputation, Events Attended, the stats footer text, the Linked accounts card and the Player Card) is deleted, along with the Main tab itself — the details section now opens on Activity, with Customise beside it. Orphaned helpers/state (`TrackedGoalsCard`, `friendsOpen`, `cardCopied`, `memberSinceLabel`) were removed with it. Only the top of the profile plus Activity/Customise remain.
- **Log-in code screen cleaned**: the "Enter your code to log in" step drops its subtitle, the "Logging in as … — password accepted" chip, the repeated "Enter the 6-digit code … one digit per box" line and the server message line. Left: heading, Resend code, the six boxes, Remember this device, Confirm, Other way, Back, and error feedback when a code is wrong. The auth subtitle render is now conditional so an empty subtitle leaves no gap.

## Sidebar Create Account removed + ads aligned; sign-in gate; sidebar dropdown order; Messages full page (edit, Nov 20 2026)

- **Sidebar Create Account button removed**: the desktop sidebar's bottom-left Create Account button is gone (owner's direction). Visitors sign up from the account trigger or page prompts. The mobile header's Create Account button (shown on phone, where there is no sidebar) stays.
- **Sidebar ad strips aligned**: the ad stack's `-mr-5` negative margin was removed, so the three strips line up with the rest of the sidebar items — same width and inset as the nav rows, no longer stretching further right.
- **Full-screen sign-in gate for guests**: pressing the sidebar's bottom account area ("Welcome to ViceSunshine / Free plan · Not signed up yet") while signed out now opens a full-screen sign-in page covering the whole web (modelled on the Tracker sign-in page): VS logo, "What's your email?", an Email box, Continue, an "or" divider, and "Log in with Username". Continue runs the existing `checkEmail` action — a Gmail that already has an account routes to Log in with the identifier prefilled; a new Gmail routes to Create Account with the Gmail step prefilled (hand-off via the `vv-auth-open` device key + event, consumed by SignUpView on mount). "Log in with Username" opens the log-in form directly; to make that real, `signInWithPassword` and `requestLoginCode` now also match the identifier against the account's gamertag (case-insensitive), and the log-in identifier label reads "Gmail, phone number or username". Signed-in users keep the account dropdown from the same trigger. Closing the gate returns to browsing as a guest.
- **Sidebar dropdown order**: the account dropdown box (Your profile / Wallet / Premium / Report an issue / Settings) no longer opens as an absolute overlay on top of the ad strips and the Community section. It renders in flow between the ad strips and the account trigger, so reading the sidebar bottom upward: trigger, dropdown box, ad strips, Community — nothing overlaps.
- **Messages fills the full page**: on the Messages tab the shell's main column drops its padding, the page wrapper becomes a full-height flex column, and the chat shell (`.dm-shell.dm-fill`) stretches edge to edge with no fixed min-height or card frame; the guest "Your messages" state centers in the full height. The About ViceSunshine footer is not rendered on the Messages tab at all (it remains on every other tab).

## Profile Share is a QR share screen; Database picture restyle per kind; Database auto-sync; character art fidelity v4 (edit, Nov 20–21 2026)

- **Profile Share → Instagram-style QR share screen**: the profile Share button no longer copies a text link — it opens `ProfileShareScreen`, a full-screen gradient share screen modelled on the Instagram QR share: X close (top left), a COLOR pill (top centre) cycling five gradient themes (Vice pink, Lagoon, Sunset, Grape, Mint), a white rounded card holding a real QR code of the profile link with the VS logo over its centre and `@HANDLE` under it in the theme colour, and three tiles — Share profile (system share sheet, copy fallback), Copy link (clipboard, flips to "Copied"), Download (renders the QR card to a PNG and downloads it). The link is `PROFILE_LINK_BASE` + `#player=<handle>` (single constant to change when ViceSunshine gets its own public domain); no playerKey is ever in a link. The QR encoder (`client/src/qr.ts`, byte mode, EC level M) was verified module-for-module against the reference Python `qrcode` encoder (all 40 test matrices identical), and the final styled render (dot modules, rounded finder patterns, centre logo) was decode-tested with ZXing — the decoder family behind phone cameras — on three real profile payloads: all decode to exactly the profile link. The Download PNG renders plain square modules for maximum scannability.
- **Profile Share → the Vice Pass (edit, Nov 22 2026)**: the owner asked for the share screen to stop copying Instagram ("make it different, make it unique"). `ProfileShareScreen` is now a ViceSunshine "Vice Pass" — a Leonida sunset licence-plate / backstage-pass card built from the sharer's real profile (new props from the Profile page: display name, avatar, member-since, crew tag + crew colour, Premium flag, follower/following counts from the social profile). Design: dark screen (no full-screen gradient), a sunset "plate" header in the chosen finish colourway with VS logo + VICESUNSHINE · LEONIDA lettering + a striped retro sun, the identity block, then a perforated ticket stub (dashed tear line with punched notches) holding a plain SQUARE-module QR of the same real `#player=<public ID>` link beside the real counts. The old COLOR pill is replaced by a "Finish" swatch row (Vice Sunset, Lagoon Dusk, Neon Night, Gold Hour, Palm Mint) that recolours only the pass and its saved image. Actions renamed to match: Share pass (system share sheet, same copy fallback), Copy link (unchanged), Save pass (the Download now draws the WHOLE pass to a 1200×760 PNG — plate, sun, avatar photo or initials medallion, identity, stub QR — instead of a bare QR card). The QR payload, encoder and link are unchanged from the verified build above; only the module styling changed from rounded dots to plain squares (the same rendering the old Download PNG already used).
- **Database picture style per kind (TRN framing, polished past TRN)**: one shared system — very dark navy-black page, slightly lighter dark navy-purple cards at 16px radius, breadcrumb `GTA VI » {Section}` with the section in pale pink, large uppercase white heading with a grey "Every {kind} in the GTA VI database" subtitle, Group/Sort dropdowns and a grid/list toggle (grid pink when active) top-right, dark search box, pale-pink filter pills, bold white uppercase group headings with the count in a small outlined square, whole card is a link, and a rating badge top-left of every picture (dark pill, thin outline, flame icon, white bold % or —). Per kind: **Characters** 3:4 portrait (~80% of the card), face at `50% 15%` so it sits in the upper third fully in frame, panel with only an outlined role pill + 18px name. **Vehicles** 16:9 in-world photo filling the frame, tiny outlined pill tags (category/type/manufacturer) then the name. **Weapons** 3:2 picture on a flat solid dark navy background the same colour as the card, one class pill, bold name, no subtitle. **Locations** ~1.65:1 picture inset 6px from the card edge, no status badge or any overlay text inside the picture, sentence-case kind pill then the name only; Locations group by Kind alphabetically. Group control offers Class / Status / None; list layout renders compact rows. Detail pages reuse the same per-kind aspect for the overview and gallery figures and the Variants cards; performance bars are pink-gradient. All pictures remain original ViceSunshine illustrations — TRN's layout/framing only, never its or Rockstar's images.
- **Database auto-sync (`refreshDbEntries`)**: a new server action keeps the Database current with no manual adding. Throttled to one real check per 30 minutes (`last_db_research_at`), it searches official Rockstar pages first, then GTA coverage; an inference pass extracts only entries a result explicitly names (kind, name, category, short description in the model's own words, status, source URL copied exactly and validated against the result set). Upserts match on (kind, normalised name): new entries are capped at 6 per run and flagged `auto_discovered` (new column, migration 0065), updates at 8 per run; a "Confirmed in trailer" status is never downgraded and an official-source entry is never overwritten by a non-official claim. `seedExamples`' catalog reconcile now skips `auto_discovered` rows so the daily sync cannot erase them. Triggers: a silent artifact cron (`vicesunshine-db-live-sync`, every 6h) plus a once-per-mount call from the Database page; new entries get original illustrations automatically from the existing artwork pipeline. Verified end-to-end on the sandbox (clean run, `{added: 0, updated: 0}` when nothing new existed).
- **Character/vehicle/weapon/location art fidelity v4**: the art generator's per-kind framing rules were rewritten — same exact subject as in GTA, drawn from a fresh angle official artwork does not use, nothing added and nothing changed. For characters, facial fidelity is mandatory wording in every prompt: same face, same features, same hair, recognisable as that exact character; facial hair appears ONLY where the entry's written spec states it (Jason Duval: stubble; Brian Heder: grey beard — every other character spec states none, so they render clean-shaven); no added beards, moustaches, extra hair, scars, tattoos or glasses. Vehicles keep their exact body/details from one of five fresh in-world viewpoints; weapons are flat-background cut-outs with natural scale discipline (long guns 85–100% of frame width, pistols/handguns stay small with empty dark space around them); locations are wide, tightly cropped, eye-level exteriors. A one-time v4 repaint queue in `seedArtwork` repaints every existing entry once under the new rules (12 per 15-minute artwork run), so the whole catalog rolls over automatically.

## Account creation actually works + phone top bar Profile/search restored (edit, Nov 20 2026)

- **The blocker**: the sign-in gate forced a new account through a "VERIFY YOUR EMAIL" stage BEFORE the account existed — `requestEmailCode` issues the code and delivery depends on a spawned agent task sending it from the official Gmail via the Gmail connector. If that task is slow, fails, or the Gmail is not connected at that moment, no code arrives and account creation can never finish. Returning sign-in had the same trap: a correct password moved to an emailed-code screen with no way to finish on the password alone, even though `signInWithPassword` already supported `skipEmailCode`.
- **The fix (gate only, server contract unchanged)**: creating an account no longer requires any email code. Email → Continue (`checkEmail`) → "Create New" now goes straight to Choose your username (live `checkUsername`) → Create a password → `createAccount` with `emailCode: ""` → signed straight in via the existing in-place adoption. The gate's verify stage, its `sendVerify`/`checkGateCode` calls and the `verifiedCode` hand-off are removed from `AuthGatePage`. On creation the server still AUTOMATICALLY emails a confirmation code from the official ViceSunshine Gmail (best-effort, never blocks), and the player confirms the address later in Settings — Settings already had exactly that copy and flow ("you don't need to do it during sign-up", `requestEmailCode`/`verifyEmailCode`). `createAccount` and `requestEmailCode` descriptions updated to say so. The `checkGateCode` action remains published (harmless, no client caller now).
- **Password-only escape on the code screen**: the gate's sign-in mutation now passes a `skip` flag through to `skipEmailCode`. On the WELCOME BACK code screen, a player who arrived with a correct password gets "Can't get the code? Finish with my password only" (finishes on the password alone; 2-step still applies when enabled). A player who arrived code-only (no password entered) gets "Use my password instead", which returns to Username sign in.
- **Phone top bar — Profile + search restored (owner's direction, phone only, desktop untouched)**: the black phone header keeps the avatar ball on the left (opens the drawer) and the VS logo centred (one tap = back to the top, unchanged), and the right side is again the search icon button (opens the same search overlay as the desktop sidebar search bar, `setSearchOpen(true)`) beside a "Profile" pill — signed out it opens the full-screen sign-in gate, signed in it opens the profile. The person-with-plus icon that replaced them is gone. The VS button is absolutely centred so the wider right cluster cannot push it off-centre. Search also remains at the top of the phone drawer.

## Smooth page transitions everywhere (edit, Nov 20 2026)

- **Ask**: moving between pages/tabs felt sudden — make the whole page transition smooth, fade/slide, on laptop and phone.
- **What existed**: `.page-enter` (theme.css) already remounted each view on navigation (shell wrapper keyed by tab + egg + navSeq), but it was only a 240ms opacity fade starting at 0.4 — too faint and short to read as a transition, so page changes still felt like a jump.
- **Fix**: `vv-page-in` is now a full crossfade (opacity 0 → 1) plus a gentle 12px settle over 380ms on the same soft ease-out curve. The settle animates `padding-top`, never `transform`/`filter` — the standing trap documented in theme.css: a transform in these keyframes makes the wrapper a containing block and breaks every `position:fixed` sheet inside the views. Padding creates no containing block, so sheets, the full-screen map and viewers inside a page are unaffected. One shared shell wrapper means the same transition covers every tab/page move, Back, and egg detail opens, identically on phone and laptop; `prefers-reduced-motion` still disables it. Splash exit, drawer, sheets, search overlay and carousel already had their own fades/slides and are unchanged.
- **Account verification (sandbox, this build)**: `checkEmail` on a fresh Gmail → available; `createAccount` with `emailCode: ""` (the new gate flow) → ok, profile created with the 500 starting credits; `signInWithPassword` with `skipEmailCode: true` (the new "Finish with my password only" path) → ok with the account's playerKey returned.

## Code email: dark-only background + fast direct delivery (edit, Nov 20 2026)

- **Owner's three asks in one report**: (1) "I'm not getting a code from Gmail" — codes were not arriving; (2) the new branded email looked nice but rendered with a LIGHT lavender/white background in his Gmail (screenshot: pale card, black code boxes, dark text) — he wants the whole email background dark navy like the ViceSunshine theme, no light/white/lavender; (3) the code must arrive fast, directly, immediately when asked for — no delay. Plus a standing instruction from the same conversation: **stop creating test accounts to test the sign-up/code flow (no more ben.test.vs116-style accounts) — Ben is the one testing and will test it himself.** This build therefore created NO test accounts and sent NO test code emails; verification was build + page-load audit + code inspection only.
- **Why the email rendered light**: Gmail's app dark mode inverted the previous build — it carried only CSS `background` styles and `<meta name="color-scheme" content="dark light">`, so Gmail flipped the dark card to pale lavender and the white code boxes to black. **Fix in `deliverEmailCode` (`server/src/actions.ts`)**: the email is now dark-only on every layer mail clients respect — `color-scheme` / `supported-color-schemes` are `only dark`; `bgcolor` attributes sit on the shell (#0a0a12), the card (#12121f), the code boxes (#1e1e30), the expiry pill (#1b1b2c) and the gradient cells (purple fallbacks where gradients are dropped); a `<style>` block re-asserts the dark colours with `!important` under `prefers-color-scheme: dark`, Gmail/Outlook's `[data-ogsc]` / `[data-ogsb]` dark-mode hooks, and the `u + .vs-body` Gmail selector, via `vs-bg` / `vs-card` / `vs-box` / `vs-pill` / `vs-white` / `vs-dim` / `vs-faint` classes. The design itself is unchanged (gradient border, white VS on the gradient, wordmark, headline, gradient CTA banner, expiry pill, security note, full footer, hidden preheader) except the six code boxes are now DARK navy (#1e1e30, subtle #3d3d5c border) with WHITE digits — there is no light surface left anywhere in the email. All three purposes (verify/signup, login, reset) share the fix.
- **Why codes were slow / missing, and the fix**: delivery is a spawned agent task on the Gmail connector (the artifact server has no direct mail channel — unchanged architecture), but the old task prompt invited preamble work and a failed HTML send ended the attempt, so a quoting/formatting failure meant no code at all. The delivery task is now minimal and speed-first: ONE `accounts` lookup, then ONE `+send --html` as its first real action (no status checks, triage, browsing or drafts), and if that send fails for any reason it immediately retries once with the plain-text body so the code still lands. The resend throttle dropped from 60s to 20s end-to-end: server `EMAIL_CODE_RESEND_MS = 20_000` in `emailCodeSentRecently` (used by `requestEmailCode`, `requestPasswordReset`, `requestLoginCode` and the password path in `signInWithPassword`), the user-facing copy updated to match, and the sign-in gate's Resend countdown in `AuthGatePage` (`client/src/App.tsx`) is now 20s on both the VERIFY YOUR EMAIL and WELCOME BACK! screens. Honest limit (unchanged): actual inbox arrival still requires officialvicesunshine@gmail.com to be connected in the Gmail connector and the delivery task to run; the task still refuses to send from any other address and reports plainly when the official Gmail is not connected. No schema change.
- **Fully automatic, seconds-fast delivery — owner's follow-up (Nov 20 2026)**: Ben is testing account creation himself and asked whether anyone has to touch Gmail for him to get a code — the answer built here is no: delivery is fully automatic (the spawned delivery task is the send; no human approves, drafts or sends anything) and fires the instant a code is issued. To cut the automatic path to its floor, the delivery task now carries the exact connector CLI commands (`hatch_gws_cli gmail accounts`, then `gmail +send --account <id> --to … --subject … --html …`) and is explicitly told not to read connector docs, skills or help output, not to run status checks or triage, and to make the send its first real action after the single account lookup. Honest limit, stated to Ben: a hard "within 3 seconds" guarantee is not possible on this platform — the artifact server has no direct mail channel, so the code rides the automatic agent task to the connected official Gmail and then Gmail's own delivery/notification; that is seconds-to-a-minute in practice, fully hands-free, but not a guaranteed 3-second SLA. No test accounts were created and no test emails were sent for this change (owner's standing instruction above — Ben tests himself); verification was build + code inspection.

## Sign-up restored to TRN's exact order — code BEFORE username/password (edit, Nov 20 2026)

- **Owner's direction (explicit, re-confirmed)**: "follow TRN how to create an account exactly: email → CREATE NEW ACCOUNT? → Create New → VERIFY YOUR EMAIL with the code → then username and password → account created → straight to Profile, and the rest are done in the profile (change name etc there). Do not put username/password first, do not skip the code step in sign-up." This supersedes the interim no-code sign-up from the "Account creation actually works" build above (that build's phone-top-bar and password-only sign-in escape are unchanged — the escape is on the returning sign-in WELCOME BACK screen only, never on the sign-up code step).
- **Gate (`AuthGatePage`, `client/src/App.tsx`)**: new stage `verify-email` between `create` and `choose-username`. Pressing Create New fires `requestEmailCode` (purpose "verify") and opens "VERIFY YOUR EMAIL" — "To register your account, please enter the code we sent to [email]", the shared `SixDigitCode` boxes (typing the 6th digit submits), Back (to CREATE NEW ACCOUNT?) + Resend with the 20s countdown, and NO skip / password fallback. The code is checked by the NEW `verifySignupCode` action; only on success does the gate advance to Choose your username → Create a password → Create Account. If the player goes Back from username to the verify stage after verifying, an "Email verified — Continue" button returns them without re-entering a code (unless they Resend, which clears the proof and requires the fresh code).
- **Server (`server/src/actions.ts`)**: `verifySignupCode` consumes the "verify" code and returns a signup proof token — a random token whose SHA-256 hash is stored in `email_codes` under purpose "signup" (`issueSignupProof`, 15-minute life). The proof exists because the emailed code itself lives only 1 minute (owner's rule): long enough to type it, not long enough to then pick a username and password, so the proof carries the verification across those two steps. `createAccount` now REQUIRES the proof (`signupToken`) or the code itself (`emailCode`, consumed directly) and refuses with a clear message when neither is given — the code step can no longer be bypassed by any caller; the account starts with `emailVerified = 1`. The old at-creation confirmation email (`autoSendSignupConfirmationCode`) is removed — the address is already verified before the account exists. No schema change (purpose is free text on the existing `email_codes` table).
- **Straight to Profile, rest done there**: unchanged wiring — `createAccount` success → gate `adopt` → shell `handleSignedIn` → Profile tab; display name / details / linked accounts / platform / 2-step all remain Profile/Settings work, and the password step copy now says so. Settings' unconfirmed-Gmail copy updated to match (new accounts verify during sign-up; the Settings code flow remains for addresses not yet confirmed).
- **No test accounts / no test code emails in this build** (owner's standing instruction — Ben tests himself): verification is build + audit + action-level checks that create nothing (e.g. `createAccount` without a proof must refuse; `verifySignupCode` with a wrong code must fail).

## Profile + navigation round — 9 items (edit, Nov 21 2026)

Owner's combined round, Muse artifact only (standalone/live site untouched). All in `client/src/App.tsx` + `server/src/actions.ts`; no schema change, no new external source.

1. **Videos tab empty state**: new `ShareClipsEmpty`, an exact twin of `SharePhotosEmpty` (same centred layout/spacing/buttons): play icon in the 76px circle, "Share Clips", "When you share clips, they will appear on your profile.", Upload button (same album-picker hand-off as Photos) and a "Share your first clip" link. Used on your own Videos tab and (name-swapped, no buttons) on other profiles' Videos tabs, replacing the old one-line "No videos or clips yet…" text.
2. **Highlights**: the highlight circles row and the + "New story" circle are GONE from `ProfileHighlights`. In their place one "Highlights" row; pressing it opens a sheet. On your own profile the sheet is your full story history — every story you ever posted from `listStoryArchive` (live + expired, with dates/views/likes; tap one to play it and the ones after it in the StoryViewer) — because you can only highlight stories you posted; your existing highlights sit on top of the sheet (tap to play, Edit, and New highlight opens the existing editor, whose picker is the same archive). On someone else's profile the sheet lists their highlights to play. Posting a story moved to tapping your own profile picture (opens the FeedCreator on STORY, as the + circle did); other people's avatar taps are unchanged (story if live, else enlarged picture).
3. **Back buttons**: Premium now takes `onBack={goBack}` and leads with the site's standard "← Back" ghost button (the HistoryView/ServiceStatusView pattern — pops the nav stack and restores the exact previous page AND scroll). Legal pages got the same button (UpdatesView pattern). Pages that already had one (Profile, Clans, Map, Leaderboard, History, Status, Updates, Analytics, Messages, egg/code detail) are unchanged; primary sidebar tabs (News, Feed, etc.) never had one by design.
4. **Followers/Following search**: `FollowListSheet` has a search box under the tabs filtering the open list by display name / @handle as you type, with a "No one in this list matches…" empty state.
5. **Mutual follows**: `getSocialProfile` now returns `followedBy` — real `profile_follows` rows: people the viewer follows who also follow this profile (max 12). Another player's profile shows "Followed by A, B and N others" under the counts when the viewer doesn't follow them yet. In the follow lists, people the viewer knows (themself, people they follow, people who follow them) are stable-sorted to the top, ahead of everyone else.
6. **Verified Followers removed**: the tab is gone from `FollowListSheet` for every profile — only Followers and Following remain; the Premium-filter list and its copy are deleted.
7. **Level removed**: every visible PERSONAL level indicator is gone — the "Level N" badge on your profile and on other profiles, the "Level / Rank" stats card (now a Rank Points card: points big, "Earned from activity inside ViceSunshine" + crew season line), the "ViceSunshine level — from your activity…" note under your name, the "· Level N" in follow-list rows, the Settings plan line's "ViceSunshine Level N" segment, and the About page's level displays (heading is now "Rewards & Rank Points", milestone chips read "Milestone N", the "your level right now" line shows Rank Points only; Refund Policy copy updated to match). The level VALUE is still computed server-side (milestone credit payouts depend on it) — only its display is removed. Crew Level / crew "LV" marks and the Rank page are untouched, as directed. The hidden Challenges page (SHOW_CHALLENGES = false, unreachable) still contains its level card — it is not a visible surface.
8. **Reposts are media-only**: `repostFeedPost` refuses a text-only original ("Only photos, videos and clips can be reposted — text posts can't be reposted.") — media = image / video / YouTube embed or an uploaded picture blob on the root post. The Feed post card renders NO Repost button (and no repost panel) for text-only posts; on a repost row the ORIGINAL's media decides. `listProfilePosts` mode "reposts" filters to reposts whose root has media, so existing text reposts disappear from every Reposts tab. Text posts themselves are untouched. Chat-message reposts (`repostChatMessage`, a separate earlier feature that quotes a chat message as your own new post, not a post repost) were left as-is — the ask and its screenshots were about Feed posts and Reposts tabs.
9. **Repost tap goes straight to the post**: both Reposts tabs (yours and other profiles') now pass an onOpen that navigates directly to the Feed on that post (`go("feed", title || author)` — the same landing the old sheet's "Open in Feed" used). The bottom preview sheet (Like / Comments / Open in Feed, with its missing bottom bar) no longer opens for reposts at all; it still serves the Posts and Videos tabs.

Sandbox verification (actions): `getSocialProfile` returned 12 real mutuals for a bot pair; `repostFeedPost` on a text-only post failed with the new refusal, on a YouTube clip it succeeded and the repost appears in that player's Reposts tab; the editor account's Reposts tab — which held the text repost from the owner's screenshot — now returns empty.

## Save button → library fix (Nov 21, 2026)

Owner report: "the save button is not functioning like it doesn't save to my library." Diagnosis from the inspection evidence + source: the library kinds (news / guide / bug / clan / egg / event) already round-tripped through `account_saves`, but the two Save buttons the owner actually presses in the Feed / Clips / Database never reached the Saved library page:
- Feed post Save and Clip Save wrote only an on-device localStorage list (`viceverse-saved-feed-v1`) plus a `feed_saves` row used purely as an author-analytics count — and nothing anywhere displayed those saves (the profile's Saved media tab had been removed in favour of the sidebar library, which had no posts/clips section; the purpose-built `getFeedPostsByIds` action was never called by the client). `setFeedSave` also silently dropped saves of your OWN posts.
- The Database detail Save button wrote only localStorage (`viceverse-saved-db-v1`) — it never called `setAccountSave` kind "db" and the library never showed Database entries.

Fix:
- New action `listSavedFeedPosts({ playerKey })`: returns the player's saved Feed posts/clips (profile-post item shape, most recently saved first) from the server-side `feed_saves` rows, with the Feed's own hidden/blocked/flagged filtering.
- `setFeedSave` now records saves of your own posts too (they belong in your library); profile analytics still exclude self-saves from the author's "saves received" counts, so nothing is double-counted.
- Saved library page: new "Posts & clips" section (full post cards with media, Open in Feed, and a working Saved/remove button) and new "Database entries" section; both count toward the library total, so the "Your library is currently empty" state only shows when the library is truly empty. Opening the library also merges the account's saved post ids into the on-device list, so Feed/Clip Save buttons on this device reflect saves made on other devices.
- Database Save button now writes through to the account (`setAccountSave` kind "db") and the Database merges account + device saves on open, pushing device-only saves up — same pattern as the main library store.
- Removed one dead constant (`FEED_POST_KINDS`, zero references) that the client typecheck flagged once the client changed.

Sandbox verification (actions, bot account): save another player's post → appears in `listSavedFeedPosts`; save own new post → appears (previously dropped); un-save → removed; `setAccountSave` kind "db" → returned by `getAccountSaves`. Probe post deleted afterwards; all probe rows live only in the sandbox copy.

## Feed + Clips instant loading fix (Nov 21, 2026)

Owner report, with screenshots of Clips stuck on "Loading clips…" and the Feed stuck on "Loading the feed…": "why does it appear so slow i want it directly there." Diagnosis from source:

- Server `listClips` re-validated EVERY clip against TikTok's live oEmbed endpoint on every single read (sequential chunks of parallel fetches, 4s timeout each), and `ensureTikTokClips` could add serial oEmbed calls on top — opening Clips waited on TikTok's servers, not on ViceSunshine's own database.
- Server `listFeed` loaded the whole feedPosts table TWICE per call, resolved each post's picture with a serial awaited blob lookup, and counted likes/comments/views/reports/reposts with a full-array `.filter()` per post (O(posts × everything)).
- Client: Feed and Clips queries had no `staleTime` and no `placeholderData`, so every tab/filter switch (For you/Following, source, sort) created a fresh query key with no cached data and blanked the page to a lone loading line; nothing prefetched either list, so the first tap on Feed/Clips always paid the full fetch.

Fix:
- `listClips` is now a pure database read: TikTok playability is validated when a clip is stored and pruned by `refreshTikTokClips` (unchanged); the client player fallback (credited link card, never a black box) is unchanged. `ensureTikTokClips` short-circuits when all curated seeds exist, throttles its network seeding attempt to once per 10 minutes per process, and checks missing seeds in parallel. Counts in both `listClips` and `listFeed` are built with one-pass Maps; `listFeed` reuses its first posts select for repost roots and resolves blob URLs in parallel.
- Client: the shell prefetches the default Feed query (`["feed", key, "All", "", "All", "Top", "foryou"]`) and the Clips query (`["clips", key]`) in the background as soon as the player's key is known, so both tabs usually open with content already cached. Feed, Clips and the Stories row use `staleTime: 60_000` + `placeholderData: keepPreviousData` — switching tabs/filters keeps the posts on screen while the new list loads behind them, and revisits within a minute are instant.
- The lone "Loading clips…" / "Loading the feed…" text lines are gone: a true cold load (no cached data at all) now shows content-shaped skeleton cards in place of the posts/clips, and the Feed's empty state no longer flashes during a load.

## Fewer recommendations + followed people first (Nov 21, 2026)

Owner direction: "make it less recomendation and when i follow someone they should appear next to mine first like priority." Diagnosis: `listStories` returned up to 24 people for the Feed Stories row — every followed person plus a long tail of recommended profiles — so the row read as a recommendation carousel; `getProfileSuggestions` returned up to 8 cards and padded any shortfall with pure-popularity profiles ("Popular in the community", no connection to the viewer at all).

Fix:
- `listStories` people: the followed people are now a guaranteed priority block — EVERY person the player follows comes first, right after "Your story", ranked among themselves by unseen → has story → score; recommendations follow only after all of them and are capped at 5 (same ranking). The 24-person mixed slice is gone.
- `getProfileSuggestions`: capped at 4 (was 8) and the popularity filler is removed — only profiles with a real stored connection to the viewer (same crew, followed by people they follow, posts they liked/commented on, same region, same platform) are ever suggested; a player with no such connection gets an empty list and the "Suggested for you" row renders nothing.
- Clips "Following" empty state: suggested creators trimmed from 6 to 3.

No schema change; ordering and caps only, all from the same real `profile_follows` / profile data as before.

## Search repair — Enter, taps, and the TikTok-style results tab (Nov 21, 2026)

Owner report: "the text bar is there but when i search nothing happens — when i press enter it should only show the search and related stuff — and when i try pressing on something search nothing happens", plus "for the community search when u search it should open a new tab like tiktok with all related things videos, clips etc."

Diagnosis, from driving the rendered page in the audit probe (the previous round had only tested the server actions):

- The GLOBAL search overlay was actually wired: typing showed suggestions, Enter opened the full results page (77 results for "lucia"), and tapping a Videos result opened Clips. Server `globalSearch` returned 22 grouped hits for "lucia" in 4-per-group mode.
- The COMMUNITY search (the Feed / Clips top-bar field, `SocialTopBar`) was the dead one: the input was not inside a form and had no submit path at all, so pressing Enter did literally nothing; tapping a suggestion only rewrote the filter text in place. Its live results/suggestions in the overlay also sat below the fold of the half-screen panel, and a suggestion/trending tap there only changed the text in the box — every one of these read as "nothing happens".
- Clips ignored the shell search entirely: tapping a Videos result in search landed on an unfiltered reel (Feed/News/Bugs/Guides all receive the shell search; Clips never did).

Fix (client only, `client/src/App.tsx`; no schema or action change):

- `SocialTopBar` takes a new `onSearchSubmit` prop. The field is now a real `<form>`: Enter or the new Search button opens the dedicated Search results tab (`searchresults`) for the typed words, and tapping a suggestion completes the words AND runs the search into the same tab. Typing still filters the current view live, as before. Feed and Clips both pass `go("searchresults", q)`.
- The results tab is now TikTok-style: it opens on All (Users, Videos, News, Feed, Events, Bugs, Guides, Crews, Database together) with a chip row — All plus one chip per kind that has results, with counts — narrowing to a single kind; the filter resets when the words change.
- Global overlay: tapping a suggestion or a Most searched term now runs that search into the full results view (records the search + saves history, same as Enter), and the "See all results" button sits directly under the suggestions instead of below every result group at the bottom of the panel.
- Videos result taps (overlay + results tab) now open the Feed filtered to the search instead of Clips: the search Videos group is Feed posts (YouTube / native video), while Clips is TikTok + uploads only — the old routing landed on an unfiltered reel, and filtering Clips by the words dead-ended on "No clips match" for videos that were never clips (verified live in the probe: "lucia" → 20 Videos results, none of them Clips). Clips deliberately keeps only its own in-view search; its box still opens the results tab on Enter / suggestion tap.

Known remaining gap (separate queued fix, not this round): tapping a Users result still routes through the own-profile path — the profile-visit fix covers search results as one of its entry points.

## Guest names at sign-up + unique names and usernames (Nov 21, 2026)

Owner direction (verbatim): "and for every user when the account is created name them gues3434 idk so they dont have the same name / no one can have the saem name / which include both name adn usernmae".

What shipped (server, `server/src/actions.ts`; no schema change — `profiles.display_name` already exists):

- Every account-creation path now assigns an automatic guest display name: `guest` + random digits (guest3434 style, 4 digits first, widening to 5–8 digits on collision, checked against every existing display name AND username, so a generated name is always one nobody else holds). Paths covered: `createAccount` (including finishing a pre-password account that had no name), `saveProfile` create branch, `updateProfile` create branch (when no name is entered), and `bootstrapEditorAccount`.
- The guest name is a placeholder, not an entered name: it never starts the display-name lock clock (`nameChangeGate` treats a guest current name like an empty one; `displayNameLockedUntil` returns "" for guest names), so the member's first real name entry is always free and is the entry that locks.
- Display-name uniqueness is now enforced server-side in both name-save paths (`updateProfile`, `saveSettings`) via `assertDisplayNameAvailable`: trimmed, case-insensitive; a name is refused when another account holds it as a display name OR as a username, with the message "That name is already taken — no two ViceSunshine members can have the same name, so pick another one." Keeping your own current name always passes (same self exception as usernames).
- Username uniqueness (already enforced) now also cross-checks display names: a new username can't be another member's display name, in `assertUsernameAvailable` and the live `checkUsername` sign-up check.
- A save never leaves an account nameless: an empty name in Edit profile / Settings keeps the existing guest name or mints a fresh one.
- Client copy updated in Edit profile and Settings: every account starts with its own guest name (like guest3434); names are unique across both name and username.

Scope note: the guest assignment applies at account creation going forward; existing accounts that already have a name keep it, and existing accounts with no name receive a guest name the next time they finish sign-up or save Edit profile / Settings with an empty name.

## Edit profile visibility switches — Birth date & Location (Nov 21, 2026)

Owner direction (verbatim): "give an extra buttom put on birthdate, location and bio." / "it should be like a switch buttom" / "or actually not for bio". Working interpretation (told to the owner): each switch controls whether that field shows on the public profile. Bio deliberately gets NO switch.

What shipped:

- Schema (migration 0066): `profiles.show_dob` (integer, default 0) and `profiles.show_location` (integer, default 1). Defaults preserve prior behaviour exactly: birth dates were never public (switch starts OFF), a set location already showed publicly (switch starts ON).
- Server (`server/src/actions.ts`): `updateProfile` accepts optional `showDob` / `showLocation` booleans — only written when sent, so other saves never reset a switch. `getProfile` returns both switches to the owner. `getSocialProfile` returns `dob` only when the owner's switch is on (and the profile isn't privacy-locked for the viewer; the owner always gets their own), and blanks `location` for non-owners when its switch is off.
- Client (`client/src/App.tsx`): a `VisibilitySwitch` component (real button, `role="switch"`, Vice pink when on) sits on the Location field and inside the Birth date editor in Edit profile; both save with the sheet's one Save via `updateProfile`. Profiles render a "Born <date>" line under the bio (cake icon, same shape as the location line) whenever a birth date is visible to the viewer; your own profile header honours your own switches so you see exactly what visitors see.

## Saved page: fade hero, press-again-to-unsave, live empty state (Nov 21, 2026)

- Ben, on the Saved page: "make it more aesthetic and nice looking like design maybe like fade away picture from top to down" / "make it available to be able to unsave when u press on save again" / "notice how it says empty so make sure it changes base of what i do".
- Design (client only, `SavedView` + `.saved-fade-mask` in theme.css): the framed empty-state-only hero card is gone. The existing `saved-hero.webp` sunset artwork (no new source) now sits at the very top of the Saved page in BOTH states and dissolves downward into the page background via a CSS mask gradient (full strength at top → transparent at ~96% height); the neon tint and a dark legibility shade share the same mask so all layers fade together. The "Saved" display heading and subtitle sit over the lower fade.
- Live state wording: the hero text is driven entirely by the live library lists — total === 0 shows "Your library is currently empty" + copy + Browse news/guides/crews; total > 0 shows the real count, one chip per type present (Posts & clips / News / Guides / Bugs to watch / Crews / Events / Database entries / Easter eggs & ideas), and the "press Saved on any item to take it back out" hint. The two account-backed queries (`listSavedFeedPosts`, `getAccountSaves`) now use `staleTime: 0` so opening Saved never shows a cached empty state after saving elsewhere.
- Unsave reliability: `SavedView`'s `unsavePost` / `unsaveDb` now AWAIT the server removal (`setFeedSave` / `setAccountSave` with saved:false) before invalidating the library query — previously the delete and the refetch fired together and the refetch could win the race, making the item look like it refused to unsave. The shared `SaveButton` now shows its toggle state (filled bookmark + teal lit style when saved, matching the Feed/Clips bookmark), so press-again-to-unsave is visible on every tab that uses it. No schema, migration or server changes.


## Profile visits actually open the other player (Nov 21, 2026)

Owner report (verbatim): "make the profile visit actually possible for instance when i press a bot profile rn it bring me back to my profile instead so i can still not go on a different person profile". Root causes found: search results navigated to the Profile tab carrying only the search words (which the Profile page never resolved, so it showed the viewer's own profile), and Feed post authors, commenters, Clips creators and Saved post authors were not pressable at all.

What shipped:

- Server (`server/src/actions.ts`): `globalSearch` player hits now carry the tapped player's public gamertag in `extra` (`refKey` stays empty — another player's key is never handed out by search); the client resolves it through the existing `findPlayerByGamertag` hand-off (`vv-player-link`), the same one shared profile links use. `listFeed` items and their `repostOf`, `listClips` items and `listFeedComments` items now return `authorTag` — the author's PUBLIC gamertag only ("" for automatic / example / TikTok rows, which have no ViceSunshine profile). A playerKey is that account's device credential, so it is never added to a content response (the audit's security review caught an earlier draft of this fix doing exactly that via `authorKey`; the shipped version carries gamertags only, resolved server-side). No schema or migration changes.
- Client (`client/src/App.tsx`): content surfaces open players by gamertag — the shell's `openPlayerByGamertag(tag)` writes `vv-player-link` and goes to Profile; inside the Profile page, `viewProfileByGamertag` resolves the tag through `findPlayerByGamertag` and switches the view. Search overlay and full search results open the exact tapped player. Feed post avatars/names, repost original authors, comment avatars/names (Feed, Clips and profile post sheets), native Clips creators, Saved post authors, profile post-sheet authors and story-viewer authors are now real buttons with accessible names that open that player's profile. Automatic posts, community examples and TikTok clips have no profile behind them and stay unlinked. The older key-based hand-off (`vv-open-player`) remains only on the surfaces that already used it (stories, suggestions, notifications, Messages).
- Also fixed en route: an unused `label` parameter in the server's `nameChangeGate` helper (renamed `_label`) that the typecheck flags.
- Verified on the sandbox database: `globalSearch("Jamal")` → player hit `extra: "JamalHaddad"` → `findPlayerByGamertag` → `bot-jamalhaddad` → `getSocialProfile` returns that bot's real profile (`found`, `isMe: false`). `listFeed` returns `authorTag: "JamalHaddad"` on the bot's reposted post and "" on automatic posts, with no `authorKey` in the response; `listClips` returns "" on TikTok clips; `listFeedComments` returns the commenter's gamertag.

- Removed from the panel: the Status pills + description, the Plan section (Free plan / Premium cards, wallet strip, Upgrade / cancel-Premium controls — Premium management remains in Premium & wallet), Redeem invite code, Refer a Friend, the Profile & account details form, the Account & security email/phone/verify fields, the Language row, the Appearance section, and the password-unlock gate card (it only guarded the removed details; changing a password still requires the current password inside the form). The left Settings list is unchanged, and Your account no longer shows the shared "Save settings" bar (nothing in the panel is saved through it any more).

Settings form state still hydrates and saves for the other sections (Connected accounts, Privacy, Notifications) exactly as before.

## Notifications tab replaces the top-bar Like/heart (Nov 21, 2026)

Owner direction (verbatim): "so instead of having the like option i want notification which should open a new tab like this" — instead of the Like (heart) option in the top bar, a Notifications bell that opens a new tab like Instagram's notifications page: filter chips at the top (All / People you follow / Comments / Follows), a Follow requests row at the top, then notifications grouped by Last 7 days / Last 30 days / Older (new followers, likes, comments, follow requests with Confirm/Delete, Message / Follow back actions). This is the surface where private-account follow requests land too.

What shipped:

- Client (`client/src/App.tsx`): new Tab `"notifications"` + `NotificationsView`. Header with Back (shell `goBack`) + "Notifications" title; filter chips All / People you follow / Comments / Follows (`People you follow` = activity whose actor the viewer follows, via the new `followedByMe` flag; Comments = comment kind only; Follows = follow + follow_request kinds); a Follow requests row at the top (first requester's avatar or a person-plus icon, pending-count badge, "N people asked to follow you" / "No pending requests", expands to the full pending list with Confirm / Delete per person); then groups Last 7 days / Last 30 days / Older. Rows: avatar + name + verb ("started following you." / "requested to follow you." / "liked your story." / "liked your post." / "commented on your post:" + quoted comment / "reposted your post.") + time + @gamertag; follow rows carry Follow back (when the viewer does not follow the actor) or Message (when they do — sets the `vv-dm-open` hand-off and goes to Messages); follow-request rows carry Confirm / Delete; post like/comment/repost rows show the post's own image thumbnail when it is a direct image URL. Opening the tab stamps `vv-activity-seen`, clearing the bell badge. Guests get an honest create-account state; per-filter empty states are honest (no invented rows). The old Feed repost Notifications card (`listNotifications`) is untouched.
- Top bar (`SocialTopBar`, shared by Feed + Clips): the heart icon and its slide-over panel are gone; a bell icon with the same unread badge now calls `go("notifications")`. Search and Messages icons unchanged; the centred VS logo / smaller phone icons on the Feed are unchanged. Settings → Messaging copy now points to the Notifications tab with a working "Open Notifications" button.
- Server (`server/src/actions.ts`): `listActivityNotifications` now also returns pending follow requests (`follow_request` kind, from the real `follow_requests` rows for this player), plus `actorGamertag`, `followedByMe`, `postId` and `postMediaUrl` per item, and the limit rose 30 → 100 so the 30-day/Older groups are real. Actors are now addressed by their one-way PUBLIC profile ID (`publicProfileId`), never their secret account key — profile opening and `setFollow` / DM actions already resolve public IDs server-side. `listFollowRequests` likewise returns public IDs, and `respondToFollowRequest` resolves a public ID back to the requester server-side (Confirm = accept, Delete = decline; same one-way follow semantics as before). No schema or migration change — all data already existed (`follow_events`, `follow_requests`, `story_likes`, `feed_reactions`, `feed_comments`, `feed_reposts`, `profile_follows`).
- Live: `activity-notifications` and `follow-requests` added to the shell's `LIVE_QUERY_KEYS`, so the tab and the bell badge refresh with the rest of the live surfaces.

Both surfaces are now FULL PAGES opening from the top — no bottom sheets: the profile's Highlights row opens "Your story history" as a full page (back chevron, sticky header), and its new "Add to highlights" option at the top opens the picker as a full page over it.
- Picker (`HighlightEditorSheet` in `client/src/App.tsx`): one grid of the owner's own stories from `listStoryArchive` (already newest-first server-side — most recent at the top, scrolling down to the oldest; live and archived alike).
