## Highlights — select on the Story history page, straight to New highlight (edit, Nov 21, 2026)

Owner direction (verbatim): "so instead of pressing add to hoghlight and selecht we should be able to selcht here and go directly to the edit cover blah vlah.. and note u can selecht mutiple at a time for highlights."

What shipped (client only, `client/src/App.tsx`; no server, schema, or migration change):

- The "Add to highlights" button at the top of the Story history page is GONE. Selection now happens on the history page itself: every "Your stories" tile carries a selection circle (top-right — empty white ring until picked, then the accent disc showing the pick number; tap order = play order, max 30, same cap as the editor) and tapping a tile picks/unpicks it, with an accent outline when picked. A hint line under "Your stories" explains the flow. Tapping a history tile no longer plays the archive in the viewer (`playFromArchive` removed) — highlights above the grid still tap-to-play as before.
- While ≥1 story is picked, a bar pinned to the bottom of the page (z-89, above the z-88 history page, below the z-90 editor) shows "N stories selected", a Clear button (unpicks all), and a "New highlight" button. Pressing it opens `HighlightEditorSheet` with the new optional `initialPicked` prop: for a new highlight the editor initialises `picked` from it and starts directly on the New highlight (cover) step — cover circle + Edit cover + name field + blue check — with no picker step first. The pick step still exists inside the editor behind the New highlight screen's back arrow, so the selection can be adjusted before saving; its "Add to highlights" button returns to the cover step.
- The zero-pick protections are structural and unchanged in effect: the bar only renders while something is picked (with no stories there are no tiles and no bar at all), the editor hand-off filters picked ids against the loaded archive, and the editor's rendered-step gate (`stepWanted === "cover" && picked.length === 0 ? "pick" : stepWanted`) still makes the New highlight screen unreachable with zero stories picked. Saving clears the history-page selection; closing the editor without saving keeps it.

## Profile Photos / Clips Upload opens the creator's picker, media-restricted (edit, Nov 21 2026)

Owner direction (verbatim): "pressing on Upload on Photos or Clips on the profile currently just brings you to the Feed; it should open the creator's picker — the tab the + button opens — straight to the upload/picker section, NOT the camera/taking-a-picture section; the post upload picker can only select photos, including Live Photos; the clip upload picker can only upload short clips and videos."

What shipped (client only, `client/src/App.tsx`; no server, schema or migration change):

- The `vv-open-create` hand-off value "upload" is split in two: the Photos tab's Upload writes "upload-photos", the Clips tab's Upload writes "upload-clips" (`openShareUpload` in the Profile; "share" and "story" unchanged).
- FeedView maps them to the creator's new `initialPicker` prop ("photos" / "clips") and dest (post / clip). The old `autoUpload` prop and its programmatic click on the camera screen's gallery label are gone — that auto-click was why Upload appeared to just land on the Feed/camera.
- FeedCreator initialises its picker state open from `initialPicker`: "photos" → the "New post" picker, "clips" → a new "New clip" picker (same picker screen, clip title, Next → preview, dest forced to Clips). The camera stage is not rendered while a picker is open, and the camera effect now also stays off while `picker` is set, so an Upload-opened creator never starts the camera behind the picker; closing the picker (X or the Camera tile) starts it as before.
- Media restriction is per session (`uploadKind`, fixed at open): every file input in the creator (picker library input, camera-screen gallery input, story Collage input) takes `pickAccept` — photos session: `image/png,image/jpeg,image/webp,image/gif` only (a Live Photo picks as its still image, so Live Photos are included); clips session: `video/mp4,video/webm` only; unrestricted sessions keep the previous combined list (which matches exactly what `uploadFeedMedia` accepts server-side). `onFiles` enforces the same split as a guarantee behind the accept lists (wrong-kind picks are dropped with an explanatory error), and a clips session also rejects videos over 60 seconds at pick time (the publish-time 60s rule for Clips/Stories already existed in `tryPublish`).
- Picker copy follows the restriction: Select / Your library labels and the empty-grid hint say photos-only (Live Photos included) or videos-only (up to 60 seconds); the Photos/Clips empty-state Upload buttons' aria-labels name the picker they open.

## Story viewer — logo, trash can, like/reply only on others' stories, 48-hour stats, replies to Messages (Nov 21, 2026)

Owner direction (verbatim): "Like this but with a logo on the left corner and trash can to the right. Also a like button and reply buttom bot for me (but to comment on other people stories, and like so these shouldn't show up on my own so I could only see who viewed and liked in the 48 hrs and after that it will disappear unless they liked your story like shown. And your the only one can see those stats on your own post and when one reply to your story it will go to your messages instead." Reference screenshots: an Instagram-style story viewer, and the own-story Likes screen (likers with red heart badges, trash can top right, footer "Viewer lists and view counts aren't available after 48 hours.").

What shipped:

- Client (`StoryViewer` in `client/src/App.tsx`): the VS logo (`vs-logo.webp`, natural wide aspect) now sits in the header's LEFT corner ahead of the creator avatar/name; a trash-can button sits on the RIGHT (before Close) on the viewer's OWN stories only, opening a confirm card — Delete calls the new `deleteStory` action and closes the viewer (stories row / archive / highlights invalidated). The bottom like (heart) button and reply box now render ONLY on other people's stories — on your own story that row is gone entirely; your own story keeps only the private stats button/panel. The stats button shows "Seen by N" inside 48 hours and "N likes" after. The insights panel is reworked to the reference: a Likes list first (avatar + red heart badge + name + time — stays visible for good), then a Viewers list inside the window (heart badge on viewers who also liked), and past 48 hours the footer line "Viewer lists and view counts aren't available after 48 hours." plus "Replies to your story land in your Messages." The old in-panel Replies section is removed. Story-history (archive) tiles show the view count only inside the 48-hour window; likes always show.
- Server (`server/src/actions.ts`): new `deleteStory` (author-only; removes the story plus its views/likes/replies rows and any highlight items pointing at it). `replyToStory` no longer stores a story_replies row — it delivers the reply to the story author as a `direct_messages` DM from the replier, body prefixed "Replied to your story:", so it lands in the author's Messages thread (with the usual unread state); it also refuses replies to your own story and replies from someone who blocked the author. `getStoryInsights` is author-only as before and now enforces the 48-hour rule: `statsOpen` flag; inside the window it returns the full viewer list (each viewer flagged `liked`) and the real view count; past it, views = 0 and the viewers list holds only people who also liked, while the new `likers` list (gamertag, avatar, likedAt) is always returned. `viewStory` returns the view count to the author only inside the same 48-hour window. `listStoryArchive` zeroes `views` and flags `statsOpen: false` for stories older than 48 hours (likes untouched). No schema or migration change — `stories`, `story_views`, `story_likes`, `direct_messages` already existed; `story_replies` rows from before this change stay stored but are no longer written or shown.

## Performance pass — slow pictures + site-wide lag (Nov 21, 2026)

Owner direction (verbatim): "so evrything nice when i press on my ptofile i=or on the feed circle to post a story one issue is it takes really long for the picture to laod so make sure whole websites stays quality no bugs or lags. Pictures take really long to load — fix that, and make the whole website stay quality, no bugs or lags. Find what is making pictures load slowly and what is making the site lag, and fix it site-wide (phone and PC)."

Diagnosis (measured in the source, not guessed):

- User photos were uploaded exactly as shot (phone originals, up to the 4 MB avatar/banner cap and ~12 MB feed-media cap), stored at full resolution, and re-downloaded at full size by every screen showing them — profile picture, story ball/viewer, feed, highlights. That round-trip was the slow picture on the profile and in the story flow.
- The story flow additionally waited for the full upload to finish and then re-downloaded the stored file before showing the preview at all.
- All 63 bundled artwork files were 2352–2736px originals totalling ~36 MB (250–800 KB each), served for slots mostly far smaller.
- Most `<img>` elements decoded on the main thread synchronously; several below-the-fold card images were eager.
- The app shell's social-profile poll ran every 12s and the profile page poll likewise, re-rendering whole screens as a backstop to invalidations that already fire instantly; the server-status poll ran every 15s against hourly pipeline stamps.
- The Database "All entries" list paints hundreds of cards at once with no off-screen skipping.

What shipped (client only — `client/src/App.tsx`, `client/src/theme.css`, and the bundled art files; no server, schema, or migration change):

- New `optimizedPhotoUpload` / `downscaleImageBlob` helpers: every photo upload is resized on the device before upload — longest edge capped at 512px (avatar), 1200px (highlight cover), 1600px (banner, event picture, feed/story/creator photos) — and re-encoded compactly (JPEG 0.85 for opaque photos; a 32×32 alpha probe keeps genuinely transparent pictures as PNG, so transparency is never flattened onto a matte). Typical phone photo: ~150–450 KB stored instead of 3–12 MB, so it uploads faster and loads faster everywhere afterwards. Non-images, GIFs, undecodable files and already-small pictures pass through untouched; any optimizer failure falls back to the original file. Wired into all six upload sites: creator `uploadOne`, `setEventImage`, story `uploadFeedMedia`, `uploadHighlightCover`, `uploadCustomAvatar`, `uploadCustomBanner`.
- Self-heal for photos stored BEFORE this pass (`optimizedStoredPhoto` + a one-time effect in ProfileView): the first time the owner opens their own profile, an oversized stored avatar (cap 512px) or banner picture (cap 1600px) is quietly re-saved at the optimized size through the normal upload actions — same picture, same server safety check, much smaller file — so the already-uploaded profile picture Ben reported is fixed too, not just future uploads. Each URL is attempted at most once per session (module-level set), a picture already within cap and ≤400 KB is left completely alone, and any failure silently leaves the current picture untouched. Other members' older feed/story photos are not rewritten (only the owner can re-save their own media; stories expire within 24h regardless).
- Story "Add to story" flow: a fresh phone/camera pick now previews INSTANTLY from the device file (local object URL) while the downscaled upload runs in the background; Share waits for that upload and posts the stored URL — the local URL is never posted. Album picks (already stored URLs) are unchanged.
- Bundled art recompressed in place (GraphicsMagick, max width 1600, WebP quality 82, original kept whenever the result was not smaller): the 63 imported files went from ~36 MB total to ~11 MB (e.g. home hero 656 KB → 185 KB). Spot-checked visually — same artwork, no visible quality loss at display sizes. The un-imported `media-generation-*` originals in the assets folder were left alone (they never ship).
- Every `<img>` now carries `decoding="async"` (77 tags updated; map tiles already had it), and the remaining eager below-the-fold card images (guide/code/event/clan images, event card headers) got `loading="lazy"`. Heroes, the story viewer, the creator/story previews and the profile banner stay eager on purpose — they are the first thing on their screen.
- Poll backstops relaxed: shell social-profile 12s → 30s, profile-page social poll 12s → 30s, server-status 15s → 30s. Mutations still invalidate instantly, so nothing the user does waits on a poll.
- New `.cv-skip` utility (`content-visibility: auto`) applied to the Database entry-list card button, so off-screen cards in the hundreds-long lists skip layout/paint; applied to the inner button so the card's hover shadow is never clipped.

Deliberately NOT changed: the `.page-enter` keyframes animate padding-top on purpose (a transform would create a containing block and break the fixed sheets/viewers inside pages — documented at the keyframes); the 30s live-data invalidation timers (that is the site's live behaviour, not lag); server-stored Database entry pictures (generated art blobs — resizing those belongs to the artwork generation pipeline, and list thumbnails already lazy-load).

## Profile taps opened the viewer's own profile — fixed (Nov 21, 2026)

Owner report (verbatim): "theres a bug again i cannot press on other people profiles when i do i just end up on my ownn."

Root cause (confirmed by probing the live actions, not guessed): profiles were opened by GAMERTAG. Most surfaces handed the tapped person's gamertag to the profile page, which resolved it with `findPlayerByGamertag` — first profile whose gamertag matches. Gamertags are NOT unique in the data: ~40 guest/editor accounts share the gamertag "ViceSunshineEditor" (the `bootstrapEditorAccount` fallback), so any tap on a person carrying a shared gamertag resolved to the FIRST matching account — for the owner, his own profile. Surfaces that already passed the exact account key (story row people list, messages, follow lists) worked, which is why the bug looked intermittent.

Fix: every profile open now uses the person's PUBLIC profile ID (`pub_` + first 40 hex of SHA-256 of `vs-public-profile:<playerKey>` — the same one-way ID `getSocialProfile` already exposed). It is unique per account and is not the raw `playerKey` credential. The gamertag hand-off remains only as a fallback for hits with no ID and for external `#player=` shared links.

- Server (`server/src/actions.ts`): new `publicProfileIdMap` helper. `listFeed` items and `repostOf` now carry `authorId`; `listFeedComments` items carry `authorId`; `listClips` items carry `authorId` (empty for TikTok clips, whose authors have no account here); `globalSearch` player hits now return the public ID in `refKey` (gamertag stays in `extra` as the fallback); `profilePostItems` returns the public ID as `authorKey` for other people's posts (the viewer's own posts keep their own key; auto/example posts get ""); `listStories` groups/people now carry public IDs instead of raw keys (the viewer's OWN story group keeps their raw key, which the profile page matches on). This also stops `listStories`/`profilePostItems` from handing out other accounts' raw player keys.
- Client (`client/src/App.tsx`): Search overlay and full search results prefer `hit.refKey` (`onOpenPlayerId`); Feed post cards, repost attributions, comments (feed, clips and profile post sheets), Saved posts and Clips authors all prefer the new ID fields (`onOpenAuthorId` / `onOpenAuthorKey`) and fall back to the gamertag only when no ID exists.

Verified: clean build; sandbox action probes showed two accounts sharing gamertag "ViceSunshineEditor" (guest2107, guest4057) now return distinct public IDs that each resolve to the right person, feed posts by "ViceSunshineEditor" accounts carry distinct `authorId`s, and comments/search carry the commenter's/search hit's own ID. Audit probe tap-tests (signed in as a probe account): guest4057's story circle → guest4057's profile; the duplicate-gamertag repost author's name in the Feed → that author's own profile (Follow/Block/Report, not the viewer's own-profile controls); commenter BackwaterBob_'s name → his profile; the guest4057 search result → guest4057's profile.

## Weapons pictures blank on the Database list — fixed (Nov 21, 2026)

Owner report (verbatim): "and where did all my pictures go from the weapons" — with a laptop screenshot of Database → Weapons (556, ADB FRA, Advanced Rifle, Assault Rifle, Bullpup Rifle, Carbine, Carbine Rifle Mk II, Hillinger Heathen, Kozlov Hind) where every card's picture area is blank/dark: name, category pill and status badge render, the picture does not. Nothing was deleted: probing `listDbEntries` on a copy of the live data returns a picture URL for every weapon, and in a driven browser the same Weapons page renders the rifles — the failure is in how a picture that fails to load is handled, not in the data.

Root cause (two halves, both fixed):
- Server: `listDbEntries` handed out `ctx.blobs.getUrl(key)` for any non-empty `image_blob_key`. `getUrl` builds a URL whether or not the picture file exists, and every painter (`seedDbArt`, the scheduled `seedArtwork`) treated a non-empty key as "has a picture". An entry whose recorded key points at a picture file that is not actually stored therefore served a dead URL forever and never re-entered any painting queue — a permanently blank card.
- Client: every Database `<img>` had no error handling, so a failed picture load rendered as an empty dark box; the studio-tile fallback only appeared when the URL was empty.

Fix:
- Server (`server/src/actions.ts`): new `dbEntryHasPicture` helper — an entry only counts as having a picture when `ctx.blobs.head(key)` finds the file. `listDbEntries` returns an empty `imageUrl` for a missing file (client shows the studio tile and the existing self-heal trigger re-queues the entry); `seedDbArt` and `seedArtwork` now count key-present-but-file-missing entries as missing, so the scheduled painter repaints them (weapons first, as before) with no manual step.
- Client (`client/src/App.tsx`): new `DbImg` component used by every Database picture (grid cards, list rows, detail hero, gallery, variants, More-like-this, Featured/Recently Updated thumbs, Saved vehicle carousel). On image error it swaps in the entry's `DbArtTile` studio tile for that URL; a repaint's fresh URL is tried again automatically. No card can sit blank again.

## Map matched to the official Leonida poster — look, furniture, names (Nov 21, 2026)

Owner direction (verbatim, Nov 21 2026 09:50–10:27 MST, corrected 10:28 MST): "I need my map exactly like this identical" / "pixel for pixel" / "We don't wnat to put fake info" / "And keep my features" / "I need every detail" — then the correction: "it shoudlntbe a picture but ir shoudl look like identically to the pic and with my features". So: Rockstar's poster picture file is NOT used as the map and is NOT sliced into tiles. The map stays the real interactive tiled map on the licensed YANIS V18 base (GTADB CC BY 4.0 via GTAMap.io), rebuilt to LOOK identical to the official poster, with every existing feature kept (left panel + search, Show all / Close all, 1,020 schematic pins, smooth zoom, Day/Night, Save-to-profile with the guest Log in / Sign up popup, notes/measure tools).

Reference studied side-by-side (look only — no image bytes copied into the artifact):
- https://gta6news.gg/gta-6-map-released/ — official-map breakdown: five counties with their places (Lummox: Mount Kalaga National Park, Bonanza, Grayson, Moreau, MacAlister Gold Mine, Tallaloko Lakeside Resort; Kelly: Port Gellhorn, Gellhorn Raceway, Leonida State Prison, Buckskin Swamp, Emerald Springs, Frogswater; Leonard: Ambrosia, Allied Crystal, Belville, Lake Leonida, Waning Sands, Richard Barebone State Park; Vice-Dale: Vice City, Vice Beach, VCIA, Little Cuba, Downtown, Ocean Beach, Port Vice City, Stockyard, Southside; Mariana: Grassrivers, Watson Bay, Pelican Valley), the Keys chain (Key Lento, Shoeless Keys, Maroon Key, Abismo Key, Permit Keys, Southwind, Goose Key, Mile Zero Air Station), roads (Interstates 97/297/397/404, US 35/82/183/628, State Route 1A, County Route 62), water (Gulf of Leonida, Atlantic Ocean, Leonida Straits), and the poster's furniture layout (VISIT LEONIDA legend top-left, star compass over the Gulf mid-left, Great Seal + "THE GREAT STATE OF LEONIDA" bottom-right, coordinate grid, white sentence-case place labels, big spaced-capital county labels).
- Poster file itself (reference only): https://gta6news.gg/wp-content/uploads/2026/11/gta-6-official-map-leonida-2600.webp and the original free download at https://www.rockstargames.com/VI/media ("Leonida Map & Cover Art Poster").

What shipped (client only — `client/src/App.tsx`; no server, schema or migration change):
- Colour grade retuned with the exact CSS filter + "hue" blend math run offline over real GTAMap tiles and rendered to test mosaics: day chain is now hue-rotate(-14deg) saturate(1.45) brightness(1.2) with the #2eb357 hue wash at 0.25 — the graded sea samples #0093ac (poster's bright cyan family, vs the old dark #067f84) and the land reads poster green without blowing the pale city fabric out (higher-brightness chains were tested and rejected for exactly that blow-out). Stage surround colours recomputed through the same math: SEA_SURROUND_DAY #0093ac, SEA_NIGHT #004b5e.
- Poster furniture drawn as ViceSunshine's own original SVG line work in the poster's spots and style, printed ON the map (pans/zooms with the geography, follows the Labels toggle): VISIT LEONIDA legend box top-left over the sea (Interstate / U.S. Route / State Route / County Route / Airport / City·Town rows + a scale bar, footed "Fan-made recreation in the official poster's style — not the official poster"), an 8-point star compass rose over the Gulf mid-left (replaces the old screen-fixed compass), and a circular Great Seal (own sun/palm/waves motif, "THE GREAT SEAL OF THE STATE OF LEONIDA" around the rim) with THE GREAT STATE OF / LEONIDA over the sea at the bottom-right. Rockstar's seal artwork, legend artwork and poster image are NOT reproduced.
- The poster's coordinate grid now ships ON by default (broad ~10-across squares, panel Grid tool still toggles it; a saved profile setup still overrides).
- Place labels restyled to the poster's type: white sentence case semibold (counties keep the big spaced capitals). Eighteen more real poster names added as overlay labels, each placed by its reported county: MacAlister Gold Mine, Tallaloko Lakeside Resort, Richard Barebone State Park, Allied Crystal, Leonida State Prison, Gellhorn Raceway, Pelican Valley, Hamlet Correctional Institution, Shoeless Keys, Maroon Key, Abismo Key, Permit Keys, Downtown, Ocean Beach, Little Cuba, Port Vice City, Stockyard, Southside, plus the Leonida Straits sea label. Henry J Edwards Airport and Wild Hog Airfield are deliberately NOT placed — the source names them with no location, and no spot is guessed.
- Credits updated everywhere the map is credited: the notice pill, the panel footer and the under-map credit line now credit Rockstar Games' official Leonida poster as the source of the look and names (with the rockstargames.com/VI/media link), and state the poster artwork itself is © Rockstar Games and not reproduced. Pins and locations stay labelled fan-made / unverified / schematic.

Honest limits (unchanged from the previous map builds): the base geography is the licensed YANIS V18 community reconstruction, not Rockstar's poster geography, so labels anchor to the community landmass in the poster's arrangement rather than the poster's literal pixels; the poster's printed road shields and fine print come from the base tiles at high zoom, not from overlay art.

## Map base — GTABase-identical in-game tile set (Nov 21 2026, "identical" build)

Finding (researched live, Nov 21 2026): GTABase's GTA 6 map (https://www.gtabase.com/gta-6/map/) defaults to an "in-game" style tile set hosted at `https://www.gtabase.com/images/gta-6/maps/tiles/in-game/{z}/{z},{y},{x}.jpg` (Leaflet, CRS.Simple transformation 1/32,512; config in https://www.gtabase.com/images/gta-6/maps/gtab-map/map.js). That tile set is the GTA VI Mapping Community's own in-game style, also served by the community CDN GTAMap.io at `https://tiles.gtamap.io/gta6/ingame-day/{z}/{x}/{y}.webp` (style config embedded in https://gtamap.io/ — frame "ingame": nativeZoom 7, tileSize 512, offset [8704,8704], image 34304x44544, background #2f90b0, gameToPixel a=2 c=24064 e=-2 f=24064; the "community" frame is the old v18 set: nativeZoom 8, offset [21504,21504], gameToPixel a=4 c=44032). Verified identical imagery: GTABase tile in-game/3/3,15,10.jpg vs the geographically matching quadrant of GTAMap.io ingame-day/4/5/7.webp — mean absolute channel difference 3.9/255 (JPEG-vs-WebP compression noise only). Dominant sea colour of both: #2f90b0 (GTABase live-page samples #2d90af after JPEG recompression).

Change made: TILE_BASE switched from `https://tiles.gtamap.io/gta6/community-v18-4x` to `https://tiles.gtamap.io/gta6/ingame-day`. The two frames share the game-coordinate grid at exactly half scale, so tile (z,x,y) covers identical ground in both — all pins, labels, crops and pan/zoom math are untouched. Tile zoom clamped to the in-game pyramid's native z7 (deeper zooms overzoom, as GTAMap.io does). The CSS colour grade, green hue wash and screen wash were REMOVED: the palette (flat dusty blue-teal #2f90b0 sea, darker shelf halo #2883a0, sky-blue inland water, textured sage land, grey-white urban, cream sand) is baked into the tiles. SEA_DAY / SEA_SURROUND_DAY = #2f90b0; Night keeps a plain darkening filter only. No flat picture was swapped in; the map is still a real interactive tile pyramid. GTABase's own tile files are NOT hotlinked or copied — the same community tiles are loaded from the community's own CDN (CC BY 4.0, credited in the panel footer and under the map).

Labels: re-anchored to the community label data's published game coordinates (GTAMap.io map config "places", converted game metres -> map-%): rank-1 headline labels Vice City (69.9,49.8), Leonida Keys (49.3,80.9), Grassrivers (50.8,70.4), Port Gellhorn (35.5,36.6), Ambrosia (56.2,35.0), Mount Kalaga National Park (53.6,24.2); tiered minor labels (Lake Leonida, Port Vice City, VCIA, Goose Key, Key Lento, Vice Beach, Ocean Beach, Watson Bay, Capri, Mile Zero Air Station, Pelican Valley, Moreau, Millport, Draper Island, Waning Sands) reveal at zoom tiers 1.5-2.8 like GTABase's minzoom label reveal; sea labels Gulf of Leonida (25.0,45.3), Atlantic Ocean (83.8,60.3), Leonida Straits (57.8,87.1). Poster-reported extras (Hamlet, Belville, etc.) kept at a deep tier 3.4 with existing placements; "Gellhorn Raceway" renamed "Gellhorn International Raceway" per the owner's GTABase label list. The airport label reads "VC International Airport" (the brief's GTABase label text) at the community label spot. Catalan Key (71.5,61.2), Gloriana Key (76.4,59.3), Dalton Island (82.9,58.1) and Thrillbilly Mud Club (49.0,33.4) ARE placed at tier 2.8 — their centres come from the community map's published areas data (https://gtamap.io/data/areas/gta6.json, game-metre centres converted the same way), not from GTABase and not guessed.

Pins/clusters: schematic pins now cluster GTABase-style (screen-space ~64px grid in map-% space): numbered bubbles, green 2-9 / pale yellow 10-99 / peach-orange 100+ (Leaflet markercluster palette), tap zooms in; individual pins are solid category colour with a pointed tip and white pictogram (gradient removed on map pins only; the side panel keeps the ViceSunshine premium finish).

Not copied from GTABase: its location database/marker positions (ViceSunshine pins remain its own schematic placements, labelled unverified), its page text, and its hosted tile files. The tile imagery itself is the community's (CC BY), loaded from the community CDN.

## Reposting removed + Profile Posts/Videos/Stats tabs (Nov 21, 2026, owner's direction)

Reposting is removed from ViceSunshine entirely: the `repostFeedPost` and `repostChatMessage` actions are deleted; Feed and Clips responses no longer carry repost counts / reposted-by-me / repost-of data; legacy repost rows (feed_posts.repost_of_id > 0) are filtered out of the Feed, Clips and profile grids server-side; profile analytics no longer counts reposts; activity + in-app notifications no longer surface repost events (legacy "Repost"/"Quote Repost" notification rows are filtered in listNotifications); the Settings "Tell me when someone reposts my post" toggle and the notifyReposts API fields are gone (the inert profiles.notify_reposts column remains in the DB, unused). Profile tabs are exactly Posts (picture posts only — listProfilePosts mode "posts" filters to image posts), Videos (video posts only) and Stats. The Stats tab shows ONLY three boxes per the owner's photos: Events Attended (count + "Community events RSVP'd Going"), Crew ("No crew yet" + Open crews button, or the crew name/role as a tappable card that opens that crew's own page via the vv-open-crew hand-off), and Linked accounts ("No linked accounts shown." when empty). Rank Points / Reputation / Friends boxes and the old standalone stats section are removed from profiles. Comments never show on the profile: Settings → Data controls gains a Comment history card backed by the new `listMyFeedComments` action (the player's own feed_comments rows, newest first, with the post's title/author).

## Create a Crew redesign + crew features (Nov 21, 2026, owner's brief)

Owner's direction (this build): Create a Crew becomes ONE page with 3 steps (1 Identity → 2 Details → 3 Review), a step indicator, and the live crew-card preview always visible (right on desktop, top on mobile). Step 1 = name, tag, emblem, colour only; Step 2 = playstyle chips (Racing/Heists/Chill/Competitive/Exploring), who-can-join, platform, description, crew rules, with Motto/Region/Language collapsed under "More details (optional)"; Step 3 = review + cost + Found. The duplicate "Your platform" field and the member-cap picker are removed. Name/tag availability is checked live while typing (`checkCrewAvailability` action) with green/red plain-words messages under the fields.

Crew rules: set at creation (one rule per line, `clans.rules`), shown in a "Crew Rules" card on the crew page and on the join/apply screen with "By joining you agree to this crew's rules". Editable afterwards ONLY by Leader/Co-Leaders (`updateCrewRules` action, logged to the crew activity log; also editable via the Leader's `updateClan`).

UNLIMITED MEMBERS (supersedes the old 50 / Premium-200 cap model recorded in earlier sections): no cap anywhere. `effectiveClanCap` deleted; joinClan and reviewApplication no longer count members; `clanSummaryOf` reports `maxMembers: 0` (unlimited) and all client surfaces show "Members: Unlimited" (crew cards, crew page, leaderboard pill, suggestions, create form, crew settings). New crews store max_members 0; the column is legacy. Premium page no longer lists a crew-cap perk.

Crew tag: 2–4 characters, letters/numbers only (zod regex on createClan/updateClan), stored and displayed UPPERCASE everywhere (clanSummaryOf and all profile/chat/activity tag reads uppercase the stored value). Unique across the whole system, enforced in createClan/updateClan and checked live by checkCrewAvailability. The tag is rendered from current membership (never stored on the profile): profile/crew-page/member-list/leaderboard reads derive it from clan_members + clans.tag, so leaving or being kicked removes it everywhere at once, and joining another crew swaps it. This supersedes the earlier "tag kept exactly as typed (e.g. [lol])" note.

Roles: ladder unchanged (Recruit → Member → Elder → Co-Leader → Leader), multiple Co-Leaders allowed, `changeRole` hierarchy + activity logging unchanged. The crew page now renders the roster (with tag chips), promote/demote/kick controls, pending applications with approve/decline, and the activity log.

Inactive-Leader auto-transfer (new): `maybeTransferInactiveLeader` runs on crew page load (getClan). If the Leader's last activity (profiles.last_seen heartbeat; fallbacks: profile created_at, then membership joined_at) is 30+ days ago, leadership passes to the best candidate down the ladder (Co-Leader → Elder → Member → Recruit; longest membership first, then most crew-activity-log entries, then lowest member id). The old Leader becomes Co-Leader (never kicked). Logged to clan_activity as "Leadership transferred to X — previous leader inactive for 30 days" (actor "ViceSunshine") and announced as a system message (senderKey "system", shown as "ViceSunshine") in the crew chat.

Crew chat: unchanged automatic model (ensureCrewChats mirrors clan_members; created at founding via createClan too). New: `deleteGroupMessage` — authors can delete their own messages; in a crew chat the Leader and Co-Leaders can delete anyone's; listGroupThread returns per-message `canDelete` and the Messages UI shows Delete accordingly. Non-members get nothing (thread returns null group).

PERMANENT CREW NAME (supersedes updateClan's old rename ability): Step 1 carries the warning "Choose carefully — your crew name is permanent and can't be changed later." The crew settings form shows the name in a disabled field with the same note, and `updateClan` refuses any request whose name differs from the stored name ("Crew names are permanent…"). Description/rules/motto/tag/colour/platform/etc. stay editable.

Files changed: `server/src/actions.ts` (createClan rework, checkCrewAvailability, updateCrewRules, deleteGroupMessage, updateClan rename refusal + tag rules, joinClan/reviewApplication cap removal, effectiveClanCap removal, clanSummaryOf unlimited + uppercase tags, getClan transfer hook, listGroupThread canDelete/system sender, bot seed maxMembers 0, help-text cap line), `server/src/schema.ts` (maxMembers comment/default — no migration, column unchanged in the DB), `client/src/App.tsx` (CreateClanForm 3-step wizard, ClanCard/leaderboard/Crews-page unlimited copy, ClanHome rules card + roster + applications + activity + settings, Messages delete button, Premium perk bullets).

## Crew customisation restructure (Nov 21, 2026)

Owner's direction: the standalone Customise section is removed and everything that stays becomes CREW customisation, owned by the crew owner (Leader), living in Crew Settings. Migration `0071_crew_customisation_restructure.sql`:

- `clans` gains `badge`, `theme_accent`, `requirements` (TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '') — the crew's equipped badge, crew-page accent theme, and join requirements.
- Personal equip state for the removed/retargeted sections is deleted: `owned_items` personal rows for avatar-*/effect-*/badge-*/personal-card-*/theme-* ids are removed; `profiles.avatar_id`, `avatar_effect`, `badge`, `calling_card`, `theme_accent` are cleared to ''. Challenge + season card personal unlock rows are KEPT (they are the owner's unlock records). No accounts, posts, messages or crew data are deleted.

Avatars and Avatar Effects are deleted completely: catalogue entries removed server-side, `AvatarFxArt`/`EFFECT_LABEL`/`EFFECT_RIM` and the `effect` prop removed client-side, no effect rendering anywhere. Profiles show the member's uploaded picture or the default silhouette. `seedAvatarArt` remains as an inert legacy action (its art is no longer referenced by any catalogue item or profile, since `avatar_id` is cleared).

Calling-card merge: the personal and crew calling-card lists merged into ONE crew catalogue = the old crew list (5 free: Vice Nights `clan-vice`, Street Heat `clan-heat`, Skull Strip `skull-strip`, Keys Runner `keys-runner`, Palm Reaper `palm-reaper`; 3 Premium: Vice Haze `clan-haze`, Dead Palms `clan-dead`, Inferno `inferno`). Six personal items were name/design duplicates of these and were folded in. The two personal-only Premium designs (Hydro Vice `armed-palms`, Magic Palms `club-leonida`) were retired in the merge so the merged section keeps exactly 5 free + 3 Premium like every other section. Existing crews' stored `clans.callingCard` values all reference the kept design keys, so no crew banner broke.

Crew customisation API: `getCustomise` is replaced by `getCrewCustomise({playerKey, clanId})` → `{items, clanName, isOwner, ownerPremiumActive}`; `equipCosmetic`/`unequipCosmetic` are Leader-only against the crew (Premium checked against the crew OWNER). `enforceCrewPremiumCosmetics` runs on crew reads (`getClan`, `listClans`, `getCrewCustomise`): if the Leader lacks Premium, Premium-equipped items unequip to free defaults (emblem `shield`→ stored default is catalogue `sunset` for new crews; enforcement resets emblem to the free catalogue default `sunset`, colour `#FF2E88`, calling card/badge/theme cleared). Challenge cards (Dark Vice, Skull Harvest, Vicestronaut) and season cards keep their exact unlock conditions, unlock for the owner personally, and equip to the crew; `getSeasonChallenges` now reports the player's crew id and crew-based equipped state.

Crew level removed site-wide: `clanSummary` no longer carries `xp`/`level`/`rankLevel`/`rankNextLevelPoints`/`rankPointsToNext`; `CLAN_LEVEL_STEPS` deleted; client `XpBar`, the "Crew Level" pill, `LV` badges and the "Crew Level (lifetime)" tile removed from crew cards, Crews list, crew page, leaderboard and Create a Crew preview. Season points, trophies, the member-bonus multiplier and PERSONAL user levels/XP are untouched.

Crew page Bio + Rules & Requirements: `clans.description` is the Bio; `clans.rules` + new `clans.requirements` form Rules & Requirements. Edited by Leader + Co-Leaders via new action `updateCrewBioRules` (Crew Settings → Bio & Rules tab — the same fields as Create a Crew Step 2, one source of truth). The join box shows rules + requirements with "By joining you agree to this crew's rules and meet its requirements". Create a Crew Step 1 uses the catalogue Crew Emblems/Crew Colours (client consts `CREW_CATALOGUE_EMBLEMS`/`CREW_CATALOGUE_COLORS`, mirroring the server catalogue), Step 2 has Bio/Rules/Requirements, Step 3 shows the Crew Settings customisation line.

Note: no crew header-background upload feature existed in the build (the crew card header is the equipped calling-card banner or the automatic crew-colour gradient); the brief's mention of one referred to a task that had not shipped a separate upload, so there was nothing separate to preserve — the calling-card banner and the automatic colour header remain exactly as they were.

## Crew card uniform layout + header background (Nov 21, 2026)

One crew card layout for every crew (the NEON TIDE CARTEL layout, no level pill/bar — crew level stays removed): fixed-height (218px) header → points pills → MEMBERS / EVENTS stat boxes only (SEASON RANK and WINS removed from the card — ViceSunshine does not track them; the underlying `wins`/`seasonRank` summary fields still exist for the crew page/leaderboard, they are just never rendered on the card) → members line showing just the count ("13 members", no "/50", no cap bar — membership is unlimited) → Save → View crew / Join pinned to the card bottom. Crew name and motto truncate to one line. The old per-crew banner strip (the equipped calling card rendered as a separate picture panel above the header, e.g. Keys Runner Collective's "STREET HEAT / ASPHALT & FIRE") is gone from the card AND from the Suggested-crews section, which now renders the same `ClanCard`.

Header background storage: `clans.header_blob_key` (migration 0072) — the Leader's own uploaded picture as an owned blob under `crew-headers/<uuid>-<name>`, cropped to the header shape (1200×500 bake) in the editor before upload and moderation-checked server-side (`assertImageAllowed`) before storage. Actions: `uploadCrewHeaderImage` / `removeCrewHeaderImage` — Leader only (server checks the caller's crew role is exactly "Leader"; Co-Leaders and members are refused). `clanSummary.headerImageUrl` carries the served URL, resolved in `listClans` and `getClan` only. Resolution order everywhere the header renders (crew card via shared `CrewCardHeader`, crew page top banner): custom picture → equipped calling card artwork (so crews with an existing banner keep it as their header background automatically — no data migration needed, nothing uploaded is lost) → gradient matched to `clans.color`. Editor lives in Crew Settings → Crew customisation tab (`CrewHeaderBackgroundEditor` in client/src/App.tsx), above the customisation sections, with drag/zoom crop and a live preview of the real card header.

## Premium Discord role sync + name rules (Nov 21, 2026)

Owner's brief (3 parts + copy clean-up), implemented on the existing Premium entitlement (`profiles.premium` / `premium_since` / `premium_ends_at`, lazy lapse in `applyPremiumWeeklyCredits`). No new payment system.

DISCORD PREMIUM ROLE (bot-ready handoff): Premium now includes a "Premium" role in the official Discord server (invite https://discord.gg/NDH9XxyVkW, admin-set via `discord_invite_url` meta) unlocking a private Premium lounge. Migration `0073_discord_premium_role_sync_state.sql` adds `discord_role_sync` (one row per in-scope player: `desired` grant/remove owned by the SITE, `bot_status` pending/granted/removed/failed + `bot_note` owned by the BOT's confirmations). `syncDiscordRoleState` recomputes desired = grant exactly when Premium is active (after the lazy lapse, so a cancelled Premium flips to removal the moment the paid month ends) AND `profiles.discord_id` is non-empty; it runs on getProfile, getWallet, signInWithPassword, saveProfile / updateProfile / saveSettings, getDiscordRoleQueue and getOwnerDashboard — re-checked on login and in normal daily use. Bot interface: `getDiscordRoleQueue` (pending + failed rows, oldest first — catch-up after downtime is inherent, the queue derives from stored desired state) and `ackDiscordRoleSync` (granted/removed/failed + note; stale confirmations that contradict the current desired state are ignored). Both bot actions are gated by a server-held bot secret (`discord_bot_secret` in meta, generated/regenerated by the owner only via `getDiscordBotSecret` / `resetDiscordBotSecret`, shown only in the Owner Dashboard's Discord section): the queue names Premium members and their Discord usernames and a forged ack could fake a grant, so neither action is public — with no secret configured, both refuse. The site NEVER claims a grant before a bot ack: `getDiscordRoleStatus` returns not_premium / link_needed ("Link your Discord to claim your Premium role") / pending ("Premium role pending — join the Discord server and link your account" + invite) / granted (bot-confirmed only). A failed grant never blocks Premium on the site — Premium state is independent of the sync row. Fallback until the bot exists: `getOwnerDashboard.discordPremiumList` renders the same states as the owner's manual grant/remove list in a new Owner Dashboard section, with the Discord-side setup steps (create "Premium" role + private #premium-lounge channel) spelled out — those server-settings steps are the OWNER's action in Discord; no bot exists yet and none was set up. Prompts render in: Premium page (PremiumView), own Profile, Edit Profile → Linked accounts (Discord field), Settings → Connectors (Discord field). Copy wording used everywhere: "Premium Discord role + private Premium lounge". Discord tab gained a "Premium lounge" benefit entry.

DISPLAY NAME RULE (verified + confirmed, was already enforced): free = one change per 3 months, Premium = one per month, server-side in `nameChangeGate` on every save path (saveProfile / updateProfile / saveSettings), rejection message carries the exact unlock date; Edit Profile shows the Name field read-only/greyed with "You can change it again on <date>" from `nameLockedUntil`. Username (gamertag) stays permanent (`assertGamertagPermanent`). No personal clan tag exists (crew-derived) — unchanged.

CREW NAME (verified): permanent for everyone incl. Premium Leaders — `updateClan` refuses any different name server-side; the crew settings form shows the name in a disabled field with the permanence note; no rename control exists anywhere and no Premium copy offers one.

PREMIUM COPY CLEAN-UP: the canonical benefits list (Premium page, Edit Profile Premium banner, Ask Vice knowledge) is now: no ads; Premium badge/identity + Premium effects; unlimited Ask Vice; monthly Vice Credits (250/week); exclusive cosmetics incl. the gold Vice Crest; display name changes per the lock rules (Premium monthly / free 3-monthly); early event access; Premium Discord role + private Premium lounge. No member-cap claim exists anywhere (crews are unlimited for everyone — confirmed by sweep); Database / guides / codes / basic map are not paywalled; no Premium points/ranking multiplier exists or was added.

Files changed: `server/src/schema.ts` (discordRoleSync table), `drizzle/0073_discord_premium_role_sync_state.sql`, `server/src/actions.ts` (syncDiscordRoleState + discordRoleStatusFor helpers, sync hooks, getDiscordRoleStatus / getDiscordRoleQueue / ackDiscordRoleSync actions, getOwnerDashboard discordPremiumList, Ask Vice Premium knowledge line), `client/src/App.tsx` (PremiumView benefits + role status blocks, Profile prompt cards, Linked accounts + Settings Connectors Discord notes, Edit Profile Premium banner copy, Owner Dashboard Discord list section, Discord tab lounge entry, Eggs ad-card line).

## Owner account fix + owner monitoring completion (Nov 21, 2026)

OWNER IDENTITY (supersedes every earlier "Owner = earliest profile / first account" note in this file): the Owner is the officialvicesunshine account — the account made with the email officialvicesunshine@gmail.com (username/gamertag officialvicesunshine). Zazzz is NOT the owner. `ownerProfileRow` (server/src/actions.ts) now resolves the owner in this order: (1) the profile whose email is officialvicesunshine@gmail.com (case-insensitive, reusing the existing OFFICIAL_VICESUNSHINE_EMAIL constant), (2) the profile whose gamertag is officialvicesunshine (case-insensitive), (3) fallback — the earliest profile by id — only when neither exists (e.g. a fresh test copy), so owner actions never break entirely. Still exactly ONE owner: no owner-switching control exists anywhere, and there is no separate owner flag — the OWNER badge, getProfile's isOwner and the Owner Dashboard gating all derive from ownerProfileRow as before. Owner Dashboard copy that described the owner as "the first account created in ViceSunshine" now names officialvicesunshine.

OWNER MONITORING (owner's rule: "make my account follow the laws but I want to be able to monitor everything if legal"): verified the Owner Dashboard covers everything legal — player counts + newest sign-ups, Premium members + honest revenue state, support requests, the moderation queue (flagged posts/bugs, reported players, flagged profiles, recent reports), the Discord Premium role list, and the auto-corrections list (Automatic systems section, from getAutomationStatus). Two gaps were filled in this build:
- RECENT PUBLIC POSTS: getOwnerDashboard now returns `recentPosts` (newest 15 original public Feed posts, repost rows excluded, flagged state shown) and OwnerView renders a "Recent public posts" section — the owner can monitor all public posts at a glance.
- REPORTED PRIVATE MESSAGES: private one-to-one DMs stay private — never screened, never monitored, never read into any owner response. The single legal exception now works end to end: new `dm_reports` table (migration 0074) + new `reportDirectMessage` action (participant-only, never your own message, 1:1 DMs only — group_id 0 — one report per player per message, standard reason list) + a Report control under each incoming message in a DM thread (reason chips + optional note, with the plain-words promise that only that one message is shared). getOwnerDashboard returns `reportedMessages` built ONLY from dm_reports rows (it resolves exactly the reported message ids — no other private message is ever selected), and OwnerView shows them in the Moderation queue under "Reported private messages". There is no way for the owner to read unreported private DMs.

Files changed: `server/src/actions.ts` (ownerProfileRow + OWNER_GAMERTAG, reportDirectMessage, getOwnerDashboard recentPosts/reportedMessages + description), `server/src/schema.ts` (dmReports table), `drizzle/0074_dm_reports_for_owner_moderation_queue.sql`, `client/src/App.tsx` (OwnerView copy + Recent public posts section + Reported private messages queue block, MessagesView DM report control/form).

## Premium price — location-based, USD base (Nov 21, 2026, v248)

Owner's decision (his exact words): "it should be usd" / "CAN IT NOT ALWAYS BE CANDIAM DOLLAR FOR ME LIKE LOCATIONS BASED?" / "BUT ORIGIAL PRICE IS USD" / "AND THE PRICE IS USD 4.99 SO THE OTHER CURRENCY SHOULD CONVERT WITH A HIGH RATE". Rule: Premium's original price is $4.99 USD; each visitor sees it in their own currency (Settings currency when picked, else the site's existing auto-detection, CAD default), converted from USD at a deliberately HIGH rate and rounded UP to a clean .99 price point, so a local price is never worth less than $4.99 USD. One shared table lives in both `server/src/actions.ts` and `client/src/App.tsx` (`FX_FROM_USD` + `premiumPriceFor`): CAD 1.42 → CA$7.99, EUR 0.95 → €4.99, GBP 0.82 → £4.99, AUD 1.55 → A$7.99, USD exactly $4.99; a currency with no entry falls back to USD (the same fallback checkout uses when Stripe has no price for a currency). Checkout (`subscribePremium`) takes the visitor's currency and charges in it once payments are connected; payments are still NOT connected (Stripe test mode), so it still returns "Payments are not connected yet" and never grants Premium. Surfaces converted from the fixed "CA$4.99": Edit Profile gold banner, Premium page (heading, explainer, Subscribe button), Settings (plan tile + one-tap checkout row + subscribe call), account popover menu, Easter Eggs ad card, crew customisation Premium note, legal page copy, Owner Dashboard Premium & revenue (fields renamed premiumPriceCad→premiumPriceUsd, revenueTakenCad→revenueTakenUsd; revenue shown converted via moneyFromUsd, no .99 rounding), getWallet (premiumPriceCad→premiumPriceUsd), and Ask Vice's knowledge (static lines + a per-player line quoting the asker's saved-currency price).

## Feed creator — POST / STORY bottom row removed (Nov 21, 2026)

Owner's direction (his words): the Feed + camera screen is fine, pressing POST bringing up the New post tab is good — "remove that row saying (story/post)" at the bottom of the New post screen. Change (client only, `client/src/App.tsx`, FeedCreator): the fixed POST / STORY switcher bar that sat pinned to the bottom of the New post / Add to story picker is deleted, along with the 88px spacer that only existed to scroll the picker grid clear of it. The picker screens now end with their grid — no bottom row. Nothing else about the flow changed: the camera screen keeps its POST mode bar (pressing it opens the New post picker, exactly as the owner approved), the Your story entry still opens the creator aimed at a story (Add to story picker), and the publish destination (Post / Story / Videos) is still chosen on the Details step's own chooser. Muse artifact only for now — the live website gets this with the next publish, per the owner's "only on muse first" instruction.

## Post Details / Share flow + hashtags everywhere (Nov 21, 2026)

Owner's brief: after taking a live picture or video — or picking from the gallery — the flow should land on an Instagram-style "New post" Details screen (his screenshot: media on top, "Add a caption…", Add link, big Share button). Pressing Share brings you straight to your Feed with the post on it, looking like every other Feed post (dates etc. automatic). Media rules: several photos per post from the gallery, but only ONE video per post, and a LIVE camera capture is always a single item (gallery picks can be several). Hashtags must work everywhere people post — captions, stories — and in Feed search; the automatic feed bots use them too.

MEDIA MODEL (migration `0075_feed_post_media_list_and_link.sql`): `feed_posts` gains `media_urls` (JSON array of the post's full media list; `media_url` still holds the first item so older readers work) and `link_url` (the post's optional attached link). `createFeedPost` accepts `mediaUrls` (max 4) + `linkUrl` and enforces the composition server-side: more than one video is refused, and a video mixed with photos is refused. The link must be a real http(s) address and passes the same screening as media URLs. `parseMediaUrls` / `isVideoMediaUrl` helpers in actions.ts; `listFeed`, `profilePostItems` (profile posts, Saved, getFeedPostsByIds) all return `mediaUrls`, `linkUrl` and `hashtags` per post.

CREATOR (client, FeedCreator): camera captures are flagged `live` on the CreatorItem — a live capture can never join or be joined by other picks (addBlob guard, onFiles / addAlbumItem guards, Preview shows "Live capture — one per post" instead of Add more; story sets exempt since each item becomes its own story). Gallery picks validate the same composition (several photos OR one video). Publish now makes ONE createFeedPost call carrying all uploaded URLs (previously one post per photo), plus the caption and link. The Details step is rebuilt as the Share screen: media preview, Caption field ("Add a caption…"), a Hashtags chip row (one tap appends #GTAVI / #GTA6 / #ViceCity / #Leonida / #CarMeet / #Gameplay / #Meme / #Screenshot; typing #tags by hand works identically), an Add link field (posts and Videos only), the category chips stay for posts, and the primary button is now "Share". The separate Title input is gone — the caption is the post's text. On Share, FeedView lands on For you with sort Latest, source All, Videos-only off and the local search cleared, so the just-shared post is the first thing in the Feed.

RENDERING: `FeedMediaBlock` takes `mediaUrls` — several photos render as `FeedPhotoCarousel` (scroll-snap slides, n/N counter, dots) inside the one post; a single video/YouTube/native clip renders exactly as before. The attached link renders as a hostname chip under the caption (Feed card, profile post sheet, Saved card). `linkifyText` now also splits out #tags: in the Feed they are real buttons that set the Feed search to that hashtag; in the post sheet / Saved they jump to the Feed with the hashtag search; in the story viewer they render as accent tags in the story text.

HASHTAGS: `extractHashtags` / `feedRowHashtags` (normalised lowercase, from title + caption). `listFeed` search: a query carrying a #tag matches only posts carrying that hashtag (every typed tag must match); plain word search unchanged. Stories need no schema change — hashtags live in the story text (createStory already stores it) and render tagged in the viewer; the creator's hashtag chips append to the story text too.

BOTS: `botHashtagLine(kind)` = "#GTAVI #GTA6 #ViceSunshine" + kind tags (#GTANews, #StreamerClip, #Meme, #CarMeet, #Gameplay, #Tips…). `refreshFeedMedia` appends it to every new GTA CLIPPER video post, GTA NEWS post and Rockstar Games post (same single shared search round — no extra searches). `refreshBotCommunity` appends it to the seeded members' new picture/video posts (by kind) and adds "#GTAVI #GTA6" to their story texts. Existing stored posts are not rewritten.

Files changed: `server/src/schema.ts` (feed_posts media_urls + link_url), `drizzle/0075_feed_post_media_list_and_link.sql`, `server/src/actions.ts` (hashtag/media helpers, createFeedPost, listFeed, profilePostItem(s), refreshFeedMedia, refreshBotCommunity), `client/src/App.tsx` (FeedCreator flow + Details/Share screen, FeedView publish landing, FeedMediaBlock carousel, linkifyText hashtags, FeedPostCard / ProfilePostSheet / Saved card rendering, story viewer).

## Story viewer bottom bars — own-story black Activity bar + Highlight picker; other-story Send message row (Nov 21, 2026)

Owner's direction: viewing your OWN story, the bottom is a solid black bar — Activity on the left (eye + view count, heart + like count, same 48-hour rule as before: after 48h the views are gone and only the like count/list remains; tapping Activity still opens the private who-saw-it list above the bar) and a Highlight button on the FAR RIGHT. The Highlight button opens a picker sheet listing the player's own highlights (cover, title, story count; a story already in a highlight shows "Added" and can't be re-added) — picking one appends the story via the NEW server action `addStoryToHighlight` (own highlight + own story enforced server-side, idempotent, 30-story cap matching createHighlight). With NO highlights, the button skips the sheet: the viewer closes and the parent (StoriesRow on the Feed, ProfilePage on profiles) opens the existing New highlight screen (HighlightEditorSheet) with this story already picked — new optional StoryViewer prop `onCreateHighlight(storyId)`, wired in exactly those two parents (the editor is portalled at z-[90], below the viewer's z-[100], so the viewer must close first, same pattern as Edit highlight). The picker's New highlight row takes the same route. Highlight-mode viewing (your own highlight balls) is untouched — Send / More stay.

Steered in mid-build (owner's second screenshot): viewing SOMEONE ELSE'S story, the bottom is now the screenshot's layout — a black strip with a rounded "Send message..." reply box (same replyToStory DM as before, placeholder changed from "Reply to {author}…") and three icons to its right: heart (the same like toggle; the count moved into its aria-label), comment bubble (focuses the message box), paper plane (sends the reply; empty text keeps the "Write a reply first." notice; the old text Send button inside the form is gone, Enter still submits). Both bars bleed full-width via -mx-4 -mb-4 inside the existing footer; auto-advance pauses while the picker sheet is open, and the picker closes on story change like the delete confirm.

Addendum (steered in mid-build, owner's highlight screenshots): viewing your OWN HIGHLIGHT gets the same treatment — the old floating Send / More row is replaced by a black bar matching his screenshots exactly: Activity on the left (people icon; opens the same private who-saw-it list above the bar — the insights panel's `!highlight` gate was dropped so it renders in highlight mode too), and Browse, Send and More on the right, icon over label. Browse is new: a full sheet with a 3-column tile grid of the highlight's stories (current story ringed, time-ago chips) — tapping a tile jumps the viewer to it. More's menu is now EXACTLY Edit highlight + Remove from highlight — NO Archive highlight, per the owner. "Remove from highlight" follows the screenshot's meaning: it removes the story ON SCREEN from the highlight (new server action `removeStoryFromHighlight`: own highlight enforced, story row untouched — it stays in the story archive; removing the last story deletes the now-empty highlight and reports highlightDeleted). This replaces the old More → "Remove highlight" whole-highlight delete (deleteHighlight action still exists server-side); the confirm names the story-level removal and warns when it is the last story. On success the viewer closes through the existing onRemoved so the profile re-renders the highlight as it now is.

## Upload picker — select one only, multi-select via Select (Nov 21, 2026)

Owner's brief: "when I try to upload a picture it does not allow me to select one only so fix that min is 1" — in the New post picker every tap on an album photo ADDED it to the picks (up to 4, all checkmarked, as in his screenshot), so there was no way to pick just one picture. Rules (client only, `FeedCreator` in `client/src/App.tsx`): the New post / New clip pickers now pick ONE by default — a tap on an album tile or a session tile makes that photo/video the ONLY pick (it replaces whatever was picked; the hero follows it), and Next already enables from a single pick (minimum 1, unchanged). Picking several happens only in multi-select mode, switched by the picker's Select button (accent-filled + `aria-pressed` while on; turning it off collapses the picks to the one on show). In multi mode the old behaviour stands — taps add up to 4 — plus tapping an already-picked album tile now un-picks it. Implementation: new `multiSelect` state + derived `singlePick` (`(picker === "post" || picker === "clip") && !multiSelect`); `addAlbumItem` branches on it (replace vs append; the 4-cap and video-mix guards only bind when a tap adds, the live-capture guard still always binds). The story picker is unchanged — a tap there already takes exactly the one tapped item to Preview, and its Select button keeps opening the device library. Device picks through the Your library tile are unchanged (the phone's own picker is where several are chosen deliberately). No server, schema or migration changes.

## Upload picker — duplicate photo tile / both tiles checked (Nov 21, 2026)

Owner's brief: "Theirs like this bug that forces me to pick 2 — but when posting only one get posted so it's more like a visual bug." His screenshot: the SAME photo twice in the New post Recents grid, BOTH tiles checked at once. Cause (found in `FeedCreator`, `client/src/App.tsx`): the picker grid rendered a picked album photo TWICE — once as its picked tile (from `items`) and once again as its album tile (from the album merge), and the album tile computed its own checkmark from `items.findIndex(url)` — so one pick showed as two checked tiles (the two tiles also shared one React key). Posting posted the one item, hence "visual only". A second copy of the same photo could also appear when a device pick's background album save landed mid-session (pick tile under its device URL + album tile under the new album URL), and posting an album photo re-uploaded it, storing a second copy under a new URL so the album itself gained the photo twice.

Fixes:
- GRID DEDUPE (client): new derived list `albumGridShown` = the kind-filtered album items MINUS anything already picked (matched by URL, and by the session twin map below). The picker grid renders `albumGridShown`; album tiles are therefore never picked and no longer render an outline/checkmark of their own — a picked photo shows exactly once, as its picked tile with the one checkmark, and grid keys are unique again. `postHero` and the album hint texts still use the full `albumGridItems`.
- UN-PICK MOVED (client): in multi-select mode the tap-again-to-un-pick that used to live on the album tile now lives on the picked tile itself — a tap puts the photo on show, tapping the one on show un-picks it (`removeItem`). Single-pick and story-picker tap behaviour unchanged.
- SESSION TWIN (client): `savePickToAlbum` now takes the pick's own URL and records pick URL → album URL in `albumTwinRef` BEFORE invalidating the album query, so when the background album save lands, the grid hides the pick's album twin tile instead of showing the same photo twice.
- NO SECOND COPY ON PUBLISH (client): in `publish`, an unedited album pick (its item URL is a stored URL, not a `blob:` device URL; videos or identity-filter photos) publishes its existing album URL instead of re-uploading the file. Edited album photos still bake + upload as before. Safe because feed-media blobs are never deleted anywhere.
- NO SECOND COPY ON UPLOAD (server, `uploadFeedMedia` in `server/src/actions.ts`): after the existing type/size/moderation checks, the file's SHA-256 is looked up in a per-player meta index (`feed-media-hash:<playerKey>:<hash>`); a hit returns the existing blob URL instead of storing a duplicate. A miss stores as before and records the hash. Per-player, so another player's identical file still gets its own copy and ownership record; moderation still runs first on every upload, exactly as before. No schema or migration change (the existing `meta` table carries the index).

Files changed: `client/src/App.tsx` (FeedCreator grid, savePickToAlbum, publish), `server/src/actions.ts` (uploadFeedMedia).

## Profile picture / banner slow-load fix (owner's report, Nov 21 2026)

- Owner's report: after uploading a profile picture or background (banner) picture, both showed the broken-image "?" placeholder for a long time before appearing; the pictures did eventually load. His screenshot shows the "?" in the banner, the profile avatar AND the shell header avatar, while a highlight cover on the same page (an older, already-cached story photo) rendered instantly.
- Diagnosis (verified against the data): the stored files were NOT the problem any more — the earlier optimizer pass works, and the owner's own stored avatar is 512×512 / 98 KB and his banner 1280×384 / 56 KB, both served from the same `./blobs/…` route as the highlight cover that loaded fine. The delay was in the delivery path around them: (1) after an upload, the client threw away the picture bytes it already had and pointed every surface at the brand-new server URL, so the picture only appeared after a profile refetch PLUS a full fresh download; the avatar handler did not even write the new URL into the profile caches (the banner handler did). (2) The pre-optimizer self-heal fired the moment the owner opened his profile and downloaded BOTH full pictures in the background just to check whether they needed re-saving — on every session, forever, competing with the visible avatar/banner at the exact moment they were painting (invisible now that photos are small, but pure contention). (3) The profile avatar and banner were `loading="lazy"` like list thumbnails, so the browser deprioritised the two most prominent images on the page behind everything else.
- Fix (`client/src/App.tsx` only; no server, schema or migration change): (a) instant paint from the device — `onPickAvatarFile` / `onPickBannerFile` (and the self-heal saves) now write the exact uploaded bytes into the `["profile", playerKey]` and `["social-profile", playerKey, playerKey]` caches as a data URL the moment the upload lands (new `applyAvatarUrlToCaches`, mirroring `applyBannerMediaToCaches`), and `swapToServerPhotoWhenLoaded` preloads the server's URL in the background and swaps it into the caches only once it has genuinely loaded, so the handoff can never flash the placeholder; if the preload fails the local copy stays for the session and the next natural refetch restores the canonical URL. (b) The self-heal now waits ~3.5 s after the profile paints and checks the stored file's size with a HEAD request first (`storedPhotoByteSize`): a picture already ≤ 450 KB is left completely alone with no picture bytes downloaded; only an oversized stored file (or an un-readable size) takes the old download-and-resave path. (c) The shell header avatars, the social top-bar avatar, the profile-page avatars (own and viewed) and the profile banner image load `eager` with `fetchPriority="high"` (new `eager` prop on `CosmeticAvatar`); list/feed/comment avatars stay lazy.
- Honest limits: the sandbox cannot reproduce the phone gateway's per-request latency, so the fix is verified by build + the rendered audit and by the data above; the owner's tap-through on his phone (upload a new pfp/banner and watch it appear immediately) is the final proof. Pictures uploaded before this build keep loading from their server URLs as before — already small, and now eager/high-priority on the profile.

## Ask Vice — no raw errors, and the no-AI fallback answers the actual question (Nov 21, 2026)

- Owner's words: "Why is this happening on the online website AI?" (screenshot: a one-character send dumped raw validation code — `[{ "code": "too_small", ... "path": ["question"] }]` — into the chat) + "Yes but it's not answering my question for instance look it's not answering my questions it's just replying random stuff..." (screenshot: "Hello" and "Where's the Easter egg" both answered with the same latest-patch news list).
- Diagnosis (two separate root causes): (1) `askVice`'s request schema required `question` min length 2, so a shorter send failed validation, and the Ask Vice panel's `onError` interpolated the raw error message into the chat bubble. (2) On the live website no inference key is configured, so every question lands in the handler's `catch` fallback — which led with the always-on latest news for EVERY question, whatever was asked. (The Gemini key for the live site is being set up separately in Railway; this fix makes Vice behave properly with or without it.)
- Changes (`server/src/actions.ts` + `client/src/App.tsx`; no schema or migration change): the request schema now accepts min length 1, and the handler answers anything under 2 alphanumeric characters with a polite "ask me a full question" reply — never an error. The client matches it: a too-short send is answered locally in the panel and never leaves the device, and the mutation's `onError` no longer interpolates the raw error — it always shows one clean sentence. The fallback is rewritten to route by intent: a greeting gets a greeting (with the player's gamertag when known), an Easter-egg question gets the matched eggs (name, location, status, how-to) or an honest not-logged-yet reply, a release/launch question gets the confirmed November 19, 2026 launch fact, a Premium/price question gets the asker's real location-based price via the existing `premiumPriceFor`, a credits question gets the real earn/spend rules, news/patch questions get the latest news + latest patch, and crew/event/bug questions get their matched rows — each with a link to the right page. Anything else falls back to keyword-matched content only (Database, Guides, eggs, events, bugs, news, crews — Guides are included now; they were matched but never shown before), and the latest news no longer leads every reply.
- Unchanged: the AI path itself (same `ctx.inference` call, same context, same privacy rules), the support-request flow, and the panel's look. When a real inference key IS configured, answers come from the AI exactly as before; the new routing is only the safety net.

## Profile grid goes 4:5, opened post goes edge-to-edge with an icons-only row, phone profile type minimized (Nov 21, 2026)

- Owner's words: "Look out them sides by side I'm taking only by the aspec ratio that ig uses do the same so that my boxes look less square and also look the size direent when you press on a picture and how there's features are so simple and clean and lux unlike ours so fix that" (four screenshots: his square profile grid vs IG's taller grid; his opened post — inset rounded photo, word-labelled Like / Discuss / views / Share / Save row — vs IG's opened post — full-width edge-to-edge photo, icons + counts only) + "Also minimize fonts it looks like it's taking over the profile" (screenshot of his own profile) + "This is general overall for the phone" + "make it a mini version of the laptop but following if style as well positron layers etc." (IG style; positions/layers unchanged).
- Changes (`client/src/App.tsx` + `client/src/theme.css` only; no server, schema or migration change): (1) `ProfileGridTile` and `ProfileAddTile` go from `aspect-square` to `aspect-[4/5]` — the whole profile grid (photos, videos, and the permanent add boxes) uses IG's taller 4:5 shape. (2) The profile post viewer goes IG-style: `FeedMediaBlock` / `FeedPhotoCarousel` gain a `bleed` mode used only by `ProfilePostFullCard` — the media bleeds past the viewer column's side padding on phone, square corners on phone (rounded again from `sm` up), a single photo shows at its own aspect (height capped at 82vh) instead of the forced 4:3 crop, carousel slides run 4:5 on phone, and YouTube/uploaded clips use their existing flush modes. The card's action row is now icons-only — 24px heart, comment bubble and paper plane with just their counts, Save bookmark pinned right, no word labels; the words survive in aria-labels and the view count still shows in the comments view's header. The auto-generated caption the composer stamps on a caption-less media post ("Shared a photo with the community." and its clip/video twins) no longer renders as a caption in this view — a body the player actually typed always shows. The Feed cards keep their labelled row (that layout was the owner's earlier direction for the Feed). (3) Phone-only mini type for the profile, extending the existing `@media (max-width: 639.98px)` profile block in theme.css via new hook classes: display name 21→17px, top-bar name 19→16px, handle 14.5→13px, bio 14.5→13.5px, joined/born lines 13.5→12.5px, posts count 13→12px, header buttons compacted to 13px/34px pills, highlight balls 72→60px wide with 54px circles and 11px labels, the tab bar pulled closer (margin-top 20px) with 22px icons, and the opened post's caption block stepped down (title/body 14px, likes 13.5px, comments link 13px, date 12px). Positions and layer order are untouched everywhere; the laptop keeps its full sizing.
- Folded in while this build ran (owner, Nov 21 2026 ~21:08 MST): "When viewing someone post remove the top bar and also at a arrow so u can go back to the person page not your own" — `ProfilePostsViewer` is now portalled to `<body>` (the same fix pattern as the highlight picker page): rendered inline, its fixed layer was trapped in the page wrapper's stacking context and the shell's phone top bar (avatar / VS / search) painted over its header. As a body child it covers the site's top bar completely; its existing back arrow (aria-label "Back to <name>'s profile") calls `onClose`, which returns to the profile the post was opened from — the poster's page. (The "ViceSunshine" bar in the owner's screenshot above the site bar is the Muse app's own shell header, which the site cannot remove and which will not exist on the real website.)
- Honest limits: verified by build + audit; the audit browser's editor profile has one photo post, so the 4:5 grid and the minimized header can be screenshotted live, but the edge-to-edge opened post is best judged on the owner's phone tap-through (open own profile → tap the photo → full-width picture, icons-only row).

## "Hit a snag" crash screen — the real error is now recorded (Nov 21, 2026)

- Owner's words: "Also check this problem it has been ongoing on muse at least is it bec it's not online or check the problem to double confirm" (screenshot: the "ViceVerse hit a snag" error screen on his phone) + "Yes, fix the snag".
- Diagnosis (double-confirmed from the captured evidence bundle, inspection 8885b4f3): NOT an offline problem and NOT the live website — a fresh render capture of the Muse artifact loads with nav 200, zero console errors and zero failed requests, and the app shell (top bar, bottom nav) is still alive in the owner's screenshot. The crash is a render error on his device that the root error boundary in `client/src/main.tsx` caught — and that boundary deliberately discarded the error (`getDerivedStateFromError` returned only `{ failed: true }`, no `componentDidCatch`), so the actual cause left no trace anywhere: not on the screen, not on the device, not on the server. Every guarded localStorage parse in App.tsx was checked and is wrapped in try/catch, so the cause cannot be pinned from source alone — it has to be captured where it happens.
- Changes: (1) `client/src/main.tsx` — the boundary now implements `componentDidCatch`: the error message, stack and React component stack are kept on the device (`vv-last-crash` in localStorage, survives the reload), sent once per page load to the new `reportClientError` action, and shown on the snag screen itself under a "What happened (already recorded for the team)" expander so the owner can screenshot the real cause. The snag screen's wording and Reload button are unchanged otherwise. (2) `server/src/schema.ts` + migration `0077_client_error_reports.sql` — new `client_errors` table (player key, message, stack, component stack, screen title, user agent, timestamp; diagnostics only). (3) `server/src/actions.ts` — `reportClientError` (any client may report; bounded field sizes) and `getClientErrors` (owner-gated via the existing `assertOwner`, newest first) so recorded crashes can be read back and the real bug fixed from evidence.
- Profile pages were NOT touched in this build (owner's standing instruction: "Don't touch the profile").
- Honest limit: this build makes the crash visible and recorded; the underlying device-specific render error itself can only be named once it fires again on the owner's phone and lands in `client_errors` (or he opens the "What happened" expander on the snag screen). The audit browser cannot reproduce it — its fresh capture is error-free.

(5) The date stamp matches the reference: "OCT 12, 2024 • 2:13 AM" (uppercased short date + time). (6) The Share paper plane carries no count number, like the reference (the count stays in its aria-label and in the post menu).
