## Linked accounts are real links now — one platform per screen, Discord opens Discord (Nov 21, 2026)

- Owner's words: "How does the link work? ( can you make it actually possible like how I put a instagram link, tiktok etc. Here can my users do the same for ps/ Xbox etx" + "when I wnat to link my ps account I press on the ps logo and it should let me actually put my ps link here so when people press on it they can go to my profile and add me or so. Same goes with all those other categories" + "Listen carefully when I pres on the ps logo I should be able to only link ps, there will no other places in that tab to allow me to link Xbox etc. If I want I have to go out and like let's say less Xbox" + "Well when they press discord help to bring them there directly so they copy and paste it under a link section idk make it smth convenient for them cuz discord plays a big role here" + "Hey hey not only discord my website is quality based".
- Before: the four profile tiles only copied the typed ID (tap-to-copy); the Linked accounts page showed all four ID fields on one page.
- Changes (`client/src/App.tsx` + `server/src/actions.ts`; no schema or migration change — the stored values are the same profile fields, only their meaning is completed): (1) New `platformLink(kind, value)` resolver: a pasted full profile link is used exactly as pasted; an ID/gamertag builds the platform's real profile page — PlayStation `https://profile.playstation.com/<PSN ID>` (Sony's public profile page; pattern verified against a live profile URL), Xbox `https://play.xbox.com/user?gamertag=<gamertag>` (verified against live profile URLs), Rockstar `https://socialclub.rockstargames.com/member/<ID>` (verified against a live member URL), Discord `https://discord.com/users/<number user ID>` (Discord's documented profile-link form). (2) The profile tiles (`LinkTile`) and the link chips (`PlayerLinks`, profile preview + crew cards) now OPEN that page in a new tab — the way an Instagram/TikTok link works — with an external-link arrow on the tile. The one honest exception: a Discord value that is only a username has no page anywhere (Discord profiles open only by number ID), so that tile keeps tap-to-copy and the Discord link screen says exactly that and how to fix it. (3) The Linked accounts page is now a platform list (four rows showing linked status); pressing a platform — its row, its unlinked profile tile, or the Premium "Link your Discord" prompt — opens a screen for THAT platform only: its own field (accepts the full link or just the ID), numbered steps for grabbing the link in that app, a live preview of the exact page visitors will land on (tappable to test), and only that platform's visibility switch (Rockstar keeps its Private/Public choice; the Premium Discord-role notes moved onto the Discord screen). Back returns to the list; linking another platform means tapping it there. The "Other links" section and the "How others see you" preview stay on the list page. (4) The Discord screen leads with an "Open Discord" button (`https://discord.com/app`) that takes the player straight into Discord, then the Developer Mode → Copy User ID steps, then the paste field. (5) Server: `psnId` / `xboxId` / `rockstarId` / `discordId` max length raised 60 → 200 in `saveProfile` and `saveSettings` so a pasted full profile link actually fits and saves; the client inputs take the same limit from the action schema automatically.
- Not done here (queued separately by the owner): the site-wide quality pass (alignment, transitions, repeated pictures) — this build is the link flow only.

## Post flow restored + feed post location (Nov 21, 2026)

- The FeedCreator's 4-step post wizard (Capture → Preview → Edit → Details, with the numbered step indicator) is REMOVED from the post path, per the owner's reference screenshots of the original process: camera → "New post" picker (Recents grid, multi-pick, Next) → "New post" details screen → Share. Picking media and tapping Next now goes DIRECTLY to the details screen; a camera capture or camera-gallery pick aimed at a post does the same. No Preview step, no Edit step (no filters/presets/sliders), no step indicator on the post path.
- The restored details screen is exactly the reference layout: sticky back-arrow + "New post" header, the picked photo/video large and centred in its own aspect ratio (arrows + counter when several photos are picked), a plain "Add a caption…" field (hashtags are typed into the caption), an "Add location" row (pin icon + chevron, expands to a text input), an "Add link" row (link icon + chevron, expands to the URL input), and one big blue full-width Share button. No destination chooser, no category chips, no hashtag chip row on this screen. Share publishes through createFeedPost (caption, mediaUrls, linkUrl, location) and the existing onPublished lands the user on For you, Latest first.
- Story and clip paths are unchanged in flow: their pickers still go Preview → Edit → their details screen (with the Post/Story/Videos chooser), because those steps are shared components and the removal was scoped to the post destination.
- Feed post location (new stored field): `feed_posts.location` (schema.ts + migration 0078_feed_post_location.sql, `text DEFAULT '' NOT NULL`). createFeedPost accepts `location` (trimmed, max 120, moderation-screened like the caption) and stores it; listFeed and listProfilePosts (profilePostItem) return it; FeedPostCard and ProfilePostFullCard render it with a pin icon under the caption/link chip when set. All other feed_posts inserts (automatic/bot posts) rely on the column default "".

## Vice AI — no fake answers (owner rule, Nov 21 2026)

- Ben's rule: Vice must never answer something fake. In `askVice` (server/src/actions.ts) the system prompt now carries a hard "NO FAKE ANSWERS" rule: any part of a question not covered by the context (or unconfirmed for GTA VI) is not filled with plausible-sounding invention — Vice answers the covered parts, says plainly it can't answer the rest, and tells the player to contact customer services (support) for more info; when the unanswerable part is the main point, needsHuman = true so the client's support form opens. The needsHuman definition and the GTA-knowledge / support-scope context lines say the same.
- The no-AI deterministic fallback follows the same rule: a question with no matched content now answers "I can't answer that one … contact customer services" with needsHuman = true (support form opens), the partial-match answer appends the contact-customer-services line for uncovered parts, and the unmatched Easter-egg answer points to customer services instead of implying an answer.

## Quality pass — repeated pictures replaced + phone balance (Nov 21 2026)

- Owner's rule: no picture repeats across tabs. Audit of App.tsx found the news-art picker (NEWS_SLIDE_ART / newsCardArt / trendingArtFor) was reusing Guide covers, section banners and Database art for story pictures on Home, News and Database; the Database page used the Home hero as its backdrop and the Map page's card art on its own map card; the Guides "All Guides" card reused the Missions cover; the Easter Eggs Premium ad slot reused the Premium page banner. Each repeat now has its own original generated illustration (media.generate_image, build-time, owned bytes in client/src/assets, recompressed to ~1600px webp like the rest of the set): news-art-racing, news-art-money, news-art-missions, news-art-weapons, news-art-characters, news-art-map, news-art-bugs, news-art-eggs, news-art-clips, news-art-tips, news-art-settings, news-art-patch, news-art-meet, news-art-newsroom, news-art-community (dedicated news-story art), db-backdrop (Database page backdrop), map-display-dusk (Database map card; the Map page keeps map-display-night), guides-library (All Guides card), premium-ad (Eggs ad slot; the Premium page keeps premium-hero). The retired Clips tab's sec-clips import was removed (its only remaining use was the old news-art rule). Section banners, Guide covers and Database category art are unchanged and now appear only in their own sections (plus the small Search recommendation tiles, which are those sections' own labelled previews).
- Phone type scale rebalanced in theme.css: the phone is a mini laptop — same hierarchy as desktop, one balanced step smaller (base 15px, sm 13px, xs 11.5px, 2xl 24px, 4xl 36px at ≤640px), replacing the earlier over-minimised scale. Desktop untouched; form fields keep 16px.
- Smooth finish in theme.css: .img-fade 450ms picture fade-in (SectionBanner + Home hero), card border/shadow easing, a 1.02 banner zoom on fine pointers only, all disabled under prefers-reduced-motion.
- Follow-up fixes in the same pass: the desktop sidebar's nav column now scrolls on its own (overflow-y-auto) so the Community section is never clipped on short laptop screens — logo, search, the sidebar ad stack and the account trigger stay pinned exactly as before, ads untouched. The Home carousel picker no longer repeats a picture: when a story's topic picture is already on another slide it takes the first unused news picture, and only reuses a topic picture when every picture is already on a slide.

## Bot stories redone — recorded-feeling clips or a GTA pic, full frame (Nov 21, 2026)

- Owner's words, with a phone screenshot of a bot story showing a small landscape "UNCONFIRMED" news video parked at the top of a black story frame: "Ok no I don't like it at all what do I mean with story and clips. short videos like TikTok that actually feels like someone recorded it either from yt, or their own gameplay whatever or just a pic of gta. Not a video link on the story that doesn't even fit the frame what is this? And all of them are the same cuz if is like this I rather have the bots not post story cuz it's really bad". His standing fallback rules this build: a story that can't look right doesn't get posted at all.
- Root causes: (1) the story player mounted the YouTube iframe at the story frame's own portrait shape, and YouTube letterboxes a 16:9 video inside whatever shape the iframe has — so every clip rendered as a small landscape strip over black. (2) The story clip pool took ANY YouTube video the Feed had stored, including news / infographic videos (the "UNCONFIRMED" rumor-check card in the screenshot), and the pool was small enough that the same videos kept cycling.
- Client (`client/src/App.tsx`, `YoutubePlayer` vertical mode — used only by the story viewer): the iframe is now sized to the VIDEO's own aspect (16:9, or 9:16 for a Short) and scaled to COVER the story frame (measured with a ResizeObserver, centred, 3% overscan, frame overflow hidden, iframe pointer-events off so the story tap zones own every tap) — the video fills the whole story, cropped at the sides the way TikTok crops landscape clips. Story embeds run chromeless (`controls=0&disablekb=1&fs=0&modestbranding=1&iv_load_policy=3`); a Short loops (`loop=1&playlist=<id>`) until the story advances; windowed clips keep their start/end window. The sound pill stays, raised above the tap zones in story mode. Feed (non-vertical) players are untouched.
- Server (`server/src/actions.ts`, `refreshBotCommunity` Stories section): the pool now carries `kind` ("short" | "video" — a Short sighting upgrades a landscape entry), and news/infographic titles are refused entry to the story pool (`STORY_NEWSY_TITLE`: news, confirmed/unconfirmed, rumour, explained, breakdown, "everything we know", release date, patch notes, top-N, delay… — those videos stay Feed posts, never stories). Pool top-up searches lead with Shorts (`site:youtube.com/shorts GTA VI`, `site:youtube.com/shorts GTA 6 gameplay`, then gameplay/funny clip searches), oEmbed-verified as before, target 90.
- Three proper story forms, mixed per member (Shorts ~45%, pictures ~27%, clip windows the rest, empty shelves fall through): (1) a real GTA VI YouTube Short — mediaUrl `youtube.com/shorts/<id>?len=<12–20>` hold, loops in the player; (2) the existing unique 5–10s clip window into a pooled gameplay-style video; (3) a portrait GTA picture from a NEW story art pool (`bot-story-art-pool`, 12 original portrait illustration briefs, generated into owned blobs via the same fillPool machinery, 3 per run) — mediaType "image", full-frame in the viewer. Uniqueness is enforced against the live set for all three: no shared video, window, picture (a picture may be live on at most one bot story) or caption; a member never reposts their last clip.
- Retirement now keeps ONLY those three forms: a live bot story survives a run only if it is a Short, a unique clip window into a video that is in the (news-filtered) pool, or a story-pool picture live on that story alone. Everything else — the letterboxed news-link stories, shared/old pictures, text stories, duplicates — is deleted with its views/likes/replies/highlight links. If no proper form is available, the run posts no story (the owner's explicit preference). Real players' stories are never touched; no schema or migration change.

## Bot stories — full-screen 9:16 PICTURES only, no video links in stories (Nov 21, 2026)

- Owner's words, with a phone screenshot of a bot story STILL showing a small landscape video strip with YouTube's own play button at the top of a black frame (the cover-crop player did not fill the frame on his phone): "Can u make them full screen 16:... and also make it play like an actual clip with sounds on if that's too hard post a pic of a meme or just gta pictures as story..." The main agent answered honestly in chat before this build: a sound-on playing clip cannot be had from a YouTube link (phones block sound-on autoplay; the YouTube player always sits in its own letterboxed box), so the owner's stated fallback is what ships — bot stories are full-frame pictures (memes / GTA pics), no video links in stories at all.
- Client (`client/src/App.tsx`, `StoryViewer`): an embed (video-link) story no longer mounts the `YoutubePlayer` iframe at all — it renders the video's own YouTube thumbnail (`i.ytimg.com/vi/<id>/hqdefault.jpg`, the thumbnail host the app already uses) as an `object-cover` picture filling the whole 9:16 story frame, exactly like a photo story, and holds 5 seconds (`storyEmbedDurationMs` now always returns 5000). Non-YouTube embed links render their URL as a cover picture the same way. No iframe, no play button, no letterbox in the story frame, for bot AND real-player link stories alike. Native uploaded videos (`mediaType "video"`) still play as real videos, and Feed / profile Video players are untouched. Story pictures already rendered `absolute inset-0 h-full w-full object-cover` inside the full-height stage, so every story now fills the frame edge to edge.
- Server (`server/src/actions.ts`, `refreshBotCommunity` Stories section): the Shorts / clip-window forms and the whole `bot-story-clip-pool` machinery (meta pool, Feed merge, oEmbed-verified Shorts search) are REMOVED. Bot stories are pictures only: the story art pool (`bot-story-art-pool`) grew from 12 to 24 briefs — the original 12 GTA scenes plus 4 GTA meme-style cartoon scenes (launch-day sick call, launch countdown calendar, controller trophy fist-bump, launch-night snack couch) and 8 more GTA scenes — and fills at 6 per run (`fillPool` gained a per-run budget parameter; the Feed post-art pool keeps 3). Retirement now keeps ONLY a story-pool picture live on that story alone: every live bot embed/video-link story, shared picture, text story and duplicate is deleted with its views/likes/replies/highlight links on the next run. Posting takes unused pool pictures in shuffled order with unique captions (clip-flavoured caption lines reworded for stills); when the free pictures run out, posting stops — no duplicates, no video links, no story rather than a bad one. Real players' stories are never touched; no schema or migration change.
- Applied on submit via `post_submit_action: refreshBotCommunity` so the live embed bot stories are retired and replaced with picture stories immediately, not only at the next scheduled run.

## Phone top bar — hide on scroll down, show on scroll up (Nov 21, 2026)

- Owner's words: "Ok I want this affect for my top bar and this is for everywhere on the phone tab that has the top bar so whenever you scroll down to top bar will dissapear like normal it's not fixed but if you scroll up that when the bar appears."
- Client only (`client/src/App.tsx` + one rule in `client/src/theme.css`); no server, schema or data change. The phone top bar (the black bar with the avatar ball, centred VS logo and search — rendered on every phone tab except Chat and Community Feed, which by the owner's Nov 21 direction have no shell top bar) is no longer fixed in place: the App shell tracks `<main>`'s scroll direction (`onMainScroll`, 6px direction hysteresis so it never flickers on jitter; it only hides once the player is 72px+ into the page). Scrolling down collapses the bar out of the layout — a grid-template-rows 1fr→0fr wrapper animates over 0.3s and the page takes the full screen; scrolling up expands it straight back; at the top of a page (scrollTop ≤ 8) it is always shown, and switching tabs resets it to shown. When fully collapsed the wrapper also flips to visibility:hidden (delayed to the end of the slide) so the bar's buttons leave the tab order while hidden.
- The header itself dropped its `z-40 flex-none lg:hidden` classes — the new wrapper carries the flex-none / phone-only behaviour (the bar is only ever rendered below lg anyway, since the wrapper only wraps this phone-only header). Desktop is untouched: nothing on desktop reads the hidden state. Pull-to-refresh, the drawer, search and the logo's tap-to-top are unchanged (the logo tap still scrolls to the top, which also re-shows the bar). Reduced-motion readers still get the hide/show behaviour, just without the slide (`.phone-topbar-slide` transition off under prefers-reduced-motion in theme.css).

## Database duplicate entries merged — same item, one entry (Nov 21, 2026)

- Owner's words, with a Mac screenshot of the Characters page showing PROTAGONIST 4 ("Jason" with a painting placeholder, "Jason Duval" with the real portrait, "Lucia" with a placeholder, "Lucia Caminos" below): "why are there repetiton?" Cause: the Database auto-sync stores each entry under the name its source used, so the same item landed twice — a short name vs the full name, the manufacturer written into the name vs kept in its own field, or the same name with a different apostrophe character. The catalog reconcile never removes auto-discovered rows, so the pairs lived side by side.
- Live audit of all four kinds (via listDbEntries) found the full set: Characters — Jason/Jason Duval, Lucia/Lucia Caminos, Freddie/Freddie Gibbs; Vehicles — the '90 Annis Venturas BLVD Elegy three times (straight/curly apostrophes), the '55 Vapid Stanier twice, and Shitzu Squalo/Squalo, Dinka Enduro/Enduro, Bravado Banshee/Banshee (manufacturer in the name vs in the field); Locations — Black Eagle/Black Eagle gun store. Weapons had no duplicates. Prefix pairs that are genuinely different items (Buccaneer/Buccaneer Custom, Dominator/Dominator GT/GTX/ASP/Buggy, Sabre Turbo/Sabre Turbo Custom, Carbine/Carbine Rifle Mk II, Coquette/Coquette Classic, Felon/Felon GT, Dubsta/Dubsta 6x6) were verified distinct and are never merged.
- Server (`server/src/actions.ts`): new `mergeDuplicateDbEntries` helper with a deliberately narrow same-entity matcher (`dbEntriesSameEntity` + `foldDbName`): folded names equal (case/whitespace/apostrophe-glyph folded); a Character whose name is a word-prefix of the other (first name vs full name); a Vehicle/Weapon whose longer name is exactly the other entry's manufacturer + name; a Location whose longer name only adds generic place words ("gun store"). Keeper scoring: catalog entry (autoDiscovered=0) first, then a real stored picture, featured, stronger status, clean typography, fuller name, oldest id. The survivor inherits any field it lacks, adopts the fuller name when it only had the short form, takes the stronger status when it is itself auto-discovered (a catalog survivor's status is untouched), adopts the dropped copy's picture when it has none, member ratings re-point to the survivor (one rating per member kept), the duplicate row is deleted, and every merge is logged in content_corrections ("Merged into …", source "ViceSunshine Database upkeep").
- Prevention: `refreshDbEntries` now matches extracted entries on the folded name and falls back to the same-entity matcher before inserting, so a differently-named sighting updates the stored entry instead of adding a copy; the merge also runs at the end of every `refreshDbEntries` run and at the end of the `seedExamples` catalog reconcile. New `dedupeDbEntries` action runs the same merge on demand; applied on submit via `post_submit_action: dedupeDbEntries` so the live duplicates are merged immediately. No schema or migration change; no client change (the Database page reads the same listDbEntries).

## Feed top bar — hide on scroll down, show on scroll up, on phone AND laptop (Nov 21, 2026)

- Owner's words (with a laptop screenshot of the Community Feed): "same concept for both mobile and laptop when u scroll down the top bar (rom for you and up) would dissapear when u scroll up it will appear" — the phone top-bar concept from the previous section, extended to the Feed's own top bar on every size: everything from the For you / Following tabs up (the tabs plus the icon row above them — avatar ball, +, VS logo, search / messages / bell).
- Client only (`client/src/App.tsx` + one rule in `client/src/theme.css`); no server, schema or data change. The shell's existing scroll-direction state (`topBarHidden`, driven by `onMainScroll` on `<main>` with the same 6px hysteresis and 72px arm) is now also passed into `FeedView`. In `FeedView`, the `SocialTopBar` row and the For you / Following tab row are wrapped in ONE sticky header (`sticky top-0 z-30`, page-background, bleeding over `<main>`'s side padding on phone and laptop): scroll down and it slides up out of view (`translateY(-100%)`, 0.3s), scroll up and it slides straight back over the posts, and at the top of the Feed it is always shown. Once slid away it flips to `visibility:hidden` (delayed to the end of the slide) so its controls leave the tab order while hidden — the same treatment as the phone shell bar. This replaces the Feed bar's earlier phone-only always-pinned layer (`SocialTopBar`'s `stickyTop` prop, now removed as unused — Clips never used it and still renders the plain bar): the bar now leaves on scroll down on every size, per the owner's "same concept for both".
- Everything else is untouched: the phone shell top bar keeps its own collapse on the other phone tabs, the desktop sidebar never moves, the Stories row / filters / posts below the header are unchanged, and reduced-motion readers get the same hide/show without the slide (`.feed-topbar-slide` transition off under prefers-reduced-motion in theme.css).

## Crew chat life updates + activity mirror + duplicate-member fix (Nov 21, 2026, owner's direction)

- Source: the owner's own words in chat (no external source). Rules as given: joining a crew must put its chat in the member's Messages with nothing for the player to create and no cost; automatic life updates (join / leave / kick / promotion / demotion with who did it / profile-picture change) appear in the crew chat; the crew page ACTIVITY appears in the crew chat too (same updates both places); Crew Award entries are removed from activity (the Awards system itself stays); the first activity message is the date the crew was founded; and the duplicate "ViceSunshineEditor" in the members list / activity was investigated.
- Server (`server/src/actions.ts`): `logActivity` now mirrors every entry into the crew's automatic chat as a system message (same actor/action/target text), except "founded the crew" — the chat instead opens with a seeded founding message (`syncCrewChat` writes it when a chat is created: founder gamertag + the crew's real `created_at` date; `listGroupThread` places the same line first for chats created before seeding). `ensureCrewChats` was refactored onto a per-clan `syncCrewChat`. `joinClan` and application approval sync the crew chat immediately, so it is in the joiner's Messages from that moment. `uploadCustomAvatar` / `removeCustomAvatar` log "updated their profile picture" / "removed their profile picture" to the player's crew. `applyClanAwards` no longer writes activity entries (grants are silent; awards show in the Awards section only). The inactive-leader transfer's hand-written chat post was removed — the mirror covers it.
- Duplicate root cause (verified in the sandbox copy of the data): three `clan_members` rows for gamertag ViceSunshineEditor in one crew, each under a DIFFERENT playerKey (fresh editor/device keys), because membership was matched by playerKey only. Fixes: `healMembershipKey` (runs on crew-page open, Messages reads and crew lookups) rebinds a stale-key row to the player's current key or deletes it when the current key already has a row in that crew; `joinClan` / application approval rebind instead of inserting a duplicate; `getClan` renders one roster row per gamertag.
- Migration `0079_crew_activity_cleanup_and_member_dedupe.sql`: deletes stored "earned a Crew Award…" activity rows; deletes exact duplicate `clan_members` / `chat_group_members` rows (same key, keeps earliest); deletes duplicate "joined as Recruit" activity entries where no leave/kick sits between the two joins.
- Client (`client/src/App.tsx`): system messages in group/crew chats render as centered activity lines, not chat bubbles, and cannot be deleted.
- Delete rule (owner's addition, same build): in a CREW chat only the crew Leader can delete other members' messages; Co-Leaders lost that power (they can still delete their own, like everyone). Enforced in `deleteGroupMessage` and reflected in `listGroupThread`'s `canDelete`; automatic system messages (the life updates) can never be deleted. Player-made group chats and event chats keep their owner-deletes rule. The crew chat info text in the client states the rule.

- **Emblem effects WORK + all crew colours fixed (owner's direction, Nov 22, 2026 — "make sure that the emblem effects work and not just a display sitting there and all colors including a customize thingy not all colors are working now so fix that")**: Emblem effects are a real equipped feature again (they had been removed Nov 19 while the Premium page still advertised them). Server `COSMETIC_ITEMS` gains a Crew Emblem Effects section (kind `effect`, exactly 5 free + 3 Premium): Neon Glow, Vice Shine, Frost, Bubbles, Sunset Shimmer (free) and Fire, Lightning, Champion Gold (Premium — matching the Premium page's "Fire, Lightning, Ice, Neon…" promise). `equipCosmetic`/`unequipCosmetic` write `clans.emblem_effect` (the column from migration 0013 — no new migration); `clanSummaryOf` returns the real stored effect (was hardcoded `""`); `getCrewCustomise` reads equipped state for effects from the stored look; `enforceCrewPremiumCosmetics` unequips a Premium effect when the owner's Premium lapses. `ClanEmblem` renders the effect as an animated CSS overlay (`.emblem-fx-<value>` in theme.css — pulse, shine sweep, frost shimmer + sparkle, rising bubbles, sunset shimmer, fire flicker, lightning flash, gold sweep; all disabled under prefers-reduced-motion) clipped inside the tile, on EVERY surface: crew card, crew page, rankings, Saved, search, live activity feed, crew chat list + header (new `clanEmblemEffect` fields on listCrewActivityFeed / listMessages / group thread payloads), Create a Crew preview and the customisation previews — an effect card's preview is the effect playing live on the crew's own emblem. Every emblem also draws its OWN mark now (`EMBLEM_GLYPHS` inline SVGs: sunset, crown, serpent, diamond/gem, wave, eclipse, royal palm/palm, phoenix/flame, star, bolt, shield, Vice Crest shield+diamond) as a watermark behind the initials, so emblems are visibly different from each other instead of one identical display tile. Seed crews now also pick a free emblem effect so the Crews page shows effects playing. COLOURS: three real bugs fixed — (1) the Crew Settings → Details form let a non-Premium Leader pick the 3 Premium colours and Save "succeeded", then `enforceCrewPremiumCosmetics` silently snapped the colour back to Vice Pink on the next read ("the colour doesn't work"): the swatches are now dimmed/disabled with a Premium label when the owner lacks Premium (new `ownerPremiumActive` on `getClan`), and `updateClan` refuses a Premium catalogue colour in plain words instead of saving it. (2) Colours are normalised to #RRGGBB uppercase before storing (`normalizeCrewColor`, used by createClan / updateClan / setCrewColor): a #RGB or lowercase value used to be stored raw and broke the `${color}99`-style alpha gradients surfaces build from it. (3) `getCrewCustomise` equipped state now comes from the crew's stored look ONLY (the old owned_items OR-fallback could mark a second colour equipped alongside the one actually showing) and hex kinds compare case-insensitively (the catalogue itself mixes cases; enforcement's Premium colour/theme checks are case-insensitive too). CUSTOM COLOUR ("a customize thingy"): a `CustomColorControl` (native colour picker + hex field that commits only a real 3/6-digit hex) in Create a Crew Step 1 and Edit crew details, plus a Custom colour card in the Crew Colours customisation section backed by the new Leader-only `setCrewColor` action — ANY colour applies at once to emblem, member tags, card header and page accent; only a colour exactly matching a Premium catalogue colour needs the owner's Premium. Ask Vice's crew-customisation copy updated to match. Verified in the sandbox by driving the actions: createClan with `#abc` stored `#AABBCC`; equip Neon Glow → `emblemEffect: "neon"` on getClan/listClans; Premium Fire effect and Premium Molten colour refused for a non-Premium owner; setCrewColor `#123abc` → `#123ABC`, Premium hex `#FF7A00` and junk `redd` refused in plain words; updateClan with lowercase Premium `#39e07a` refused; unequip cleared the effect.
- Correction to the entry above (same build, after the audit's P0): the custom-colour path must accept EVERY valid hex — the first pass refused a custom colour whose hex matched a Premium catalogue swatch (#FF7A00 / #39E07A), which is the owner's "not all colours are working" complaint all over again. Now: `setCrewColor`, `createClan` and `updateClan` accept any valid hex (normalised); Premium gating lives ONLY on the named Premium catalogue items (their swatches stay dimmed/disabled for non-Premium pickers in the direct forms, and `equipCosmetic` still refuses the named Premium items without the owner's Premium). `enforceCrewPremiumCosmetics` resets the crew colour only when a Premium colour ITEM is flagged equipped on the crew (owned row), never for a stored hex match — and `setCrewColor`/`updateClan` clear stale equipped colour rows when they write a colour directly, so a custom colour can never be mistaken for a catalogue equip and snapped back.
- Crew chat membership fix (owner's report, Nov 22 2026: "on my crew section I have two more crew chats" — Palm Reaper Syndicate and Neon Tide Cartel showed in his Crews & Events tab alongside Keys Runner Collective, the crew he is actually in): joining a crew puts you in its chat and leaving takes you out, but the same person had picked up membership rows in several crews across device keys (healMembershipKey merges rows by gamertag onto the current key), so every merged crew's chat legitimately mirrored a membership row. Fix in server/src/actions.ts: (1) healMembershipKey now enforces ONE crew per person after consolidating — it keeps the crew joined first and removes the extra membership rows, announcing each removal in that crew's Activity log and crew chat like any other leave, so ensureCrewChats takes the player out of those crews' chats and only the one real crew chat remains; (2) joinClan's stale-key rebind shortcut now runs the one-crew check first, so a rebind can never become a second membership; (3) reviewApplication heals the applicant's rows before the one-crew check; (4) listDmConversations lists a crew chat ONLY when the player is a current member of that crew (membership is the source of truth), whatever chat rows history left behind. No schema change, no migration.
- Premium colours REMOVED, Custom colour tool is the Premium colour perk (owner's direction, Nov 22, 2026 — "Remove the premium colors and instead make the customize color tool premium with like chrome options etc."): the 3 Premium named colours (Molten Orange #FF7A00, Emerald #39E07A, Midnight Violet #5B2A86) lost their premiumOnly flag in BOTH catalogues (server COSMETIC_ITEMS ids color-molten / color-emerald-premium / color-midnight-violet kept, names/descriptions now "Free for every crew"; client CREW_CATALOGUE_COLORS has no premiumOnly at all) — all 8 named colours equip free everywhere, and their swatches in Create a Crew / Edit crew details are never dimmed or disabled again (this also ends the "last 3 colours still not working" report: they were Premium-locked with no visible explanation). In their place, the CUSTOM colour tool is Premium: `setCrewColor` now refuses without the Leader's Premium and takes a `finish` ("" solid | chrome | gold | rose-gold | iridescent — server CREW_COLOR_FINISHES holds each finish's name + representative hex; client CREW_COLOR_FINISHES mirrors keys/hexes and holds the gradients). Migration 0080 adds `clans.color_finish`: a finish stores its representative hex as the crew colour (member tags / text accents) plus the finish key, and `ClanEmblem` + the crew-card header render the finish gradient (Chrome silver bands, Gold Chrome, Rose Gold, Iridescent holographic) — threaded as `colorFinish` on clanSummaryOf/clanSummary and `clanColorFinish` on the crew activity feed and both chat payloads, and passed to ClanEmblem on the crew card, crew page, rankings, Saved, Saved-page recommendations and crew chat list/header. `createClan` and `updateClan` refuse CHANGING to a non-catalogue hex without the creator's/Leader's Premium in plain words (saving with the existing colour untouched always works; updateClan clears the finish only when the colour actually changes); `equipCosmetic` on a named colour clears the finish; `enforceCrewPremiumCosmetics` now falls a custom colour (any stored hex outside the 8 catalogue hexes) and any finish back to Vice Pink solid when the owner's Premium lapses, and never touches a catalogue colour. The Crew Colours customisation section reads "8 free · 0 Premium" with the reworked Custom colour card (Premium badge, Solid + 4 finish swatches, locked state with an Included-with-Premium route when the owner lacks Premium); Create a Crew and Edit crew details show the custom control disabled with a plain Premium note for non-Premium leaders. Premium page perks and Ask Vice's crew-customisation knowledge updated to match. NOTE: this supersedes the two entries directly above where they describe custom colours as free and Premium gating as living only on named catalogue items.

- **Crew emblems COMPLETELY redesigned as AI-generated artwork (owner's direction, Nov 22, 2026 — "can't u not lik change the emblem completely like cool designs like really cool ones like use ai to generate")**: the old look — a crew-colour tile with the tag initials over a faint line glyph (`EMBLEM_GLYPHS`) — is replaced for every catalogue emblem by a full AI-designed artwork generated at build time with the media generation tool (1600×1600 webp, owned bytes under `client/src/assets/`): `emblem-sunset.webp` (striped neon sunset over waves, palm silhouettes, glowing ring), `emblem-crown.webp` (spiked gold crown with pink/teal jewels over a laurel wreath), `emblem-serpent.webp` (emerald serpent coiled around a hot-pink neon ring), `emblem-diamond.webp` (faceted diamond blasting cyan/pink light rays), `emblem-wave.webp` (teal wave curling around a pink sunset glow), `emblem-vicecrest.webp` (Premium — ornate gold shield crest, radiant sun, palm fronds, glowing pink gem), `emblem-eclipse.webp` (Premium — black eclipse with violet/pink plasma corona in a steel ring), `emblem-royal-palm.webp` (Premium — crowned palm backlit by sunset in a gold ring), `emblem-phoenix.webp` (Crew Award — blazing phoenix in a bronze ring). The stored emblem VALUES are unchanged (sunset / crown / serpent / diamond / wave / vicecrest / eclipse / royal-palm / phoenix, plus legacy aliases gem→diamond, palm→royal-palm, flame→phoenix), so no migration and no crew loses its emblem; Premium gating, equip/unequip and enforcement are untouched. `ClanEmblem` (client) now renders the artwork full-bleed inside the tile, framed by the crew colour — or the Premium finish gradient — with the equipped emblem effect animation still playing clipped over the art, and the Vice Crest keeps its gold frame + corner diamond. Emblem values with NO artwork (legacy shield/star/bolt defaults) keep the old monogram tile as a fallback. Server catalogue descriptions and Ask Vice's crew-customisation knowledge now describe the new artwork; the Crew Emblems section blurb in Crew Settings says every emblem is a full AI-designed artwork.
- **Crews page rebuilt — create-first, aligned, and the whole rank system removed from crew surfaces (owner's direction, Nov 22, 2026 — "fix the whole crew page make it align and less suggestions… there's no ranking in the system so remove that as well, all rank system… move the create clan up… premium ones that only premium members (leaders) can put on the clan… remove the crew perks as well… remove the 0 points total pill")**: ClansView (client/src/App.tsx) is restructured top to bottom — hero (the "{totalTrophies} points total" pill is deleted, the subtitle no longer mentions rankings) → a new neon "Found your own crew" panel directly under the hero with the Create a crew button and the founder form opening beneath it (creating leads the page; the old create button buried beside the filters is gone) → gamertag note → Live in crews right now → a "Browse crews" directory with one aligned header, the even 6-tile playstyle filter grid and a full-width search in line with the card grid → crew cards → Community Events promo. REMOVED from the page: the "Highest-ranked crew" card, the separate "Suggested for you" / "Trending crews" block (fewer suggestions — the directory grid below lists every crew with Join), the Crew Leaderboard entry card, and the Rank ladder section (ROLE_ORDER / ROLE_SHORT deleted with it; crew roles still exist inside each crew for member management, just not displayed as a ladder). The Crew perks and How-to-earn-points panels stay off via their existing restore flags (SHOW_CREW_PERKS / SHOW_POINTS_EARNING, unchanged). Rank displays are stripped from every crew surface: crew cards lose the trophies pill, the league badge (LeagueBadge + LEAGUE_ICONS deleted) and the "season pts / x member bonus" pills — a card is now header → Members / Events stat boxes → members line → Save → View crew / Join; the crew detail page loses its league badge too. The underlying points data and the (already switched-off) Leaderboard page are untouched — only crew-facing displays changed. CreateClanForm got a GTA VI neon header (Crew founder kicker, gradient rule) with its steps, live preview and Premium gating unchanged: Premium emblems (Vice Crest, Eclipse, Royal Palm), the custom colour tool and chrome finishes remain Premium-Leader-only, exactly as the owner asked. **Events vs Events Held — checked and restored as separate stats**: the old crew page's two stats grids listed "Events" and "Events Held" as separate tiles (see the ClanHome entry above); the current detail page had collapsed to just "Events held". `getClan` now returns `eventsCount` — the crew's Community Events on the books right now, counted live from real events rows (events.clan_id) — and the crew detail stats grid is four even tiles again: Members · Unlimited / Events (eventsCount) / Events held (the stored lifetime counter, which only goes up) / Crew wallet (VC). No schema change, no migration.

## Crew join anti-spam gate — one direct join a week + Leader-accepted return requests (owner's rule, Nov 22 2026, migration 0081)

Owner's words: "you can only join a crew once a week to prevent spamming after the 1 time if you decide to leave you won't be able to directly join but instead could only request to join and wait for the leader approval (which will be shown in the group chat and the leader has to press accept)".

- New table `crew_join_history` (clan_id, player_key, gamertag, how ∈ direct/approved/founded, joined_at) — every successful join is recorded forever, because clan_members rows are deleted on leave/kick. Migration 0081 backfills it from the current clan_members rows, so existing members' joins count from day one.
- The gate (server helper `crewJoinGate`, matched by playerKey OR gamertag, same identity rule as healMembershipKey): direct join of a PUBLIC crew is allowed only when (1) the player has NEVER been in that crew before, and (2) their last join of ANY crew (direct, Leader-approved, or founding) was 7+ days ago (or never). One direct join per rolling week, across all crews.
- When the gate closes, `joinClan` on a public crew files a join REQUEST instead of joining: a `clan_applications` row with new `kind = 'join_request'` (classic applications keep kind 'application'), plus a card message in that crew's group chat (`direct_messages.join_request_id` points at the application row). One open request per player per crew — re-tapping never duplicates the card.
- ONLY the Leader can accept or decline a join request: `reviewApplication` refuses Co-Leaders for kind 'join_request' (classic applications stay Leader/Co-Leader). The crew chat card (in `listGroupThread`, whose messages now carry joinRequestId + the application's LIVE status) renders Accept/Decline only for the Leader; after a decision the card permanently shows "Accepted by the Leader" / "Declined by the Leader". The crew page Applications list also shows join requests, labelled, with buttons only for the Leader.
- Approving a request/application records an 'approved' join (starts the weekly clock); founding records 'founded'; a direct walk-in records 'direct'. Kicked players are covered by the same history — coming back always needs the Leader's accept.
- Client: the crew page join box reads `getClan.myJoinState` (directAllowed / reason / pendingRequest) — the button becomes "Request to join" with the reason spelled out, or "Request sent — waiting for the Leader" while a request is open; the Crews directory join shows the same outcomes as a notice; the leave confirmation warns there is no direct way back in. Application/Invite-Only crews are unchanged (they always needed approval).
